# ModForge editor text: 中文（简体） (zh-Hans)
# 
# 
# Every word the editor shows is here, one text per line: key = text.
# A translation is a copy of this file with the text after each = in another
# language, named for that language: es.txt, pt-BR.txt, zh-Hans.txt...
# Language > New or update a translation, in the editor, writes one for you
# to start from.
# The texts under [In the game] are the ones the game shows players; the
# rest is the editor. One file does both: put it in the editor's languages
# folder, and in the game's BepInEx\plugins\SMSModForge\Languages folder.
# 
# Lines starting with # are notes, and [headings] only help you find your way.
# Neither changes anything.
# Keep {words in braces}, <tags> and [TOKENS:like this] exactly as they are:
# something fills them in or reads them.
# \n starts a new line. An underscore marks the letter Alt reaches a menu by.
# Nothing after the = shows the English. "" shows nothing, on purpose.
# A key ending in .one or .other is one form of a sentence with a number in it.
# A translation has the forms its own language uses; ModForge writes them out.

[This file]

# The language's name, written the way its speakers write it
# en: English
language.name = 简体中文

# yes for a machine translation nobody has checked yet
# en: no
language.machineTranslated = yes

# Who made or checked this translation
# en: 
language.translators =

# The save window, one pack. {pack} is its name, {what} what it changes: "the game's own quests and conversations"
# en: {pack} changes {what}, and this save hasn't been played with that yet. Your progress could break - now, or if you remove the pack later.

# The save window, one pack, when the save is older than ModForge's record of what it has been played with
# en: {pack} changes {what}, and ModForge can't tell whether this save has been played with that. Your progress could break - now, or if you remove the pack later.
game.save.onePack.cannotTell = {pack} 会改动{what}，而 ModForge 无法判断这个存档是否已经在这种情况下玩过。你的进度可能会损坏——现在，或者以后移除该模组包时。

# The save window, several packs; what each changes is listed below it
# en: These packs change the game's own content, and this save hasn't been played with that yet. Your progress could break - now, or if you remove them later.
game.save.somePacks = 这些模组包会改动游戏原有的内容，而这个存档还没有在这种情况下玩过。你的进度可能会损坏——现在，或者以后移除它们时。

# The save window, several packs, when the save is older than ModForge's record of what it has been played with
# en: These packs change the game's own content, and ModForge can't tell whether this save has been played with all of it. Your progress could break - now, or if you remove them later.
game.save.somePacks.cannotTell = 这些模组包会改动游戏原有的内容，而 ModForge 无法判断这个存档是否已经在全部这些改动下玩过。你的进度可能会损坏——现在，或者以后移除它们时。

# Tooltip on a tick box
# en: Move between its own size and the one below. The game uses all of it: (1,0,1) is a flat line - the shop unfolds from one on the way in and folds back to one on the way out - while (0.5,0.5,0.5) is small and (2,2,1) is oversized.
uiOpen.moveBetweenOwnSize.tip = 在自身尺寸与下面的尺寸之间变化。游戏里各种值都有：(1,0,1) 是一条扁平的线——商店打开时从这条线展开，关闭时又折回一条线——而 (0.5,0.5,0.5) 偏小，(2,2,1) 偏大。

uiOpen.playUiOpenAnimation.tip = 在预览中播放一次动画。只是绘制，并不应用：界面保持原样，也不会向模组包写入任何内容。

# en: Variable

# Tooltip on the 'Variable' field of the SetVariable action
# en: Pack variable to set.

# Label beside a field of an action or condition
# en: Value
param.label.value = 值

# Tooltip on the 'Value' field of the SetVariable action
# en: New value. Strings round-trip as-is; use 'true' / 'false' for booleans, and numbers in invariant-culture form (e.g. '3.14'). '$varName' copies that variable's current value instead ('$$' escapes a literal dollar). Leave it blank to CLEAR the variable to an empty string — that's kept as a real value, not treated as an unfilled row.
action.setVariable.value.tip = 新值。字符串原样保存；布尔值用 'true' / 'false'，数字用固定区域性格式（例如 '3.14'）。'$varName' 则改为复制该变量的当前值（'$$' 表示一个真正的美元符号）。留空可把变量“清空”为空字符串——这会作为真正的值保存，而不是未填写的行。

# Tooltip on the 'Variable' field of the IncrementVariable action
# en: Numeric pack variable to add to (negative delta subtracts).
action.incrementVariable.name.tip = 要累加的数值型模组包变量（负增量即为减）。

# Label beside a field of an action or condition
# en: Delta
param.label.delta = 增量

# Tooltip on the 'Delta' field of the IncrementVariable action
# en: Amount added to the current value each time the action runs.
action.incrementVariable.delta.tip = 动作每次执行时加到当前值上的数量。

# Label beside a field of an action or condition
# en: Focused
param.label.focused = 置于前景

# Tooltip on the 'Focused' field of the SetSpriteFocus action
# en: When true, every actor's bust in the dialogue is bumped above the CG layer; when false, their resting sorting orders are restored. No per-actor target — it focuses the whole cast, and resets automatically when the dialogue ends.
action.setSpriteFocus.focused.tip = 为真时，对话中每位演员的立绘都会提到 CG 层之上；为假时恢复各自原本的绘制顺序。没有针对单个演员的目标——它作用于全体角色，并在对话结束时自动恢复。

# Label beside a field of an action or condition
# en: Actor
param.label.actor = 演员

# Tooltip on the 'Actor' field of the LeaveBust action
# en: Triggers the vanilla MBase1/Leave fade-out child if present; falls back to an immediate deactivate.
action.leaveBust.actor.tip = 如果存在，就触发游戏的 MBase1/Leave 淡出子对象；否则立即停用。

# Label beside a field of an action or condition
# en: GO path
param.label.goPath = GO 路径

# Tooltip on the 'GO path' field of the SetGameObjectActive action
# en: A pack overlay/bust name (autocompleted), a vanilla GO name, or a full hierarchy path ('5_Levels/14_Beach/Foreground/Thing'). Inactive objects are found too, so this can activate something that starts disabled.
action.setGameObjectActive.path.tip = 模组包的叠加层或立绘名称（可自动补全）、游戏的 GO 名称，或完整的层级路径（'5_Levels/14_Beach/Foreground/Thing'）。未激活的对象同样能找到，所以可以启用一开始就处于关闭状态的东西。

# Label beside a field of an action or condition
# en: Active
param.label.active = 激活

# Tooltip on the 'Active' field of the SetGameObjectActive action
# en: true to switch the target on, false to switch it off, or toggle to flip it to the opposite of whatever it currently is. Toggle reads the object's own state, so a parent being hidden around it does not count as off.
action.setGameObjectActive.active.tip = true 开启目标，false 关闭目标，toggle 则把当前状态反转。toggle 读取对象自身的状态，所以外层父对象被隐藏并不算作关闭。

# Label beside a field of an action or condition
# en: Sprite
param.label.sprite = 精灵图

# en: Pack-relative PNG to draw. Applied at the image's own size.
action.setSprite.sprite.tip = 要绘制的、相对于模组包的 PNG。按图片自身尺寸应用。

# Label beside a field of an action or condition
# en: Mask (optional)
param.label.maskOptional = 遮罩（可选）

# Tooltip on the 'Mask (optional)' field of the SetSprite action
# en: Pack-relative PNG for the material's _MaskTex. Leave blank to keep the current mask. Warns if the target has no material or its shader has no mask slot.
action.setSprite.mask.tip = 用于材质 _MaskTex、相对于模组包的 PNG。留空则保持当前遮罩。若目标没有材质，或其着色器没有遮罩槽位，会给出警告。

# Label beside a field of an action or condition

param.label.component = 组件

# Tooltip on the 'Component' field of the SetComponentProperty action
# en: Which component on the target to write to. A closed list, not a type name: each entry is supported on purpose so the property list below means something and a bad value fails with a readable log line.
action.setComponentProperty.component.tip = 写入目标的哪个组件。这是一份固定列表，而不是类型名：每一项都是有意支持的，这样下面的属性列表才有意义，错误的值也会以一行可读的日志失败。

# Label beside a field of an action or condition
# en: Property
param.label.property = 属性

# Tooltip on the 'Property' field of the SetComponentProperty action
# en: Which field to set. CanvasGroup: 'alpha' hides the whole UI subtree without deactivating it (so anything animating underneath keeps running); 'interactable' turns its controls on or off; 'blocksRaycasts' decides whether clicks stop here or pass through to what is behind. Hiding a panel usually wants all three, which is three rows.
action.setComponentProperty.property.tip = 要设置的字段。CanvasGroup：'alpha' 会隐藏整个 UI 子树而不停用它（下面正在播放的动画会继续运行）；'interactable' 开关它的控件；'blocksRaycasts' 决定点击是停在这里还是穿透到后面的东西。隐藏一个面板通常三者都要，也就是三行。

# Tooltip on the 'Value' field of the SetComponentProperty action
# en: The new value, read to suit the property: a number for 'alpha' (0..1), 'true' / 'false' for the two flags.
action.setComponentProperty.value.tip = 新值，按属性来解读：'alpha' 用数字（0..1），两个开关用 'true' / 'false'。

# Tooltip on the 'Duration (s)' field of the SetComponentProperty action
# en: 0 sets the value immediately; anything higher tweens to it. Numeric properties only — a flag has nothing to tween through, so this is ignored for those and the change lands at once.
action.setComponentProperty.seconds.tip = 0 立即设置该值；大于 0 则渐变到该值。仅对数值属性有效——开关没有中间状态，所以这里会被忽略，改动一次到位。

# Tooltip on the 'Chance' field of the Random condition
# en: DEPRECATED — re-rolls on EVERY evaluation. In a per-frame context (integration rule, dialogue start conditions, button visibility) that means ~60 rolls/second and the probability is meaningless. Switch to 'DailyChance' (once per day) or a LevelRandom variable + comparison.
condition.random.chance.tip = 已弃用——每次求值都会重新掷一次。在逐帧求值的场合（集成规则、对话开始条件、按钮可见性），这相当于每秒约 60 次，概率就失去意义了。请改用 'DailyChance'（每天一次）或 LevelRandom 变量 + 比较。

# Tooltip on the 'Chance' field of the DailyChance condition
# en: Probability of passing, as a whole percentage. 50 = passes on ~half of in-game days. Rolled once per in-game day: the result holds all day, survives save/reload, and changes at each day roll-over. Every DailyChance rolls independently — the console names each one after its dialogue/rule at the day change.
condition.dailyChance.chance.tip = 成立的概率，用整数百分比表示。50 = 大约一半的游戏日成立。每个游戏日掷一次：结果整天保持不变，存档/读档后仍然有效，并在每次日期切换时变化。每个 DailyChance 各自独立掷点；日期切换时，控制台会按各自的对话/规则名称逐一列出。

# Tooltip on the 'Stagger start' field of the Timer condition
# en: Wait a full interval before the FIRST fire instead of firing immediately. Use it when several rules would otherwise start together — one per character, say — so they don't all act on the same frame. Later waits are unaffected: each rule already re-rolls its own interval independently.
condition.timer.stagger.tip = 在第一次触发前先等满一个间隔，而不是立即触发。当多条规则本来会同时开始（比如每个角色一条）时，用它避免它们在同一帧全部动作。之后的等待不受影响：每条规则本来就各自重新抽取间隔。

# Tooltip on the 'Weather is' field of the Weather condition

# Tooltip on a dropdown

Editable, so a track neither list knows can still be typed in.

gameObject.levelSBuiltNpcs = 关卡自带的 NPC 对象——它的 transform 会整体偏移房间里的每个 NPC，而且你可以像对待任何 GameObject 一样给它添加组件和子对象。把 NPC 放进去并不算改动它。

gameObject.overrideTransform = 覆盖它的 transform

# Tick box

gameObject.overrideActive = 覆盖激活状态

# Text
# en: Components, Active-when conditions, children and NPCs always apply — they're additions either way. Everything else is left untouched unless overridden above, so a bound object you haven't changed is a no-op. If the object isn't found the runtime logs a warning and skips it rather than creating a look-alike.
gameObject.componentsActiveWhenConditions = 组件、“激活条件”、子对象和 NPC 始终生效——无论如何它们都是新增内容。其余内容除非在上面覆盖，否则保持原样，因此一个你没有改动过的绑定对象什么也不会做。如果找不到该对象，运行时会记录一条警告并跳过它，而不是另造一个相似的。

# Tooltip on a box
# en: The level's NPCs container — where NPC placements live. Its transform offsets every NPC in the room, and its subtree composes transforms down the chain.
gameObject.levelSNpcsContainer.tip = 关卡的 NPC 容器——NPC 摆放就放在这里。它的 transform 会整体偏移房间里的每个 NPC，其子树会逐级叠加 transform。

# Tooltip on a box
# en: An object the vanilla level already has. The pack reaches into it rather than creating it, and anything you don't change here is dropped on save.
gameObject.objectVanillaLevelAlready.tip = 游戏原版关卡里已有的对象。模组包是去改它而不是创建它，凡是你在这里没有改动的内容，保存时都会被丢弃。

# Tooltip on a box
# en: This differs from the vanilla object it's bound to, so it will be saved. Match the vanilla values again and it drops out.
gameObject.differsVanillaObjectS.tip = 这里与它绑定的原版对象不同，所以会被保存。把值改回与原版一致，它就会从模组包里消失。

# Tooltip on "Reset"
# en: Put this object back to what the vanilla level actually has — the extracted transform, active state and sorting, not zeroes. It then drops out of the manifest again.
gameObject.resetToVanilla.tip = 把这个对象还原为原版关卡里真实的样子——提取到的 transform、激活状态和排序，而不是清零。之后它会重新从清单里消失。

# Tooltip on "This GameObject already exists (bind, don't create)"
# en: On: the Name above is looked up in the scene (a bare name or a A > B > C path) and only what you author here is applied to it — the way to modify a vanilla level you don't own. Off: this pack creates the GameObject.
gameObject.gameobjectAlreadyExistsBind.tip = 开启：会在场景中按上面的名称查找（可以是纯名称，也可以是 A > B > C 这样的路径），并且只把你在这里写的内容应用上去——这就是修改不属于你的原版关卡的方式。关闭：由本模组包创建该 GameObject。

# Tooltip on "Override its transform"
# en: Off (default) leaves the existing object where it is. On applies the Transform row below as LOCAL position / rotation / scale.
gameObject.overrideTransform.tip = 关闭（默认）会让已有对象保持原位。开启则把下面的 Transform 行作为“局部”位置 / 旋转 / 缩放应用上去。

# Tooltip on "Override active"
# en: Off (default) leaves the object's own active state alone. On applies the Start-active checkbox. Not needed when you're gating it with Active-when conditions.
gameObject.overrideActive.tip = 关闭（默认）不会动对象自身的激活状态。开启则应用“初始激活”复选框。当你用“激活条件”来控制它时，不需要开启。

# Tooltip on a part of the window
# en: Pack-relative PNG path. Leave blank for a pure container GameObject (an empty transform that just groups children).
gameObject.packRelativePNGPath.tip = 相对于模组包的 PNG 路径。若只想要一个纯容器 GameObject（一个只用来归拢子对象的空 transform），请留空。

# Tooltip on a text box
# en: World X position (level centre ≈ 0).
gameObject.worldXPositionLevel.tip = 世界坐标 X（关卡中心 ≈ 0）。

# Tooltip on a text box
# en: World Y position (up = positive); e.g. two overlays use 15/18.
gameObject.worldYPositionUp.tip = 世界坐标 Y（向上为正）；例如有两个叠加层用的是 15/18。

# Tooltip on a text box
# en: Z rotation in degrees (in-plane spin).
gameObject.zRotationDegreesPlane.tip = Z 轴旋转角度（平面内旋转）。

# Tooltip on a text box
# en: Local X scale (1 = native; negative mirrors).
gameObject.localXScale1.tip = 局部 X 缩放（1 = 原始大小；负值为左右翻转）。

# Tooltip on a text box
# en: Local Y scale.
gameObject.localYScale.tip = 局部 Y 缩放。

# Tooltip on a text box
# en: SpriteRenderer sorting order (lower draws further back).
gameObject.spriterendererSortingOrderLower.tip = SpriteRenderer 的排序值（越小越靠后）。

# Tooltip on "Parallax"
# en: Let this object drift with the mouse. Off (the default) pins it to the level, which is what almost every overlay wants.
gameObject.parallax.tip = 让这个对象随鼠标漂移。关闭（默认）会把它钉在关卡上，这也是几乎所有叠加层想要的。

# Tooltip on "Start active"
# en: Uncheck for overlays a dialogue reveals later (Flash, Portal).
gameObject.startActive.tip = 对于由对话稍后揭示的叠加层（Flash、Portal），请取消勾选。

# Tooltip on a text box
# en: Initial alpha 0..1 (start at 0 for fade-in overlays like the Portal).
gameObject.initialAlpha01.tip = 初始透明度 0..1（像 Portal 这类淡入的叠加层请从 0 开始）。

# Tooltip on "Drift with the level"
# en: On (the default) keeps the level's own strength, which is what a created object always did. Turn it off to set this object's depth independently.
gameObject.driftLevel.tip = 开启（默认）会沿用关卡自身的强度，这也是创建出来的对象一贯的行为。关闭后可以单独设定这个对象的景深。

# Tooltip on a text box
# en: Depth relative to the level's main sprite, which every vanilla level fixes at 0.75: lower sits further away and lags behind the room, higher sits nearer and overshoots. Vanilla objects use 0.1, 0.5, 0.75 and 1.5.
gameObject.depthRelativeLevelS.tip = 相对于关卡主精灵图的景深，每个原版关卡都固定为 0.75：更小则更远，落后于房间；更大则更近，会超出房间的移动幅度。原版对象使用 0.1、0.5、0.75 和 1.5。

# Tooltip on "Reversed"
# en: Drift WITH the cursor instead of against it.
gameObject.reversed.tip = 跟随光标漂移，而不是与之相反。

# Tooltip on "UI space"
# en: Only for an object under a Canvas — it switches the effect to canvas coordinates. A pack's own GameObjects are world sprites, so leave this off unless you're binding to a canvas object.
gameObject.uiSpace.tip = 仅用于 Canvas 下的对象——它会把效果换成画布坐标。模组包自己的 GameObject 是世界精灵图，所以除非你绑定的是画布对象，否则请保持关闭。

# Tooltip on a part of the window
# en: Optional pack-relative mask PNG bound to _MaskTex, for shader-masked overlays (a cameo).
gameObject.optionalPackRelativeMask.tip = 可选的、相对于模组包的遮罩 PNG，绑定到 _MaskTex，用于带着色器遮罩的叠加层（比如一次客串出场）。

# Tooltip on "+ Component"
# en: Attach a generic utility component (fade, disappear, random child, blink) to this GameObject.
gameObject.addComponent.tip = 给这个 GameObject 附加一个通用工具组件（淡入、淡出、随机子对象、闪烁）。

# Tooltip on "+ Condition"
# en: Gate this GameObject on a condition. It switches on while the conditions pass.
gameObject.addActiveCondition.tip = 给这个 GameObject 加上条件。条件满足时它就会打开。

# Tooltip on "Switch back off when the conditions stop matching"
# en: Checked (default): the object is active exactly while the conditions hold. Unchecked: it turns on the first time they pass and stays on — a one-way reveal.
gameObject.switchBackOffWhen.tip = 勾选（默认）：对象恰好在条件满足期间处于激活状态。不勾选：条件第一次满足时打开，之后一直保持打开——一次性的揭示。

# Tooltip on "+ Add NPC child"
# en: Place an NPC (from the NPCs tab) as a child of this GameObject.
gameObject.addNpc.tip = 把一个 NPC（来自 NPC 选项卡）作为这个 GameObject 的子对象放置。

# Tooltip on "+ Add child"
# en: Add a child GameObject nested under this one. Children have the same fields, components, and their own children.
gameObject.addChild.tip = 在它下面嵌套添加一个子 GameObject。子对象拥有同样的字段、组件，也可以有自己的子对象。

# In place of a GameObject's name when it has none

gameObject.unnamed = （未命名的 GameObject）

# Beside a field: what the game's own level has there; {0} is the value

# Tooltip on a component
# en: Not seen in any extracted level. The runtime will still try to find a loaded type with this name.
component.unknown.tip = 在提取到的任何关卡中都没有见过。运行时仍会尝试查找同名的已加载类型。

# Tooltip on a component
# en: A Unity engine component. It will be attached, but its parameter names are engine internals and mostly won't apply — the values below are what the extraction saw, not a promise they can be set.
component.engine.tip = 一个 Unity 引擎组件。它会被附加上去，但它的参数名是引擎内部的，多半并不适用——下面的值是提取时看到的，并不保证可以设置。

# Tooltip on a component
# en: One of the game's own scripts. Parameter names come from the extraction, so they match its real fields.
component.games.tip = 游戏自己的脚本之一。参数名来自提取结果，因此与它真实的字段一致。

# Tooltip on one of the game's conditions in a script
# en: Taking it out changes the game's script, so everything it guards - the conversation it plays, and anything beside it - happens without it too.
gameConditions.scriptNote = 移除它会改变游戏的脚本，于是它所守护的一切——它播放的那段对话，以及它旁边的内容——在没有它的情况下也会发生。

# Tooltip on one of the game's conditions on a line
# en: Taking it out changes this line in your version of the conversation, so the line itself is offered without it too. The Dialogues tab shows the same change.
gameConditions.lineNote = 移除它会改变你这一版对话里的这句台词，于是这句台词在没有它的情况下也会被提供。对话选项卡里会显示同样的改动。

# Heading over the room a script of the game's sits in
# en: Where the game's script is — it only runs while the player is here
gameConditions.locationHeader = 游戏脚本所在的位置——只有玩家在这里时它才会运行

# Tooltip on the room a script of the game's sits in
# en: The game's script sits on this place, so it only runs while the player is here. It is not one of the conditions in the script's list, so there is nothing to take out, and the place is the game's: moving it would mean moving the game's own script.
gameConditions.levelNote = 游戏的脚本挂在这个地点上，所以只有玩家在这里时它才会运行。它并不是脚本条件列表中的条件，因此没有什么可移除的；而且这个地点属于游戏：移动它就等于移动游戏自己的脚本。

# Tooltip on the room, where it can be taken out
# en: The game's script sits on this place, so the game only ever does this while the player is here. Take it out and your pack's own conditions decide on their own, wherever the player is.
gameConditions.roomNote = 游戏的脚本挂在这个地点上，所以游戏只在玩家身处此地时才会这么做。把它移除后，你的模组包自己的条件就会独立决定，无论玩家在哪里。

# Tooltip on how a script of the game's picks a conversation
# en: How the script picks this conversation among its children. Not a condition that can be taken out.
gameConditions.pickedNote = 脚本如何在它的子项中挑选这段对话。这不是一个可以移除的条件。

# One of the things listed in the note below
# en: {count} other branches
gameConditions.otherBranches.other = 另外 {count} 个分支

# {what} is one conversation the game plays instead
# en: The game tries this first: {what}. If one of them runs, this one doesn't.
gameConditions.earlierOne = 游戏会先尝试：{what}。如果它运行了，这一条就不会运行。

# {what} lists what the game plays instead
# en: The game tries these first: {what}. If one of them runs, this one doesn't.
gameConditions.earlierSeveral = 游戏会先尝试这些：{what}。其中只要有一个运行了，这一条就不会运行。

# Tooltip on a condition the pack added to one of the game's lines

gameConditions.addedToLine = 在你这一版对话里为这句台词添加的。请到对话选项卡修改。

# Tooltip on "_Spell-check node text"
# en: Underline misspellings using Windows' own spell checker, in the language the lines are written in - the pack's own, or the one you are editing in - both in a node's Text field and down the node list, so mistakes are visible while scanning. A language Windows has no spelling dictionary for is not underlined at all; Windows' language settings can add one. Right-click a squiggle in the Text field for suggestions, to ignore it for this session, or to add the word to your Windows dictionary (the same list Edge and Office use). Turning this off also makes the node list lighter to scroll on very long dialogues. Your choice is remembered between sessions.
menu.spellCheckNodeText.tip = 用 Windows 自带的拼写检查器，按台词所用的语言（模组包自己的语言，或你正在编辑的语言）标出拼写错误——既在节点的“文本”字段里，也在节点列表中，这样浏览时就能看到错误。Windows 没有词典的语言完全不会被标出；可以在 Windows 的语言设置中添加词典。在“文本”字段的波浪线上点击右键可以查看建议、在本次会话中忽略它，或把这个词加入你的 Windows 词典（与 Edge 和 Office 使用的是同一个列表）。关闭它也会让很长的对话中的节点列表滚动起来更轻快。你的选择会在不同会话之间保留。

# Tooltip on "_Version the pack automatically"
# en: Move the pack's version on every save: up a minor when the pack gains something, up a patch when something it already had changes. The major number is never moved for you — that one says "this is a new version of the pack", which is yours to decide. Off means the version only ever changes when you type it.
menu.versionPackAutomatically.tip = 每次保存都推进模组包的版本：模组包新增了内容就升次版本号，已有内容发生改动就升修订号。主版本号永远不会自动推进——那一位表示“这是模组包的新版本”，要由你来决定。关闭后，版本只有你亲手输入时才会改变。

# Tooltip on "Warn when the version goes _backwards"
# en: Ask before saving a version lower than the one already on disk. Going backwards is nearly always a typo, and it makes a new release read as older than the copy a player already has.
menu.warnWhenVersionGoes.tip = 在保存低于磁盘上已有版本的版本号之前先询问。版本往回退几乎总是笔误，而且会让一次新发布显得比玩家手里的副本还旧。

# Tooltip on "Warn about _large exports"
# en: Ask before exporting a pack folder over 150 MB or 1600 files, in case it has swept up something that is not part of the pack. Unticking clears the per-pack "don't warn again" you may have set from the prompt itself.
menu.warnAboutLargeExports.tip = 在导出超过 150 MB 或 1600 个文件的模组包文件夹之前先询问，以防它把不属于模组包的东西也一并收了进来。取消勾选会清除你在提示里为某个模组包选择的“不再提醒”。

# Tooltip on "Offer to _translate before exporting"
# en: Before an export or a publish, when some of what players read in this pack is not yet in every language ModForge has, offer to translate it by machine. Only what is still untranslated is sent. For this pack only.
menu.offerTranslationOnExport.tip = 导出或发布前，如果本模组包中玩家读到的内容还没有覆盖 ModForge 的所有语言，就提议用机器翻译。只发送仍未翻译的内容。仅对本模组包生效。

# Tooltip on "Add pack _names to Windows dictionary…"
# en: Put every character, actor, place and NPC name this pack invents into your Windows dictionary in one go, so they stop being flagged as misspellings. Shows you the list first.
menu.addPackNamesToDictionary.tip = 一次性把这个模组包新造的所有角色、演员、地点和 NPC 名称加入你的 Windows 词典，这样它们就不会再被标成拼写错误。会先把列表给你看。

# Tooltip on "Check for _updates on start"
# en: Ask the releases page whether a newer version has been published, once, when the editor starts. Off means no request is made at all. Your choice is remembered between sessions.
menu.checkUpdatesStart.tip = 编辑器启动时向发布页询问一次是否有更新版本。关闭后则完全不会发出请求。你的选择会在下次启动时保留。

# Tooltip on "_Install updates without asking"
# en: Skip the what-changed window: a found update downloads and installs itself, saying so in a line at the top of the window rather than opening anything. The editor still restarts to finish, and still asks about an unsaved pack first.
menu.installUpdatesWithoutAsking.tip = 跳过“更新了什么”的窗口：找到的更新会自动下载并安装，只在窗口顶部用一行字说明，而不会打开任何东西。编辑器仍会重启以完成更新，也仍会先询问未保存的模组包。

# Tooltip on "_Starmaker Story folder…"
# en: Where the game is installed, with BepInEx beside it. Only used by the updater, so it can replace the plugin at the same time as the editor - the two are versioned together and a mismatched pair can fail without saying why.
menu.setGameFolder.tip = 游戏的安装位置，BepInEx 就在旁边。只有更新程序会用到，以便在更新编辑器的同时替换插件——两者是一起发版的，版本不匹配可能会出问题却不说明原因。

# Tooltip on "Dialogue rows look like the _game"
# en: Draw each line in the node list the way the game draws it — its own font, outline and drop shadow, the speaker's name in their colour, on a dark panel. Costs the spelling squiggles on those rows; they stay in the Text box below the list.
menu.dialogueRowsLookLike.tip = 按游戏的样子绘制节点列表中的每一行——游戏自己的字体、描边和投影，说话者的名字用他们的颜色，衬在深色面板上。代价是这些行上不再显示拼写波浪线；它们仍会出现在列表下方的“文本”框里。

# Tooltip on "Check for updates _now"

menu.checkForUpdates.tip = 现在就询问，无论有无更新都会给出答复。

home.authorModPacksStarmaker = 为 Starmaker Story 制作模组包：对话、地点、立绘、场景、壁纸、音乐、音效、变量和集成规则——导出为单个 .smspack，由游戏插件加载。

# Text
# en: 1.  File ▸ New pack, then Save — most previews and pickers need the pack on disk to resolve file paths.
home.1FileNewPack = 1.  文件 ▸ 新建模组包，然后保存——大多数预览和选择器都需要模组包已在磁盘上，才能解析文件路径。

# Text
# en: 2.  Work left to right — the tabs follow the authoring order: who (Characters, NPCs) → where (Places, Map Buttons) → what they say (Dialogues) → what plays (Scenes, Music, SFX, Wallpapers) → state and logic (Variables, Integration). An NPC is defined on the NPCs tab and then placed into a room from the Places tab.
home.2WorkLeftRight = 2.  从左往右做——选项卡就是制作顺序：谁（角色、NPC）→ 在哪（地点、地图按钮）→ 说什么（对话）→ 播放什么（场景、音乐、音效、壁纸）→ 状态与逻辑（变量、集成）。NPC 在 NPC 选项卡里定义，然后从地点选项卡放进房间。

# Text
# en: 3.  Validate (F5) and fix anything listed on this page — double-click an issue to jump straight to it.
home.3ValidateF5Fix = 3.  验证（F5）并修好本页列出的问题——双击某个问题可直接跳到它那里。

# Text

home.4FileExportPack = 4.  文件 ▸ 导出模组包（Ctrl+E），把 .smspack 放进 BepInEx/plugins/SMSModForge/ModPacks/。

# Tooltip on the box
# en: How this character is spoken of. Translation needs it: in Russian, Spanish, French, Portuguese and German, "I'm ready" and the words describing somebody change with it, and a translator that is not told guesses male. The player's and the game's characters' come from the game.
characters.pronouns.tip = 如何称呼这个角色。翻译需要它：在俄语、西班牙语、法语、葡萄牙语和德语中，“我准备好了”以及描述某人的词语会随之变化，而没被告知的翻译器会猜测为男性。玩家和游戏角色的代词取自游戏。

# Text

characters.playerBuiltSharedEvery = 玩家是内置的，并由所有模组包共用：任何模组中对“你”说话的对话，指向的都是同一个角色，显示同样的名字和颜色。正因如此，名字、颜色、键名和来源都是固定的。打字音仍然由你设定。

characters.derivedCharacterNameAbove = 由上面的角色名称推导而来，并在本模组包内去重；两个模组包可以各带一个 Sarah，因为运行时还会按模组包区分。已有角色保留原本的名称——它们是真正的 GameObject 和对话引用，所以改动其中一个就会重命名模组包构建出来的东西。

# Text
# en: What a dialogue node's Expression field selects. Every character starts with neutral, which is no expression showing and cannot be changed. On one of the game's characters the faces the game already gave them are listed first and cannot be changed either - they belong to the game, and its own conversations ask for them by these names.
characters.whatDialogueNodeS = 对话节点的“表情”字段所选择的内容。每个角色都以 neutral 开始，表示不显示任何表情，且无法更改。对于游戏自己的角色，游戏已经给他们的表情会排在最前面，同样无法更改——它们属于游戏，游戏自己的对话就是按这些名称来调用的。

# Text
# en: Add a row to give a character a face it never had. Its art is named after it: on a bust this pack draws, the outfit's expression prefix picks it up as {prefix}{Name}.png; on one of the game's busts it gets a row of its own under Replace textures.
characters.addRowGiveCharacter = 添加一行，给角色一张他本来没有的表情。它的美术资源按它命名：在本模组包绘制的立绘上，服装的表情前缀会以 {prefix}{Name}.png 的形式取用；在游戏自己的立绘上，它会在“替换贴图”里获得单独的一行。

# Text
# en: Tick only what you are replacing. Anything left unticked stays exactly as the game drew it - this is not a whole bust, it is the parts of one you are painting over. A face the game did not give this character is created for it; the others are swapped in place.
characters.tickOnlyWhatReplacing = 只勾选你要替换的部分。未勾选的一切都会保持游戏绘制时的样子——这不是一整张立绘，而是你要在其上作画的那些部分。游戏没给这个角色的表情会为他新建；其余的则就地替换。

# Tooltip on "Reset"
# en: Put the default outfit back to the bust the game has this character enter in.
characters.resetField.tip2 = 把默认服装还原为游戏让这个角色登场时所用的立绘。

# Tooltip on a dropdown
# en: Which bust this character appears in before any dialogue changes it. Set to the first outfit you add; editable, so a bust built elsewhere can be named directly. Fixed for one of the game's characters: which bust they enter in is the game's, in every scene it wrote.
characters.defaultOutfitPicker.tip = 在对话改变它之前，这个角色以哪套立绘出现。会设为你添加的第一套服装；此处可编辑，因此也可以直接指定在别处构建的立绘。对于游戏自己的角色，这一项是固定的：他们以哪套立绘登场由游戏决定，游戏写的每一幕都如此。

# Tooltip on "Outfit name:"
# en: What dialogue and actions reference this outfit by, and the GameObject the runtime builds.
characters.outfitName.tip = 对话和动作引用这套服装时使用的名称，也是运行时构建的 GameObject。

# Tooltip on a text box
# en: Which of the game's busts this is. Not a name to change: it is how the game and this pack both find that bust.
characters.whichGameSBusts.tip = 这是游戏的哪一张立绘。这不是用来改的名字：游戏和本模组包都靠它找到那张立绘。

# Tooltip on "Replace textures on this bust"
# en: Paint over part of the game's art for this bust - a mouth, a face - leaving the rest of it alone.
characters.replaceTexturesBust.tip = 在游戏为这张立绘提供的美术资源上局部作画——嘴、脸——其余部分保持不动。

# Tooltip on "Reset dialogue key"
# en: Put the key back to the one this character's name derives, and repoint every dialogue line that names them at it.
characters.resetCharacterKey.tip = 把键名还原为由这个角色名字推导出的那个，并把所有指名他的对话台词重新指向它。

# Tooltip on "Dialogue key:"
# en: What a dialogue node names as its speaker.
characters.dialogueKey.tip = 对话节点用来指明说话者的名称。

# Tooltip on "GameObject:"
# en: The object the character's bust is built under at runtime.
characters.gameobject.tip = 运行时角色立绘所构建在的对象。

# Tooltip on "Enable typewriter voice"
# en: When off, this character's line types out silently.
characters.enableTypewriterVoice.tip = 关闭时，这个角色的台词会无声地逐字显示。

# Tooltip on "Reset all"

characters.resetField2.tip = 去掉本模组包对声音所做的一切设定，让角色像原来那样说话。

# Tooltip on a dropdown
# en: Male → freq 45, pitch 0.6–0.9. Female → freq 45, pitch 1.0–1.5. Custom → your own values.
characters.maleFreq45Pitch.tip = 男声 → 频率 45，音高 0.6–0.9。女声 → 频率 45，音高 1.0–1.5。自定义 → 你自己的数值。

# Tooltip on "Reset"

characters.resetField.tip3 = 把频率还原为这个角色自己的值。这一行的其他内容不变。

# Tooltip on "Has Blink frame"
# en: Untick for a bust with no blink art. Blink is not optional by omission — leaving the path empty makes the runtime treat the outfit as broken and skip the whole bust, so this is the way to say the character simply does not blink.
characters.hasBlink.tip = 若立绘没有眨眼美术资源，请取消勾选。眨眼不能靠留空来省略——把路径留空会让运行时认为这套服装有问题，从而跳过整张立绘，所以这才是表示“这个角色本来就不眨眼”的方式。

# Tooltip on the preview's size picker
# en: How big the bust is drawn. 1× puts one of the preview's pixels on one of the screen's, which is the sharpest it gets; 2× doubles every pixel and is just as exact. The half steps in between have to be resampled, so they look a little softer. The panel grows with it, and the choice is remembered.
characters.previewSize.tip = 半身像绘制得多大。1× 让预览的一个像素正好对应屏幕上的一个像素，这是最清晰的；2× 把每个像素翻倍，同样精确。中间的半档需要重新采样，所以看起来会稍软一些。面板会随之一起变大，你的选择也会被记住。

characters.mouthPicker.tip = 让立绘显示某一个特定的嘴部帧。仅用于预览——不会改动模组包里的任何内容。

characters.yappingToggle.tip = 在预览中让嘴部动起来，就像台词逐字显示时那样。仅用于预览。

# Tooltip on "Breathing"
# en: Run the idle breathing motion in the preview. Preview only.
characters.breathingToggle.tip = 在预览中运行待机呼吸动作。仅用于预览。

# Tooltip on a slider
# en: Preview-only: how far the bust bobs, in preview pixels. Match it to the game by eye.
characters.breathingDepth.tip = 仅用于预览：立绘上下起伏的幅度，单位是预览像素。凭眼睛与游戏对齐即可。

# Text
# en: Up to 12 buttons per place. Order = runtime instantiation order = on-screen left→right; use ▲▼ to reorder. The preview shows them numbered in this order. The mod-side loader resolves target names into level indices at load time, so two mods can both add buttons without colliding.
places.up12ButtonsPer = 每个地点最多 12 个按钮。顺序 = 运行时的创建顺序 = 屏幕上从左到右；用 ▲▼ 调整顺序。预览会按这个顺序给它们编号。模组端的加载器会在加载时把目标名称解析成关卡索引，因此两个模组都添加按钮也不会冲突。

# Box heading
# en: GameObjects (this place's scene hierarchy)
places.placeGameObjects = GameObject（这个地点的场景层级）

# Text
# en: Everything this place builds under its level, as one hierarchy. A GameObject with a Sprite is a layered sprite object (backdrop, prop, animated layer); leave the Sprite blank for a pure container that just groups children. Every node takes utility components (fade, disappear, random child, blink), nested children, and NPC placements. The NPCs node is the level's own NPCs object — always present, and the home for the NPC hierarchy; its subtree composes local transforms down the chain, so moving a container moves everything under it. Target any node from dialogue by Name with SetGameObjectActive / FadeSprite / MoveGameObject / SpinGameObject. Lower sorting draws further back.
places.everythingPlaceBuildsUnder = 这个地点在它的关卡下构建的一切，作为一整个层级。带精灵图的 GameObject 就是分层的精灵对象（背景、道具、动画图层）；若只想要一个用来归拢子对象的纯容器，把精灵图留空即可。每个节点都能加工具组件（淡入、淡出、随机子对象、闪烁）、嵌套子对象和 NPC 摆放。NPCs 节点就是关卡自己的 NPC 对象——它始终存在，也是 NPC 层级的归属之处；它的子树会逐级叠加局部 transform，所以移动一个容器就会移动它下面的一切。在对话里可用 SetGameObjectActive / FadeSprite / MoveGameObject / SpinGameObject 按“名称”定位任意节点。排序值越小，画得越靠后。

Blank = vanilla behaviour. Written as secondaryMaskSprite.

places.boundMasktexFRONTSprite.tip = 仅作为 _MaskTex 绑定到“前景”精灵图的材质上。后景精灵图以及该地点中的所有 GameObject 都不受影响。它按线性数据而非颜色加载，因为它承载的是数值而不是像素。写作 maskSprite。

# Tooltip on "Same as front"
# en: On (the default for an existing place) makes the back sprite copy the front, exactly as before this setting existed. Turn it off to give the level depth.
places.sameFront.tip = 开启（已有地点的默认值）会让后景精灵图照抄前景，和这个设置出现之前完全一样。关闭它，关卡才会有纵深。

# Tooltip on a slider
# en: The back sprite. THIS is what creates the depth — set it away from the front. Vanilla uses 0.1 or 0.5 for a far background, 1.5 for one that overshoots the room. Written as parallaxSecondaryStrength.
places.backSpriteWhatCreates.tip = 后景精灵图。纵深正是由“它”产生的——把它设得与前景不同。原版对远景用 0.1 或 0.5，对超出房间幅度的用 1.5。写作 parallaxSecondaryStrength。

# Tooltip on a text box
# en: The back layer's own drift. Setting it away from the front is what creates depth.
places.backLayerSOwn.tip = 后景图层自身的漂移。把它设得与前景不同，才会产生纵深。

# Tooltip on "Reverse back"
# en: Front sprite drifts WITH the cursor instead of against it.
places.reverseBack.tip = 前景精灵图“跟随”光标漂移，而不是与之相反。

# Tooltip on "Reverse front"
# en: Back sprite drifts opposite the front art. Vanilla does this once, in 53_Hotelroom.
places.reverseFront.tip = 后景精灵图与前景美术反向漂移。原版只这么做过一次，在 53_Hotelroom。

# Tooltip on a dropdown

places.whetherGameSRain.tip = 游戏的雨雪是否在这里生效。“无”表示没有天气，“室内”是透过窗户看到的弱化版本，“室外”是完整效果。

# en: Click a node to select it. + Child and + Sibling copy everything from the selected node except its text (and its tag), and land directly under it; + Root takes only the speaker. Naming an actor who has spoken earlier in this conversation brings back the expression and outfit they last wore. Drag a node to move it (with its children): drop on the upper or lower edge of a row to place it before or after as a sibling, drop on the middle to make it a child, or drop below the list to make it a root. Ctrl+C / Ctrl+V copy a node and its children and paste them below the selection; Del removes the selected node. Nodes play top to bottom, exactly as listed.
dialogues.clickNodeSelectChild = 点击节点即可选中它。“+ 子节点”和“+ 同级”会复制选中节点的一切，只除了它的文本（和标签），并落在它正下方。拖动节点可移动它（连同子节点）：放在某一行的上边缘或下边缘，就作为同级放在它前面或后面；放在中间，就成为它的子节点；放到列表下方，就成为根节点。Ctrl+C / Ctrl+V 可复制一个节点及其子节点，并粘贴到所选项下方；Del 移除选中的节点。节点自上而下播放，与列出的顺序完全一致。

# Text
# en: This node is one of a Choice's options, so it has no Actions on start. When an option counts as having started is not something the pack can rely on — it may be the moment the menu is drawn rather than the moment the player picks it, which would run the actions for options nobody chose. Put them on Actions on finish, which happens when the option has actually been taken.
dialogues.nodeOneChoiceS = 这个节点是某个“选择”的一个选项，所以它没有“开始时的动作”。一个选项从什么时候算作已开始，并不是模组包能依赖的——那可能是菜单被绘制出来的时刻，而不是玩家点选它的时刻，那样就会把没人选的选项的动作也执行掉。请把它们放到“结束时的动作”，那发生在这个选项确实被选中之后。

# Tooltip on "Runtime name:"
# en: Stable identifier — what jumps, logs and other packs reference this dialogue by. Filled in from the display name while you have not set one yourself; type here and it stops following, so an established dialogue is never renamed underneath whatever already points at it.
dialogues.runtimeName.tip = 稳定标识符——跳转、日志和其他模组包都用它来引用这段对话。在你没有自行设定之前，它会跟随显示名称自动填写；一旦你在这里输入，它就不再跟随，这样已经确立的对话就不会在已有引用之下被悄悄改名。

# Tooltip on a text box
# en: The name the pack itself uses — what the runtime calls this in its log, what validation messages name, and what another pack would reference it by. It is written for you from the display name, so there is nothing to fill in; it is shown because those three places quote it back at you. Select it to copy.
dialogues.namePackItselfUses.tip = 模组包自己使用的名称——运行时日志里写的、验证消息里提到的，也是别的模组包引用它时用的。它会根据显示名称自动写好，所以这里没什么要填；之所以显示出来，是因为那三处都会把它原样引用给你看。选中即可复制。

# Tooltip on "Prioritize this dialogue over vanilla"
# en: Suppresses the vanilla room's own entry dialogue while this one's start conditions hold, so yours plays instead of competing with it. Only available in vanilla rooms that have an entry dialogue to suppress.
dialogues.prioritizeDialogueOverVanilla.tip = 在这段对话的开始条件成立期间，压住原版房间自己的进场对话，让你的这段播放，而不是与它相互抢占。只有在本身就有进场对话可压住的原版房间里才可用。

# Tooltip on "Also leave on the Talk button (replayable)"
# en: Play on arrival like normal, AND stay on the vanilla Talk button so the player can hear it again — for as long as its start conditions still hold. Without this a dialogue interrupts once and is then gone until its conditions fail and pass again. Greyed out when 'Wait for Talk button' is on, which already leaves it there.
dialogues.alsoLeaveTalkButton.tip = 照常在进入时播放，同时还留在游戏的“交谈”按钮上，让玩家可以再听一次——只要它的开始条件仍然成立。没有这一项时，对话只会插入一次，之后就消失，直到它的条件先不成立、再重新成立。当“等待交谈按钮”打开时，这一项会变灰，因为那一项本来就把它留在那里了。

# Tooltip on "Wait for Talk button (no auto-play)"
# en: Don't start on arrival: the dialogue parks behind the vanilla Talk button, and the player starts it by clicking (with the normal cinematic fade). No jump-scare on level entry. Greyed out when the replay option above is on — that one plays on arrival, which is the opposite of waiting.
dialogues.waitTalkButtonNo.tip = 进入时不会开始：对话停在游戏的“交谈”按钮后面，由玩家点击来开始（带有正常的过场淡入淡出）。进关卡时不会被吓一跳。当上面的重播选项打开时，这一项会变灰——那一项是进入时就播放，与“等待”正好相反。

# Tooltip on "Queue behind active dialogue"
# en: If another dialogue is playing when this one's conditions pass, wait and start right after it ends. Unchecked (original behavior): it misses that window and only fires when its conditions trigger again while nothing is playing.
dialogues.queueBehindActiveDialogue.tip = 如果这段对话的条件成立时另有对话正在播放，就先等着，等它结束后立刻开始。不勾选（原本的行为）：它会错过那个时机，只有当它的条件在没有任何对话播放时再次成立才会触发。

# Tooltip on "Set for condition debugging (F12)"
# en: In-game, pressing F12 logs a pass/fail breakdown of this dialogue's start conditions (with current variable values) to the BepInEx console. Diagnostic only — no gameplay effect.
dialogues.setConditionDebuggingF12.tip = 在游戏里按 F12，会把这段对话开始条件的逐条通过/未通过明细（连同变量的当前值）输出到 BepInEx 控制台。仅用于诊断——不影响玩法。

# Tooltip on a text box
# en: When several dialogues become eligible on the same tick, the highest priority starts first (ties: manifest order). Default 0.
dialogues.whenSeveralDialoguesBecome.tip = 当同一帧里有多段对话都符合条件时，优先级最高的先开始（相同则按清单顺序）。默认 0。

# Tooltip on "+ Add group"

dialogues.addDialogueStartConditionGroup.tip = 添加一个“与/或”分组，像括号一样把条件括起来

dialogues.copyStartConditions.tip = 把这些开始条件（固定的关卡条件除外）复制到剪贴板

# en: Append clipboard conditions
dialogues.pasteStartConditions.tip = 追加剪贴板里的条件

dialogues.overwriteStartConditions.tip = 用剪贴板里的内容替换这些开始条件（保留固定的关卡条件）

# Tooltip on a toggle button
# en: Draw each line in the list the way the game draws it — its own font, outline and drop shadow, the speaker's name in their colour, on a dark panel. Costs the spelling squiggles on those rows; they stay in the Text box below the list.
dialogues.gameLookSwitch.tip = 按游戏的样子绘制列表中的每一行——游戏自己的字体、描边和投影，说话者的名字用他们的颜色，衬在深色面板上。代价是这些行上不再显示拼写波浪线；它们仍会出现在列表下方的“文本”框里。

# Tooltip on "+ Root"
# en: Add a new top-level node (no parent). It takes the speaker from the line it follows - actor, expression and outfit - and nothing else, because a root starts a fresh strand.
dialogues.addDialogueRootNode.tip = 添加一个新的顶层节点（没有父节点）。

# Tooltip on "+ Child"
# en: Add a child of the currently-selected node, copying its actor, expression, outfit, conditions and actions — everything but the text and the jump tag.
dialogues.addDialogueChildNode.tip = 为当前选中的节点添加一个子节点，并复制它的演员、表情、服装、条件和动作——除了文本和跳转标签之外的一切。

# Tooltip on "+ Sibling"
# en: Add a node directly below the selected one, sharing its parent (or another root when the selection is a root). Copies everything but the text and the jump tag.
dialogues.addDialogueSiblingNode.tip = 在选中节点的正下方添加一个节点，与它同父（若选中的是根节点，则新增另一个根节点）。除文本和跳转标签外，其余全部复制。

# Tooltip on "− Remove"
# en: Delete the selected node. References to it from other nodes' children + the root list are scrubbed automatically.
dialogues.removeDialogue.tip3 = 删除选中的节点。其他节点的子节点列表以及根节点列表中对它的引用都会被自动清理。

dialogues.nodeKind.tip = “文本”播放一句台词。“选择”会把这个节点的子节点作为选项提供给玩家挑选。“随机”则由它自己从中挑一个。

dialogues.choiceOption.tip = 这个节点是某个“选择”的一个选项——也就是玩家点的那个按钮。它的类型由父节点决定。

# Tooltip on a dropdown

dialogues.nodeActorPicker.tip = 谁说这句台词，以及之后的每一句，直到另一个节点改变它为止。这里填角色的运行时名称；台词上显示的是该角色的显示名称。

# Tooltip on a dropdown

dialogues.whichExpressionSpeakerWears.tip = 从这个节点起，说话者使用哪种表情。

# Tooltip on a dropdown
# en: Switch the actor to one of their outfits for this node onward. Pick an outfit, or type $variableName to use the outfit named by a pack variable's current value (so this node chooses the outfit dynamically). Leave blank to keep the actor's current bust.
dialogues.switchActorOneTheir.tip = 从这个节点起，把演员切换到他的某套服装。可以选一套服装，也可以输入 $变量名，用某个模组包变量当前值所指定的服装（这样这个节点就能动态选择服装）。留空则保持演员当前的立绘。

# Tooltip on a button
# en: Bold  (Ctrl+B) — wraps the selected words in <b></b>, or opens an empty pair where the cursor is.
dialogues.markupBold.tip = 加粗（Ctrl+B）——把选中的文字包进 <b></b>，若没有选中则在光标处插入一对空标签。

# Tooltip on a button
# en: Italic  (Ctrl+I) — wraps the selected words in <i></i>, or opens an empty pair where the cursor is.
dialogues.markupItalic.tip = 斜体（Ctrl+I）——把选中的文字包进 <i></i>，若没有选中则在光标处插入一对空标签。

# Tooltip on a button
# en: Colour  (Ctrl+Shift+C) — pick a colour, and the selected words are wrapped in <color=#RRGGBB></color>, or an empty pair opens where the cursor is. The value can be edited in place; the words take the colour as you type it.
dialogues.markupColor.tip = 颜色（Ctrl+Shift+C）——选一个颜色，选中的文字就会被包进 <color=#RRGGBB></color>，若没有选中则在光标处插入一对空标签。数值可以就地编辑；文字会随你的输入立刻变色。

# Tooltip on a button
# en: Size  (Ctrl+Shift+S) — wraps the selection in <size=70%></size>. The one tag the game uses in its own lines, for a muttered aside.
dialogues.markupSize.tip = 字号（Ctrl+Shift+S）——把选中内容包进 <size=70%></size>。这是游戏在自己台词里唯一用到的标签，用于低声的旁白。

# Tooltip on a part of the window
# en: The line itself. [PV:name] is replaced with a pack variable. {PC} is the player's name, and {M} {D} {B} are what this player calls their mother, father and brother — write those rather than the words, because players choose them. Markup is shown as the player will see it: the tags are set apart in their own colour and the words they wrap take their formatting. The game’s own tokens — {PC} and the rest — are marked the same way, in place, because a name goes there rather than those characters.
dialogues.nodeTextBox.tip = 台词本身。[PV:name] 会被替换成模组包变量。{PC} 是玩家的名字，{M} {D} {B} 则是这名玩家对母亲、父亲和兄弟的称呼——请写这些标记而不是具体的词，因为它们由玩家自己选择。标记会按玩家将看到的样子显示：标签以自己的颜色单独呈现，被包住的文字带上相应格式。游戏自己的标记——{PC} 等——也以同样方式就地标出，因为那里放的是一个名字，而不是这几个字符。

# Under a line's Text box. {size} is the size the game shrinks the line to, from its usual 38.

dialogues.fit.shrinks = 游戏中这句台词会缩小到 {size} pt 才能放进对话框。

# en: Too long for the dialogue box: even at its smallest size it runs out of the box in game. Split it into two lines.
dialogues.fit.spills = 对话框放不下：即使缩到最小，游戏里也会超出对话框。请把它拆成两句台词。

# Tooltip on a dropdown

dialogues.wherePlayGoesAfter.tip = 这个节点之后播放往哪走。“继续”会移到列表中的下一个节点，无论它是不是子节点。“退出”结束对话。“跳转”会从带有下面所指标签的节点继续。

# Text
# en: Comma-separated list of text patterns. When any pattern appears in a dialogue node's text on node-start, the SFX fires automatically. If the effect has variants, one is picked at random each time — add them by putting files named <name>_1, <name>_2, … beside the base file (numbered without gaps). Convention: asterisk-bracketed lowercase tokens like *plap*, *smooch*. One SFX can carry multiple patterns — *yank*,*yeet* both fire the same clip just like the host mod does. Leave blank for an SFX that only plays via explicit PlaySFX actions.
sfx.commaSeparatedListText = 以逗号分隔的文本匹配词列表。节点开始时，只要对话节点的文本里出现其中任何一个词，这个音效就会自动播放。如果该效果有变体，每次都会随机挑一个——把名为 <name>_1、<name>_2… 的文件（编号连续、不跳号）放在基础文件旁边即可添加。惯例：用星号括起来的小写词，例如 *plap*、*smooch*。同一个音效可以带多个匹配词——*yank*,*yeet* 会触发同一段音频，与原版模组一致。若某个音效只应由明确的 PlaySFX 动作播放，请留空。

# Box heading
# en: Repeat for each (optional)
integration.repeatEachOptional = 逐项重复（可选）

# Text
# en: Runs this whole rule once per value, so one rule can cover a set of subjects instead of copying it per subject. Values: a comma-separated list (A,B,C), or $variableName naming a List variable (or a String variable holding CSV) — so the set can change at runtime. Each value is substituted wherever {name} appears in any condition or action, including branches: e.g. Location_{char} as a variable name, or {char}/Slot as a GameObject path. It composes with the rest of the syntax — $Prefix_{char} dereferences the per-value variable. Every value gets its own edge state and its own Timer deadlines, so values behave exactly like the separate rules they replace (tick 'stagger' on a Timer to spread their first fire apart). Leave blank to run the rule once.
integration.runsWholeRuleOnce = 让整条规则对每个值各执行一次，这样一条规则就能覆盖一组对象，而不必为每个对象复制一份。值可以是：以逗号分隔的列表（A,B,C），也可以是 $变量名，指向一个列表变量（或存着 CSV 的字符串变量）——这样这组值可以在运行时改变。每个值都会替换掉任何条件或动作中出现的 {name}，包括分支里的：例如把 Location_{char} 用作变量名，或把 {char}/Slot 用作 GameObject 路径。它也能和其余语法组合——$Prefix_{char} 会解引用该值对应的变量。每个值都有自己的边沿状态和自己的计时器期限，所以这些值的行为与它们所替代的那些独立规则完全一样（在计时器上勾选“错开开始”即可把它们的首次触发岔开）。留空则整条规则只执行一次。

# en: For each value in:
integration.eachValue = 遍历以下各值：

# Text
# en: If the rule's main conditions fail, these branches are tried top to bottom and the FIRST one whose conditions pass fires its actions. Edge trigger modes fire whenever the winning branch changes — ideal for schedule-style cascades (if slot A → …, else if slot B → …, else → …).
integration.ifRuleSMain = 如果规则的主条件不满足，就自上而下依次尝试这些分支，条件满足的“第一个”分支会执行它的动作。边沿触发模式会在获胜分支发生变化时触发——非常适合日程式的层层判断（如果是位置 A → …，否则如果是位置 B → …，否则 → …）。

# Box heading
# en: Rule body (JSON)
integration.ruleBodyJSON = 规则主体（JSON）

# Text
# en: Edit the rule's trigger mode + conditions + actions as a JSON object. Format: { "triggerMode": "OnRisingEdge", "conditions": [...], "actions": [...], "branches": [ { "conditions": [...], "actions": [...] } ] }. branches is the optional else-if chain (first match wins; empty conditions = plain Else). The conditions / actions arrays carry the same shape as dialogue nodes' actionsOnStart.
integration.editRuleSTrigger = 以一个 JSON 对象来编辑规则的触发模式 + 条件 + 动作。格式：{ "triggerMode": "OnRisingEdge", "conditions": [...], "actions": [...], "branches": [ { "conditions": [...], "actions": [...] } ] }。branches 是可选的“否则如果”链（第一个匹配的生效；条件为空 = 普通的 Else）。conditions / actions 数组的结构与对话节点的 actionsOnStart 相同。

integration.whatCalledEditorS.tip = 这个东西在编辑器自己的列表里叫什么，也是你唯一需要填写的名字——旁边的运行时名称会一直跟着它，直到你自己设定一个。

# Tooltip on "Runtime name:"
# en: Stable identifier — used in logs and for the rule's edge tracking. Filled in from the display name while you have not set one yourself; type here and it stops following, so an established rule is never renamed underneath whatever already points at it.
integration.runtimeName.tip = 稳定标识符——用于日志以及这条规则的边沿跟踪。在你没有自行设定之前，它会跟随显示名称自动填写；一旦你在这里输入，它就不再跟随，这样已经确立的规则就不会在已有引用之下被悄悄改名。

# Tooltip on a text box
# en: The name the pack itself uses — what the runtime calls this in its log, what validation messages name, and what another pack would reference it by. It is written for you from the display name, so there is nothing to fill in; it is shown because those three places quote it back at you. Select it to copy.
integration.namePackItselfUses.tip = 模组包自己使用的名称——运行时日志里写的、验证消息里提到的，也是别的模组包引用它时用的。它会根据显示名称自动写好，所以这里没什么要填；之所以显示出来，是因为那三处都会把它原样引用给你看。选中即可复制。

# Tooltip on a text box
# en: Free text — shown in the editor only, not at runtime. Useful for noting why a rule exists.
integration.freeTextShownEditor.tip = 自由文本——只在编辑器里显示，运行时不会出现。适合记下这条规则存在的原因。

# Tooltip on a dropdown
# en: OnRisingEdge = fires once when conditions become true. OnFallingEdge = fires once when they stop. WhilePassing = fires every frame conditions match. OnSceneLoad / OnDayChange = one-shot per lifecycle event.
integration.triggerMode.tip = OnRisingEdge = 条件由不满足变为满足时触发一次。OnFallingEdge = 条件不再满足时触发一次。WhilePassing = 条件满足期间每帧都触发。OnSceneLoad / OnDayChange = 每次生命周期事件触发一次。

# Tooltip on "Set for condition debugging"
# en: Log what this rule decides to the BepInEx console: which branch won, which won last tick, and whether it fired — plus a per-condition breakdown with live values whenever no branch passes. Logged only when the decision changes, so it won't flood. Diagnostic only; no gameplay effect.
integration.ruleDebug.tip = 把这条规则的判断写到 BepInEx 控制台：哪个分支获胜、上一帧是哪个获胜、以及是否触发了——当没有任何分支通过时，还会逐条列出条件及其实时取值。只有在判断发生变化时才记录，所以不会刷屏。仅用于诊断；不影响玩法。

# Tooltip on a dropdown
# en: A List variable as $name, or a literal comma-separated list. Blank = no repetition.
integration.listVariableNameLiteral.tip = 用 $名称 表示的列表变量，或一个以逗号分隔的字面列表。留空 = 不重复。

# Tooltip on a text box
# en: What to write inside braces in the conditions/actions. Defaults to 'item', i.e. {item}. Set it to 'char' to write {char}.
integration.whatWriteInsideBraces.tip = 在条件/动作里的花括号中要写什么。默认是 'item'，也就是 {item}。改成 'char' 就写 {char}。

# Tooltip on "Code mode (edit conditions + actions as JSON)"
# en: When checked, the picker rows below are replaced with a JSON textbox. The textbox round-trips losslessly with the picker — toggling between modes preserves all data. Parse errors keep the toggle in code mode until you fix the JSON.
integration.codeModeEditConditions.tip = 勾选后，下面的选择器行会被一个 JSON 文本框取代。这个文本框与选择器可以无损互转——在两种模式之间切换不会丢失任何数据。解析出错时，开关会停留在代码模式，直到你修好 JSON。

# Tooltip on "+ Add group"
# en: Add an AND/OR group to bundle conditions like parentheses
integration.addIntegrationConditionGroup.tip = 添加一个“与/或”分组，像括号一样把条件括起来

# Tooltip on "Copy all"
# en: Copy all conditions here to the clipboard
integration.copyConditions.tip = 把这里的所有条件复制到剪贴板

# Tooltip on "Paste"
# en: Append clipboard conditions (works across dialogues, rules and level hooks)
integration.pasteConditions.tip = 追加剪贴板里的条件（可跨对话、规则和关卡挂钩使用）

# Tooltip on "Overwrite"
# en: Replace all conditions here with the clipboard
integration.overwriteConditions.tip = 用剪贴板里的内容替换这里的所有条件

# Tooltip on "Copy all"
# en: Copy these actions to the clipboard
integration.copyConditions.tip2 = 把这些动作复制到剪贴板

# Tooltip on "Paste"
# en: Append clipboard actions (works across dialogues, rules and level hooks)
integration.pasteConditions.tip2 = 追加剪贴板里的动作（可跨对话、规则和关卡挂钩使用）

# Tooltip on "Overwrite"
# en: Replace these actions with the clipboard
integration.overwriteConditions.tip2 = 用剪贴板里的内容替换这些动作

# Tooltip on "+ Add else-if branch"
# en: Add a branch tried when the conditions above (and every earlier branch) fail. Leave its conditions empty to make it a plain Else.
integration.addBranch.tip = 添加一个分支，仅在上面的条件（以及之前所有分支）都不满足时才尝试。把它的条件留空，它就成了普通的“否则”。

# Tooltip on a text box
# en: This rule's conditions and actions as JSON. The two views round-trip, so you can move between them freely; a parse error keeps you here until it is fixed.
integration.ruleSConditionsActions.tip = 以 JSON 表示的这条规则的条件和动作。两种视图可以无损互转，所以你可以随意切换；解析出错时会把你留在这里，直到改好为止。

# Tooltip on "+ UI"

# en: Start from a screen the game already has. Change what is there, add to it, and only the differences are saved.
ui.addVanillaUi.tip = 从游戏已有的界面开始。改动其中的内容、往里添加东西，只有差异部分会被保存。

# Tooltip on a tick box
# en: Ticked, the screen is one object among the game's own and follows that canvas - which is right for anything belonging to normal play. Clear it to put the screen on a layer of its own, which is what makes the draw order below mean anything.
ui.tickedScreenOneObject.tip = 勾选时，这个界面就是游戏自有对象中的一个，并跟随那个画布——这对属于正常游戏流程的内容是对的。取消勾选可以把界面放到它自己的层上，这样下面的绘制顺序才有意义。

# Tooltip on a tick box
# en: Most UI is not on screen at the start - the game keeps 34 of its 49 canvases switched off and turns one on when its moment arrives.
ui.mostUINotScreen.tip = 大多数 UI 一开始并不在屏幕上——游戏把它 49 个画布里的 34 个关着，等时机到了才打开其中一个。

# Tooltip on a tick box
# en: Buttons on this screen click when pressed, using the game's own button sound unless you name a different one below. Untick for a screen that should be silent.
ui.buttonsScreenClickWhen.tip = 这个界面上的按钮在按下时会发出声音，使用游戏自己的按钮音效，除非你在下面指定别的。若某个界面应当安静无声，请取消勾选。

# Tooltip on a dropdown
# en: Leave blank for the game's own button click, which is what buttons do by default. Otherwise one of the pack's own sounds by its key, or one of the game's by its name - a pack's own wins where both exist, so you can replace a game sound with your own. A single button can still say something different.
ui.leaveBlankGameS.tip = 留空则使用游戏自己的按钮点击声，这也是按钮的默认行为。否则可以填模组包自己某个声音的键名，或游戏某个声音的名称——两者都存在时以模组包的为准，所以你可以用自己的声音替换游戏的。单个按钮仍然可以另行指定。

# Tooltip on a text box
# en: Draw order against other UI that is not hidden with the game's interface. Higher is nearer the front.
ui.drawOrderAgainstOther.tip = 相对于其他不随游戏界面隐藏的 UI 的绘制顺序。数值越高越靠前。

# Tooltip on a dropdown
# en: Every UI the game has that can be drawn here. One entry per screen, not per canvas — the main canvas alone holds twenty-one of them.
ui.everyUIGameHas.tip = 游戏中所有可以在这里绘制的 UI。按界面列出，而不是按画布——光是主画布就有二十一个。

# Tooltip on "Visible at start"

# Tooltip on "+ Condition"

# Tooltip on a dropdown

# Tooltip on "Padding"

ui.padding.tip = 距离边缘的内缩：左、右、上、下。

# Tooltip on "Set width"
# en: Take over each child's width instead of leaving it as authored. With nothing else declaring a size, tick 'Share width' as well or they collapse - that is Unity's own behaviour, not a quirk here.
ui.setWidth.tip = 接管每个子对象的宽度，而不是保留它们原本的设定。如果没有别的东西声明尺寸，请同时勾选“均分宽度”，否则它们会被压扁——这是 Unity 自己的行为，不是这里的怪癖。

# Tooltip on "Share width"
# en: Divide the leftover space between the children. This is what makes a row of items fill it evenly.
ui.shareWidth.tip = 把剩余空间分给各个子对象。正是这一点让一行条目均匀地铺满空间。

# Tooltip on "Reverse"
# en: Fill from the far end - right to left, or bottom to top.
ui.reverse.tip = 从另一端开始填充——从右到左，或从下到上。

# Tooltip on a text box
# en: How many columns, or how many rows - whichever the setting beside it fixes.
ui.howManyColumnsHow.tip = 多少列，或多少行——取决于旁边的设置固定的是哪一项。

# Tooltip on a dropdown
# en: Flexible fits as many across as the width allows; the other two fix the count.
ui.flexibleFitsManyAcross.tip = “弹性”会按宽度允许的数量横向排布；另外两种则固定数量。

# Tooltip on a tick box
# en: Nineteen things in rows of seven leave a last row of five, and it is normally packed against the start corner - so a grid that is even everywhere else ends lopsided. This centres that row, and only that row. Unity has no such setting, so the preview and the game both do it here.
ui.nineteenThingsRowsSeven.tip = 十九个东西按每行七个排，最后一行剩五个，通常会挤在起始的那一角——于是别处都很整齐的网格，结尾却歪了。这个选项会把那一行居中，而且只居中那一行。Unity 没有这样的设置，所以预览和游戏都在这里自己处理。

# Tooltip on a text box
# en: Tint while the pointer is over this object, as #RRGGBB or #RRGGBBAA. Leave blank for no hover feedback. The game's own hover is per-button artwork rather than a rule, so there is nothing general to copy - this is the part a pack can state.
ui.tintWhilePointerOver.tip = 指针悬停在这个对象上时的着色，格式为 #RRGGBB 或 #RRGGBBAA。留空则没有悬停反馈。游戏自己的悬停效果是逐按钮的美术资源，而不是一条规则，所以没有通用做法可抄——这正是模组包可以自己声明的部分。

# Tooltip on a dropdown
# en: What THIS object sounds like when clicked. Blank follows the screen, which is usually what you want - set this only where one button should differ. A sound named here plays even on a screen whose buttons are otherwise silent.
ui.whatObjectSoundsLike.tip = “这个”对象被点击时的声音。留空则跟随整个界面，通常这就是你想要的——只在某个按钮需要不同的时候才设置它。这里指定的声音，即使在按钮本来静音的界面上也会响。

# Tooltip on "+ Only if…"
# en: What has to be true for this click to do anything. Failing it runs none of the actions - the button still presses, it just does nothing.
ui.addClickCondition.tip = 这次点击要生效需要满足什么。不满足时一个动作都不会执行——按钮照样按下去，只是什么也不做。

# Tooltip on "+ Action"
# en: Run one of the pack's actions when this is clicked - the same actions dialogues and rules use.
ui.addAction.tip = 点击时执行模组包的某个动作——和对话、规则使用的是同一批动作。

# Tooltip on a dropdown
# en: A TextMeshPro font asset, not a font file. The same typeface appears more than once with different outlines and shadows, and they are not interchangeable.
ui.textmeshproFontAssetNot.tip = 一个 TextMeshPro 字体资源，而不是字体文件。同一种字型会以不同的描边和阴影出现多次，它们之间不能互换。

# Text
# en: A task of yours in the game's quest. The game treats it as one of its own: a task before it holds the ones after it back, and the quest does not finish until it is done. Nothing of the game's names it, so only what you give it completes it: its completion conditions, a Quest action of yours, or its subtasks. Quest actions and conditions name it on the Vanilla side, by the game's quest and this runtime name.
quests.taskYoursGameS = 你加进游戏任务里的一个目标。游戏会把它当作自己的目标：排在它前面的目标会把后面的拦住，而任务要等它完成才算结束。游戏里没有任何东西会指名它，所以只有你给它的东西才能让它完成：它的完成条件、你的某个任务动作，或者它的子目标。任务动作和条件在“原版”一侧指名它，使用游戏的任务加上这个运行时名称。

# Text
# en: The game still moves its own quest: its scenes complete its tasks as they always did. Descriptions, hidden tasks and actions change nothing about when the quest finishes. Tasks you add or take out do - see the warning above. Use the QuestState and QuestCounter conditions to ask where a player has got to in it, and the Quest action to move it or your tasks in it.
quests.gameStillMovesOwn = 游戏仍然推进它自己的任务：它的场景会像以往一样完成它的目标。说明、隐藏的目标和动作都不会改变任务何时结束。你添加或拿掉的目标则会——请看上面的警告。用 QuestState 和 QuestCounter 条件可以查询玩家在其中进行到哪里，用任务动作可以推进它或推进你在其中的目标。

# Text
# en: A quest moves along by itself: it starts when its start conditions pass, and each task completes when its own completion conditions pass, running its completion actions. Only a task with no subtasks has completion conditions and actions - a task with subtasks finishes through them. A dialogue or a rule can still drive a quest with the Quest action, and check on it with the QuestState and QuestCounter conditions. Completing a quest gives the player the same reward the game's own quests do.
quests.questMovesAlongItself = 任务会自行推进：开始条件满足时它就开始，而每个目标在自己的完成条件满足时完成，并执行它的完成动作。只有没有子目标的目标才有完成条件和动作——带子目标的目标通过子目标来完成。对话或规则仍然可以用任务动作来推动任务，并用 QuestState 和 QuestCounter 条件来查询它。完成一个任务会给玩家与游戏自身任务相同的奖励。
