[Docs: What a mod pack is]

# Docs: a topic's title
# en: What a mod pack is
docs.modPack.title = 什么是模组包

# Docs: the line under the topic's title
# en: The whole idea, before any of the detail.
docs.modPack.summary = 在进入细节之前，先说整体思路。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: A pack is one folder. Everything you author ends up in a single file inside it called modpack.json, and the images and audio sit beside that file.
docs.modPack.packOneFolderEverything = 一个模组包就是一个文件夹。你创作的一切最终都会落到其中一个叫 modpack.json 的文件里，图片和音频则放在这个文件旁边。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: You never edit modpack.json by hand. The editor writes it, and the tabs across the top are the things it can hold.
docs.modPack.neverEditModpackJson = 你永远不必手动编辑 modpack.json。它由编辑器写入，而顶部的各个选项卡就是它能容纳的内容。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Export turns the folder into one .smspack file. That is what you hand to players, and the only thing the game reads.
docs.modPack.exportTurnsFolderInto = 导出会把这个文件夹变成单个 .smspack 文件。那才是你交给玩家的东西，也是游戏唯一会读取的东西。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: The game does not know the editor exists. It reads your pack through the SMSModForge plugin, which is what actually builds your characters, rooms and conversations inside the running game.
docs.modPack.gameDoesNotKnow = 游戏并不知道编辑器的存在。它通过 SMSModForge 插件来读取你的模组包，而正是这个插件在运行中的游戏里真正构建出你的角色、房间和对话。

# Docs: a heading inside the topic
# en: Worth knowing
docs.modPack.heading.worthKnowing = 值得了解

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: A pack adds to the game rather than replacing it. Your places sit alongside the game's own, and a vanilla extension adds to a room the game already has instead of swapping it out.
docs.modPack.packAddsGameRather = 模组包是在游戏之上做加法，而不是替换游戏。你的地点与游戏自有的地点并存，而原版扩展是在游戏已有的房间上添加内容，而不是把它换掉。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Nothing you do in the editor touches your game installation until you export and copy the pack in yourself.
docs.modPack.nothingDoEditorTouches = 在你自己导出并把模组包复制过去之前，你在编辑器里做的任何事都不会碰你的游戏安装目录。

[Docs: The order to work in]

# Docs: a topic's title
# en: The order to work in
docs.orderWork.title = 制作顺序

# Docs: the line under the topic's title
# en: Which tabs depend on which, so you are not blocked halfway through.
docs.orderWork.summary = 哪些选项卡依赖哪些，免得你做到一半卡住。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: First
docs.orderWork.fileThenNewPack.term = 首先

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: File then New pack, and Save straight away. Previews and file pickers resolve paths against the pack folder, so much of the editor stays empty until that folder exists on disk.
docs.orderWork.fileThenNewPack = 先“文件”再“新建模组包”，然后马上保存。预览和文件选择器都相对模组包文件夹来解析路径，所以在该文件夹落到磁盘上之前，编辑器的大部分内容都是空的。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Who
docs.orderWork.charactersThenNpcsCharacter.term = 谁

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Characters, then NPCs. A character can speak; an NPC just stands in a room. Both want their art before anything else can point at them.
docs.orderWork.charactersThenNpcsCharacter = 先角色，再 NPC。角色能说话；NPC 只是站在房间里。两者都要先有美术资源，别的东西才能指向它们。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Where
docs.orderWork.placesThenMapButtons.term = 在哪

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Places, then Map Buttons. A place is a room. A map button is how a player reaches one.
docs.orderWork.placesThenMapButtons = 先地点，再地图按钮。地点就是一个房间。地图按钮是玩家抵达房间的方式。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: What they say
docs.orderWork.dialoguesDialogueBelongsLevel.term = 说什么

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Dialogues. A dialogue belongs to a level and its lines name characters, so both are worth having first.
docs.orderWork.dialoguesDialogueBelongsLevel = 对话。一段对话归属于某个关卡，它的台词又要指名角色，所以这两者值得先准备好。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: What plays
docs.orderWork.scenesMusicSFXWallpapers.term = 播放什么

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Scenes, Music, SFX and Wallpapers. These are things a dialogue or a rule can trigger, so add them before the logic that fires them.
docs.orderWork.scenesMusicSFXWallpapers = 场景、音乐、音效和壁纸。这些都是对话或规则可以触发的东西，所以要在触发它们的逻辑之前先加好。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: State and logic
docs.orderWork.variablesThenIntegrationVariable.term = 状态与逻辑

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Variables, then Integration. A variable has to exist before a condition or an action can name it.
docs.orderWork.variablesThenIntegrationVariable = 先变量，再集成。变量必须先存在，条件或动作才能指名它。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Last
docs.orderWork.validateF5FixLists.term = 最后

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Validate (F5), fix what it lists, then Export.
docs.orderWork.validateF5FixLists = 验证（F5），修好列出的问题，然后导出。

# Docs: a heading inside the topic
# en: Worth knowing
docs.orderWork.heading.worthKnowing = 值得了解

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: An NPC is defined once on the NPCs tab and then placed into a room from the Places tab, so you will come back to Places after making one.
docs.orderWork.npcDefinedOnceNpcs = NPC 在 NPC 选项卡里定义一次，然后从地点选项卡放进房间，所以做完一个之后你还会回到地点选项卡。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Nothing enforces this order. It is simply the order that avoids pointing at things you have not made yet.
docs.orderWork.nothingEnforcesOrderSimply = 没有什么强制这个顺序。它只是能避免你去指向尚未做出来的东西的顺序。

[Docs: How things point at each other]

# Docs: a topic's title
# en: How things point at each other
docs.thingsPointEachOther.title = 各部分如何相互指向

# Docs: the line under the topic's title
# en: The four ways one part of a pack names another, and where each turns up.
docs.thingsPointEachOther.summary = 模组包的一部分指名另一部分的四种方式，以及各自出现在哪里。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: By runtime name
docs.thingsPointEachOther.mostReferencesJustOther.term = 按运行时名称

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Most references are just the other item's runtime name: a dialogue node names a character, a placement names an NPC, a condition names a variable.
docs.thingsPointEachOther.mostReferencesJustOther = 大多数引用就是另一个条目的运行时名称：对话节点指名角色，摆放指名 NPC，条件指名变量。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: By place token
docs.thingsPointEachOther.anywhereFieldMeansRoom.term = 按地点标记

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Anywhere a field means a room or a level. vanilla:Name is one of the game's own levels; place:Key and self:Key are both a place your pack defines, spelt place: where the field means which level this belongs to and self: where it means where to go; pack:PackId.Key reaches a place in somebody else's pack.
docs.thingsPointEachOther.anywhereFieldMeansRoom = 凡是表示房间或关卡的字段。vanilla:名称 指游戏自有的关卡；place:键名 和 self:键名 都指你的模组包定义的地点——当字段表示“这属于哪个关卡”时写 place:，表示“去哪里”时写 self:；pack:模组包Id.键名 则能指向别人模组包里的地点。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: By hierarchy path
docs.thingsPointEachOther.anywhereFieldMeansOne.term = 按层级路径

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Anywhere a field means one particular object inside a room, written with arrows, as in NPCs > Shower > Anis > Naked. A path rather than a bare name because names repeat all over a level, and a bare name would find whichever copy the game happened to reach first.
docs.thingsPointEachOther.anywhereFieldMeansOne = 凡是表示房间内某个具体对象的字段，用箭头写出，例如 NPCs > Shower > Anis > Naked。用路径而不是光秃秃的名称，是因为同样的名称在关卡里到处都有，光给名称只会找到游戏碰巧先遇到的那一份。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: By file path
docs.thingsPointEachOther.artAudioRelativePack.term = 按文件路径

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Art and audio, relative to the pack folder.
docs.thingsPointEachOther.artAudioRelativePack = 美术和音频，相对于模组包文件夹。

# Docs: a heading inside the topic
# en: Worth knowing
docs.thingsPointEachOther.heading.worthKnowing = 值得了解

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: You pick all of these from dropdowns, but the boxes are editable and show the raw token once you have picked. That is expected: the token is what gets written to the pack, and the friendly name only lives in the list.
docs.thingsPointEachOther.pickAllTheseDropdowns = 这些你都可以从下拉框里选，但输入框是可编辑的，选完之后显示的是原始标记。这是正常的：写进模组包的正是那个标记，友好的名称只存在于列表里。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Validate flags a token that does not resolve and suggests near matches, which is usually enough to spot a typo or something renamed underneath it.
docs.thingsPointEachOther.validateFlagsTokenDoes = 验证会标出无法解析的标记，并给出相近的建议，通常这就足以发现拼写错误，或是被悄悄改过名字的东西。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Renaming an item leaves everything pointing at its old name. Variables are the one exception, where the rename is applied everywhere at once.
docs.thingsPointEachOther.renamingItemLeavesEverything = 重命名一个条目后，指向它的一切仍然指着旧名称。变量是唯一的例外，它的重命名会一次性应用到所有地方。

[Docs: Putting values into text]

# Docs: a topic's title
# en: Putting values into text
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.title = 把数值放进文本里

# Docs: the line under the topic's title
# en: Showing a variable inside a line, a label, or another value.
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.summary = 在台词、标签或另一个值里显示某个变量。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: [PV:name]
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.dialogueTextButtonLabels.term = [PV:name]

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Replaced with one of your pack's variables, in any text a player reads: dialogue lines, character names, quest titles, descriptions and tasks, button labels and the text on your screens. It keeps up as the value changes - except in a dialogue line, where it is read as the conversation is built.
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.dialogueTextButtonLabels = 在玩家会读到的任何文本里，被替换成你模组包的某个变量：对话台词、角色名字、任务的标题、描述和子任务、按钮文字，以及你界面上的文字。它会随着值的变化更新——只有在对话台词里，它在对话构建时读取。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: {PC}
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.dialogueTextPlayerS.term = {PC}

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: The player's name — whatever they chose at the start. Like the other words in braces here, it works in any text a player reads: dialogue lines, character names, quest titles, descriptions and tasks, button labels and the text on your screens.
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.dialogueTextPlayerS = 玩家的名字——他们一开始选的那个。和这里其他花括号里的词一样，它在玩家会读到的任何文本里都能用：对话台词、角色名字、任务的标题、描述和子任务、按钮文字，以及你界面上的文字。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: {M} / {D} / {B}
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.playerCallsTheirMother.term = {M} / {D} / {B}

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: What this player calls their mother, father and brother — Anna, Josef and Adrian. Players pick those words themselves, so the same line reads Mum for one and Mom for another. {B} works both ways: Adrian says it back to the player.
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.playerCallsTheirMother = 这名玩家对母亲、父亲和兄弟的称呼——即 Anna、Josef 和 Adrian。这些词由玩家自己选，所以同一句台词对一个人显示“妈”，对另一个人显示“妈妈”。{B} 是双向的：Adrian 也用它来称呼玩家。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: {S} / {DA}
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.wordChildFamilyS.term = {S} / {DA}

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: The word for a child of the family: {S} is what Anna and Josef call the player and Adrian, {DA} is what Mario calls Emma.
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.wordChildFamilyS = 对家里孩子的称呼：{S} 是 Anna 和 Josef 对玩家与 Adrian 的称呼，{DA} 是 Mario 对 Emma 的称呼。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: {F}
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.playerCallsFamily.term = {F}

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: What this player calls the family.
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.playerCallsFamily = 这名玩家对这个家庭的称呼。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Write these rather than the words. A line that says Mom says Mom to everybody, including the player who chose something else, and there is no way for them to tell it apart from the game getting it wrong.
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.writeTheseRatherThan = 请写这些标记，而不要写具体的词。写死“妈妈”的台词会对所有人都说“妈妈”，包括那些选了别的称呼的玩家，而他们完全无法分辨这是你写的，还是游戏出了错。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: These are the game's own: a list of words in its dialogue settings, each filled in as the text is shown, bold or coloured if the game says so. Those seven are the ones known to be on it. A word in braces that is not on the list is shown exactly as you typed it.
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.thoseSevenOnesKnown = 这些是游戏自己的：它对话设置里的一份词表，每个词在文本显示时被填入，游戏设定的话会加粗或上色。已知在这份表里的就是这七个。不在表里的花括号词会按你输入的原样显示。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: $name
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.actionConditionValueBox.term = $name

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: In an action or condition value box, stands for that variable's current value instead of a fixed one.
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.actionConditionValueBox = 在动作或条件的取值框里，它代表那个变量的当前值，而不是一个固定值。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: $$
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.literalDollarSignRare.term = $$

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: A literal dollar sign, for the rare case you need one.
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.literalDollarSignRare = 一个真正的美元符号，供少数确实需要它的场合使用。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: {item}
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.integrationRuleRepeatsOver.term = {item}

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: In an integration rule that repeats over a list, stands for whichever value the rule is running for.
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.integrationRuleRepeatsOver = 在按列表重复执行的集成规则里，它代表规则当前正在处理的那个值。

# Docs: a heading inside the topic
# en: Markup in a line
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.heading.markupLine = 台词中的标记

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: <size=70%>
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.setsTextSizeRest.term = <size=70%>

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Sets the text size for the rest of the line, as a percentage or a number. The game writes this in its own dialogue — a muttered aside, usually — so it is known to render in the speech bubble.
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.setsTextSizeRest = 为这句台词接下来的部分设定字号，可以用百分比或数字。游戏在自己的对话里就写过它——通常用于低声的旁白——所以可以确定它会在对话气泡里生效。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: <b> / <i> / <color=#FF6666>
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.boldItalicColouredText.term = <b> / <i> / <color=#FF6666>

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Bold, italic and coloured text. These are TextMeshPro's, which is what draws the bubble, and the fact that <size> works at all means markup is parsed rather than printed. But the game never uses these three in its own dialogue, so nobody here has watched one render — try a line before you rely on one.
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.boldItalicColouredText = 加粗、斜体和彩色文字。它们属于 TextMeshPro，也就是绘制气泡的那套系统；而 <size> 能生效这一点说明标记是被解析而不是被原样打印的。但游戏从未在自己的对话里用过这三个，所以这边没人亲眼见它们生效——在依赖它们之前，请先用一句台词试试。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Colours
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.writtenRRGGBBRRGGBBAATransparency.term = 颜色

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Written #RRGGBB, #RRGGBBAA with transparency, the short #RGB and #RGBA, or one of ten names: red, lightblue, blue, grey, black, green, white, orange, purple, yellow. Those are the forms the game's text engine reads; anything else leaves the words their ordinary colour. The Colour button picks one for you and writes six digits.
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.writtenRRGGBBRRGGBBAATransparency = 可写作 #RRGGBB、带透明度的 #RRGGBBAA、简写的 #RGB 和 #RGBA，或十个名称之一：red、lightblue、blue、grey、black、green、white、orange、purple、yellow。这些就是游戏文字引擎能读懂的写法；其他写法只会让文字保持原本的颜色。“颜色”按钮会替你挑一个，并写成六位数字。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: The formatting buttons over the Text box write these for you: Bold (Ctrl+B), Italic (Ctrl+I), Colour (Ctrl+Shift+C) and Size (Ctrl+Shift+S). Each one is a single step for Ctrl+Z.
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.formattingButtonsOverText = “文本”框上方的格式按钮会替你写出这些：加粗（Ctrl+B）、斜体（Ctrl+I）、颜色（Ctrl+Shift+C）和字号（Ctrl+Shift+S）。每一个对 Ctrl+Z 来说都算一步。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: A tag that is not understood is shown to the player exactly as you typed it, the same way a token in the wrong place is. If a line reads <b> in game, that is this.
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.tagNotUnderstoodShown = 无法识别的标签会原样显示给玩家，就像放错位置的标记一样。如果游戏里某句台词出现了 <b>，就是这个原因。

# Docs: a heading inside the topic
# en: Worth knowing
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.heading.worthKnowing = 值得了解

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: The two are separate. A braced token cannot see a pack variable, and [PV:name] does not resolve the game's names.
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.twoSeparateBracedToken = 两者是分开的。花括号标记看不到模组包变量，而 [PV:name] 也解析不了游戏的称呼。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: A condition or action can still read and write the game's own variables — that is the Source toggle on a Variable row. It is only the text token that is one-sided.
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.conditionActionCanStill = 条件或动作仍然可以读写游戏自己的变量——那就是“变量”行上的“来源”开关。单向的只有文本标记这一项。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: A variable that has never been set reads as empty. That usually means nothing happens, rather than an error.
docs.puttingValuesIntoText.variableHasNeverBeen = 从未赋过值的变量读出来是空的。通常这意味着什么都不会发生，而不是报错。

[Docs: When something does not work]

# Docs: a topic's title
# en: When something does not work
docs.whenSomethingDoesNotWork.title = 当某些东西不起作用时

# Docs: the line under the topic's title
# en: The three things to check, in the order worth checking them.
docs.whenSomethingDoesNotWork.summary = 要检查的三件事，以及值得检查的先后顺序。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Validate (F5)
docs.whenSomethingDoesNotWork.everyCheckAcrossWhole.term = 验证（F5）

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Every check across the whole pack, listed on this tab. Double-click a row to jump to whatever it is complaining about. Start here: most problems are a missing file or a name that no longer resolves.
docs.whenSomethingDoesNotWork.everyCheckAcrossWhole = 针对整个模组包的所有检查，都列在这个选项卡上。双击某一行即可跳到它所抱怨的地方。从这里开始：多数问题不过是文件缺失，或某个名称不再能解析。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: The previews
docs.whenSomethingDoesNotWork.theyComposeArtExactly.term = 预览

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: They compose your art exactly as the game lays it out, but they do not run the game's logic. Conditions, variables and rules are not evaluated, so anything gated on state is drawn regardless — a preview looking right does not mean the gate works.
docs.whenSomethingDoesNotWork.theyComposeArtExactly = 它们会完全按照游戏的排布方式来组合你的美术资源，但并不运行游戏的逻辑。条件、变量和规则都不会被求值，所以任何受状态控制的东西照样会被画出来——预览看着没问题，并不意味着那道条件真的管用。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: In-game logging
docs.whenSomethingDoesNotWork.flagDialogueConditionDebugging.term = 游戏内日志

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Flag a dialogue for condition debugging on the Dialogues tab, or a rule for decision logging on the Integration tab, and the game will log why it did or did not fire.
docs.whenSomethingDoesNotWork.flagDialogueConditionDebugging = 在对话选项卡把某段对话标记为条件调试，或在集成选项卡把某条规则标记为记录决策，游戏就会把它为何触发或未触发写进日志。

# Docs: a heading inside the topic
# en: Worth knowing
docs.whenSomethingDoesNotWork.heading.worthKnowing = 值得了解

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: A pack that does not appear at all is usually installed wrong rather than broken. The .smspack belongs in BepInEx/plugins/SMSModForge/ModPacks/ inside your game folder.
docs.whenSomethingDoesNotWork.packDoesNotAppear = 完全不出现的模组包，通常是装错了位置，而不是坏了。.smspack 应当放在游戏文件夹内的 BepInEx/plugins/SMSModForge/ModPacks/ 里。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: An empty preview on a pack you have not saved yet is expected. Previews resolve paths against the pack folder.
docs.whenSomethingDoesNotWork.emptyPreviewPackHave = 尚未保存的模组包预览是空的，这是正常现象。预览是相对模组包文件夹来解析路径的。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Both logging flags are stored in the pack, so tracking left switched on ships to players as extra logging. Clear them before a release.
docs.whenSomethingDoesNotWork.bothLoggingFlagsStored = 这两个日志标记都会保存在模组包里，所以一直开着跟踪就等于把额外的日志一起发给玩家。发布前请把它们清掉。

[Docs: Packs and the .smspack]

# Docs: a topic's title
# en: Packs and the .smspack
docs.packsSmspack.title = 模组包与 .smspack

# Docs: the line under the topic's title
# en: What a pack is made of, and what actually reaches the game.
docs.packsSmspack.summary = 模组包由什么构成，以及真正抵达游戏的是什么。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Pack folder
docs.packsSmspack.whereWorkHoldsModpack.term = 模组包文件夹

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Where you work. It holds modpack.json plus every image and audio file the pack uses, in whatever subfolders suit you.
docs.packsSmspack.whereWorkHoldsModpack = 你工作的地方。它包含 modpack.json，以及模组包用到的所有图片和音频文件，子文件夹怎么分由你决定。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: modpack.json
docs.packsSmspack.everythingAuthorEditorCharacters.term = modpack.json

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Everything you author in the editor: characters, places, dialogues, variables, rules. Media files sit beside it and are referred to by path.
docs.packsSmspack.everythingAuthorEditorCharacters = 你在编辑器里创作的一切：角色、地点、对话、变量、规则。媒体文件放在它旁边，通过路径引用。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: .smspack
docs.packsSmspack.singleFileGameLoads.term = .smspack

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: The single file the game loads, built by Export. It is a zip with modpack.json at the top and every other file keeping exactly the path it had in the folder.
docs.packsSmspack.singleFileGameLoads = 游戏加载的那个单一文件，由导出生成。它是一个 zip，modpack.json 在最上层，其他所有文件都保持它们在文件夹里原本的路径。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Installing
docs.packsSmspack.dropSmspackIntoBepinex.term = 安装

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Drop the .smspack into BepInEx/plugins/SMSModForge/ModPacks/ inside your game folder. See Exporting and installing.
docs.packsSmspack.dropSmspackIntoBepinex = 把 .smspack 放进游戏文件夹内的 BepInEx/plugins/SMSModForge/ModPacks/。参见“导出与安装”。

# Docs: a heading inside the topic
# en: Worth knowing
docs.packsSmspack.heading.worthKnowing = 值得了解

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Because paths inside the archive match the folder exactly, moving a sprite and re-exporting is all it takes — nothing needs re-pointing.
docs.packsSmspack.becausePathsInsideArchive = 由于压缩包内的路径与文件夹完全一致，移动一张精灵图后重新导出就够了——不需要再去改任何指向。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: A pack owns its whole folder. Export bundles everything inside it, subfolders included, whether the pack refers to it or not — so give a pack a folder of its own rather than saving it into somewhere like your Desktop, which would put every file there into the exported pack.
docs.packsSmspack.packOwnsWholeFolder = 模组包拥有它的整个文件夹。导出会打包其中的一切，包括子文件夹，无论模组包是否引用它们——所以请给每个模组包单独一个文件夹，而不要把它保存在桌面之类的地方，否则那里的每个文件都会被塞进导出的模组包里。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Version-control folders, editor clutter and any earlier .smspack sitting in the folder are left out.
docs.packsSmspack.versionControlFoldersEditor = 版本控制文件夹、编辑器杂物，以及文件夹里已有的旧 .smspack 都会被排除在外。

# en: Display name
docs.runtimeNamesDisplayNames.editorSListsShow.term = 显示名称

# Docs: a heading inside the topic
# en: Worth knowing
docs.runtimeNamesDisplayNames.heading.worthKnowing = 值得了解

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: A dialogue node's Actor field takes a character's runtime name, and the line is then labelled with that character's display name.
docs.runtimeNamesDisplayNames.dialogueNodeSActor = 对话节点的“演员”字段取的是角色的运行时名称，而那句台词随后会标上该角色的显示名称。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: The Edit menu calls the same thing the item's key, when duplicating or renaming.
docs.runtimeNamesDisplayNames.editMenuCallsSame = 在复制或重命名时，“编辑”菜单把同一个东西称作条目的“键名”。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Two items of the same kind cannot share a runtime name, and Validate flags duplicates.
docs.runtimeNamesDisplayNames.twoItemsSameKind = 同一类型的两个条目不能共用一个运行时名称，验证会标出重复。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Renaming one leaves anything pointing at it pointing at the old name — variables excepted, where the rename is applied everywhere at once.
docs.runtimeNamesDisplayNames.renamingOneLeavesAnything = 重命名其中一个之后，指向它的东西仍然指着旧名称——变量除外，它的重命名会一次性应用到所有地方。

# Docs: a topic's title
# en: Condition and action lists
docs.conditionActionLists.title = 条件与动作列表

# Docs: the line under the topic's title
# en: The shared editor used everywhere the pack asks when something happens, or what happens next.
docs.conditionActionLists.summary = 凡是模组包询问“什么时候发生”或“接下来做什么”的地方，用的都是这同一个编辑界面。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: + Add condition
docs.conditionActionLists.addRequirementEveryCondition.term = + 添加条件

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Add a requirement. Every condition in a list has to pass, and an empty list always passes.
docs.conditionActionLists.addRequirementEveryCondition = 添加一个要求。列表里的每条条件都必须满足，而空列表总是满足。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: + Add action
docs.conditionActionLists.addSomethingDoWhen.term = + 添加动作

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Add something to do when the list is satisfied. Actions run top to bottom.
docs.conditionActionLists.addSomethingDoWhen = 添加列表满足时要做的事。动作自上而下执行。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: + Add group
docs.conditionActionLists.bundleConditionsWayBrackets.term = + 添加分组

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Bundle conditions the way brackets would, for when a flat list of requirements is not enough. A group can ask for all of its conditions or any of them.
docs.conditionActionLists.bundleConditionsWayBrackets = 像括号那样把条件打包起来，用于一串平铺的要求不够用的时候。一个分组可以要求它的全部条件，也可以只要求其中任意一条。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen

docs.conditionActionLists.moveWholeListSomewhere.term = 全部复制 / 粘贴 / 覆盖

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Move a whole list somewhere else. Paste adds to what is already there; Overwrite replaces it.
docs.conditionActionLists.moveWholeListSomewhere = 把一整份列表挪到别处。粘贴是追加到已有内容之后；覆盖则是整个替换。

# Docs: a heading inside the topic
# en: Worth knowing
docs.conditionActionLists.heading.worthKnowing = 值得了解

docs.maskPainter.characterNPCMaskHas = 角色或 NPC 的遮罩有三层，分别绘制：弹跳、波动和噪声。地点遮罩只有一层，叫作强度，另外两个面板会隐藏，因为那个效果不读取别的内容。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: In the file itself a character mask keeps Bounce, Wave and Noise in the red, green and blue channels with alpha scaling all three, and a place mask keeps its one layer in alpha alone. That only matters if you ever paint a mask in another program — here it is handled for you.
docs.maskPainter.fileItselfCharacterMask = 在文件本身里，角色遮罩把弹跳、波动和噪声分别放在红、绿、蓝通道，透明通道则统一缩放这三者；地点遮罩只把它唯一的那层放在透明通道里。只有当你哪天要在别的软件里画遮罩时，这才要紧——在这里一切都替你处理好了。

# Docs: a topic's title
# en: Previews
docs.previews.title = 预览

# Docs: the line under the topic's title
# en: What the preview panes show, and what only ever happens in game.
docs.previews.summary = 预览面板显示什么，以及哪些只会在游戏里发生。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Previews compose your art the way the game lays it out — at the game's own resolution, with the interface frame and navigator strip in place — so positions and sizes are true to what players will see.
docs.previews.previewsComposeArtWay = 预览会按照游戏的排布方式来组合你的美术资源——按游戏自身的分辨率，带上界面边框和导航条——所以位置和尺寸与玩家将看到的一致。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: They do not run the game's logic. Conditions, variables and integration rules are not evaluated, so anything gated on state is drawn regardless.
docs.previews.theyDoNotRun = 它们不运行游戏的逻辑。条件、变量和集成规则都不会被求值，所以任何受状态控制的东西照样会被画出来。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Previews resolve image paths against the pack folder, so they stay empty until the pack has been saved.
docs.previews.previewsResolveImagePaths = 预览是相对模组包文件夹来解析图片路径的，所以在模组包保存之前它们都是空的。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Preview quality on the Options menu trades smoothness against processor load.
docs.previews.previewQualityOptionsMenu = “选项”菜单里的“预览质量”是在流畅度和处理器负担之间取舍。

Right-click a squiggle for suggestions, to ignore the word for this session, or to add it to your Windows dictionary. Switching it off also makes very long node lists lighter to scroll.

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Male, Female or Neutral: how the character is spoken of. Nothing in English changes, but a translation does - "I'm ready" from a woman is "Estoy lista" in Spanish, and a translator that is not told guesses male. The Translate window tells it who says each line and who each name is. Every character of the pack's own needs one; until it has, the validation list says so. The player's and the game's characters' are the game's, and shown greyed.
docs.characters.pronouns = 男性、女性或中性：如何称呼这个角色。英语中没有变化，但翻译中会有——女性说的“I'm ready”在西班牙语中是“Estoy lista”，没被告知的翻译器会猜测为男性。“翻译”窗口会告诉它每句台词是谁说的、每个名字是谁。模组包自己的每个角色都需要设置；未设置时，验证列表会提示。玩家和游戏角色的代词来自游戏，并以灰色显示。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: The pack file calls these two layers baseSprite and secondarySprite — the game's own naming, and the opposite way round from what you would expect, since baseSprite is the front one. The editor says back and front instead, and each field's tooltip names the key it writes.
docs.places.packFileCallsThese = 模组包文件把这两层叫作 baseSprite 和 secondarySprite——这是游戏自己的命名，而且与你的直觉相反，因为 baseSprite 才是前景那一层。编辑器改称后景和前景，并且每个字段的提示都会说明它写入的是哪个键。

docs.dialogues.doNotPlayArrival = 进门时不播放：它停在「交谈」按钮后面，由玩家来开始。只要勾选了上面的可重复选项，这一项就不可用，因为那一项按定义就是进门时播放。

docs.dialogues.ifSomethingElsePlaying = 如果它符合条件时正好有别的内容在播放，它会等待并紧接着开始。不勾选时，这段对话会错过那个时机，等下一次。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Set for condition debugging (F12)

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: In game, F12 logs which start conditions passed and failed, with the values behind them. Diagnostic only — it changes nothing about play.
docs.dialogues.gameF12LogsWhich = 在游戏中，F12 会记录哪些开始条件通过了、哪些没有，以及它们背后的取值。仅用于诊断：不会改变游戏里的任何东西。

# Docs: a heading inside the topic
# en: The game's conversations
docs.dialogues.heading.gameSConversations = 游戏自己的对话

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: The game plays this when
docs.dialogues.gameChecksBeforePlays.term = 游戏在以下情况播放它

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: What the game checks before it plays the conversation, one group per place that plays it - any one of them is enough. Take out removes one of the game's conditions from that place's script; it stays listed, struck through, with Put back beside it. The script is the room's, not the conversation's, so anything else it guards - another conversation, a quest it starts - happens without the condition too, and the Quests tab shows the same change. Where the place is (the room it belongs to) cannot be taken out here - a conversation is always played somewhere; a quest's place on the Quests tab can. A room tries its conversations in order and plays the first one that can, so the ones it says it tries first still come before this one, whatever you take out. Reset all puts these back as well.
docs.dialogues.gameChecksBeforePlays = 游戏在播放这段对话前所检查的内容，每个播放它的位置各成一组：任意一组成立即可。「移除」会把游戏的某个条件从那个位置的脚本里拿掉；它仍然列在那里，带删除线，旁边有「放回」。脚本属于房间而不属于对话，所以它守护的其他一切（另一段对话、某个任务的开始）在没有这个条件时也照样发生，任务页签里会显示同一处改动。位置在哪里（它所属的房间）不能在这里移除：一段对话总要在某个地方播放；任务页签里某个任务的地点则可以。房间会按顺序检查自己的对话并播放第一段可播放的，所以它说会先检查的那些，无论你移除什么，仍然排在这一段前面。「全部还原」也会把这些放回去。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Saves under way
docs.dialogues.lineOneGameS.term = 进行中的存档

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: A line of one of the game's conversations that you change, add or take out can set or skip something the game's story needs, so it can break a save already under way - and removing your pack later can too. The Dialogues tab says so in orange. The first time a player loads an existing save with a pack that does this, the game warns them and recommends a new game; new games are not warned. A conversation you picked but left as the game has it changes nothing and is not warned about.
docs.dialogues.lineOneGameS = 你在游戏自有对话里改动、添加或移除的一行，可能会触发或跳过游戏剧情所需要的东西，因而可能弄坏已经开始的存档，之后移除你的模组包也一样。对话页签会用橙色提示这一点。当玩家第一次用这样的模组包载入已有存档时，游戏会提醒他并建议开新档；新开的存档不会有提醒。你选进来但保持游戏原样的对话不会改变任何东西，也不会触发提醒。

docs.dialogues.heading.nodes = 节点

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: A dialogue is a tree. Play starts at the first root and works down: a node runs, then its children in order, until something exits.
docs.dialogues.dialogueTreePlayStarts = 一段对话是一棵树。播放从第一个根节点开始向下推进：先运行一个节点，再按顺序运行它的子节点，直到某处退出。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Add a child of the selected node. The speaker, expression, outfit, timing, jump and the whole conditions and actions lists all carry over, so writing the next line of a scene is not a matter of re-picking all of it.
docs.dialogues.addChildSelectedNode = 为所选节点添加一个子节点。说话者、表情、服装、时长、跳转以及整份条件和动作列表都会继承，这样写一场戏的下一行就不必把所有设置重新选一遍。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: + Sibling
docs.dialogues.sameButBesideSelected.term = + 同级节点

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: The same, but beside the selected node rather than under it. It lands directly below what you had selected, not at the end of the row.
docs.dialogues.sameButBesideSelected = 同样的效果，但放在所选节点旁边而不是下面。它会落在你所选节点的正下方，而不是这一排的末尾。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: The text and the tag never carry over. The text is the one thing that is always different, and two nodes sharing a tag would make every jump aimed at the first one ambiguous.
docs.dialogues.textTagNeverCarry = 文本和标签从不继承。文本是唯一总是不同的东西，而两个节点共用一个标签会让指向前一个的跳转变得含糊。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Who is speaking and how they look, from this node onward. Outfit also accepts $variableName, so a node can pick the outfit from a variable. Expression and Outfit disappear when the player is the speaker: the player's bust belongs to the game, so a pack has neither to offer.
docs.dialogues.whoSpeakingTheyLook = 从这个节点起，是谁在说话、他们是什么样子。服装也接受 $variableName，所以一个节点可以从变量里取它。当说话的是玩家时，表情和服装都会消失：玩家的半身像属于游戏，模组包没有可供提供的表情或服装。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: An optional label so another node can jump here. Give at most one node any given tag.
docs.dialogues.optionalLabelSoAnother = 可选的标签，以便另一个节点跳到这里。同一个标签最多只给一个节点。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Jump
docs.dialogues.wherePlayGoesAfter.term = 跳转

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Where play goes after this node. Continue, the default, moves to the next node down the list — whether that is a child of this one or something further out. Exit stops the conversation and skips everything below. Jump resumes at whichever node carries the tag you name.
docs.dialogues.wherePlayGoesAfter = 这个节点之后播放去往哪里。继续是默认值，前往列表中的下一个节点，无论它是本节点的子节点还是更外层的内容。退出会停止对话并跳过下面的一切。跳转则在你指定的标签所在的节点继续。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Jump to
docs.dialogues.whichTaggedNodeResume.term = 跳转到

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Which tagged node to resume at. Only appears once Jump is the mode, since Continue and Exit have nowhere to go, and it lists the tags in this dialogue — still typeable, so you can aim at a tag before writing the node that carries it.
docs.dialogues.whichTaggedNodeResume = 在哪个带标签的节点继续。只有模式为跳转时才出现，因为继续和退出无处可去；它会列出本段对话里的标签，也可以直接输入，所以你可以先指向一个标签，之后再写带这个标签的节点。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: At the very bottom of a dialogue, Continue and Exit do the same thing, because there is nothing left to skip either way. Exit only means something when something comes after it.
docs.dialogues.veryBottomDialogueContinue = 在一段对话的最末尾，继续和退出的效果相同，因为反正已经没有什么可跳过了。只有后面还有内容时，退出才有意义。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Duration
docs.dialogues.untilinteractionWaitsPlayerTimeout.term = 时长

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: UntilInteraction waits for the player; Timeout moves on by itself once the line has finished typing.
docs.dialogues.untilinteractionWaitsPlayerTimeout = UntilInteraction 等待玩家；Timeout 在这一行打完字后自行推进。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Timeout (s)
docs.dialogues.longFinishedLineLingers.term = 超时（秒）

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: How long the finished line lingers before it advances. Only appears when Duration is Timeout, since it means nothing otherwise.
docs.dialogues.longFinishedLineLingers = 已经打完的台词在推进前停留多久。只有时长为 Timeout 时才出现，因为其他情况下它没有意义。

# Docs: a heading inside the topic

docs.dialogues.heading.perNodeConditionsActions = 每个节点的条件与动作

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen

# Docs: a point in the topic

docs.dialogues.skipNodeWhenThey = 条件不成立时跳过该节点。在「选择」之下，不满足条件的子节点根本不会摆给玩家。

docs.dialogues.runLineEndsUsual = 在台词结束时运行：凡是不该在玩家读完之前生效的东西，通常都放在这里。

# Docs: a heading inside the topic
# en: Auto-trigger patterns

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: A comma-separated list of text patterns. When one of them appears in a line, the effect fires on its own — no action needed.
docs.sfx.commaSeparatedListText = 以逗号分隔的文本模式列表。其中任意一个出现在台词里时，音效就会自行播放，不需要动作。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: The convention is a lowercase word between asterisks, like *plap*. One effect can carry several patterns, so different words can share a sound.
docs.sfx.conventionLowercaseWordBetween = 惯例是用星号括起的小写单词，例如 *plap*。一个音效可以有多个模式，所以不同的词可以共用同一个声音。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Leave it blank for an effect that should only ever play when an action asks for it.
docs.sfx.leaveBlankEffectShould = 如果某个音效只应在动作调用时播放，就把它留空。

# Docs: a heading inside the topic
# en: Variants
docs.sfx.heading.variants = 变体

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: An effect can have several recordings, and one is picked at random each time it plays, so a repeated sound does not become monotonous.
docs.sfx.effectCanHaveSeveral = 一个音效可以有多段录音，每次播放时随机挑一段，这样重复的声音就不会变得单调。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: There is no field for them: put the extra files beside the first one with the same name and _1, _2, _3 on the end. Audio/Plap.ogg picks up Audio/Plap_1.ogg and Audio/Plap_2.ogg by itself.
docs.sfx.thereNoFieldThem = 没有专门的字段：把额外的文件和第一个放在一起，用相同的名字并在末尾加上 _1、_2、_3。Audio/Plap.ogg 会自行收集 Audio/Plap_1.ogg 和 Audio/Plap_2.ogg。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Number them without gaps. Counting stops at the first missing number, so _1, _2, _4 quietly loads only the first two.
docs.sfx.numberThemWithoutGaps = 编号不要跳号。收集会在第一个缺失的编号处停止，所以 _1、_2、_4 只会悄悄载入前两个。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: The extensions do not have to match, so a WAV variant of an OGG effect is fine.
docs.sfx.extensionsDoNotHave = 扩展名不必一致，所以 OGG 音效配一个 WAV 变体是可以的。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Play above previews the first recording only — the random pick happens in game.
docs.sfx.playAbovePreviewsFirst = 上面的「播放」只试听第一段录音：随机挑选发生在游戏里。

# Docs: a point in the topic

docs.variables.savePlayerSGame = 把它随玩家的存档一起保存，这样关掉游戏再打开它依然存在。不勾选时，它每次运行都从头开始：凡是只在一次到访中才有意义的东西，这样才对。

# en: Refreshing
docs.variables.heading.refreshing = 刷新

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Refresh mode
docs.variables.neverLeavesVariableAlone.term = 刷新模式

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Never leaves the variable alone. Daily resets it to its default each in-game day. DailyRandom gives it a fresh random number each day. LevelRandom re-rolls it every time the player enters one particular place.
docs.variables.neverLeavesVariableAlone = Never 不去动这个变量。Daily 在游戏中每天把它恢复为默认值。DailyRandom 每天给它一个新的随机数。LevelRandom 在玩家每次进入某个特定地点时重新掷一次。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Which place re-rolls it, for LevelRandom.
docs.variables.whichPlaceReRolls = 由哪个地点重新掷值，用于 LevelRandom。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Min value / Max value
docs.variables.limitsNumberGameKeeps.term = 最小值 / 最大值

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Limits for a number: the game keeps every write inside them. They also set the range the random refresh modes roll within, which is 0 to 100 if you leave them blank.
docs.variables.limitsNumberGameKeeps = 数字的上下限：游戏会把每一次写入都限制在其中。它们同时也定义随机刷新模式的取值范围；留空时该范围是 0 到 100。

# Docs: a heading inside the topic
# en: Worth knowing
docs.variables.heading.worthKnowing = 值得了解

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: A daily refresh happens when the player sleeps, at the point the new day actually begins.
docs.variables.dailyRefreshHappensWhen = 每日刷新发生在玩家睡觉时，也就是新的一天真正开始的那一刻。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: See Putting values into text for the ways a value can be used once it is declared.
docs.variables.seePuttingValuesInto = 声明之后如何使用一个值，参见「把值放进文本里」。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: The quest is in progress again the next time the player loads their save: every task they did stays done, and the first new task starts. Only when it is a clean addition - every new task comes after every task the quest already had, and the player had done all of those. Otherwise it stays finished, so nobody is stuck behind a task they cannot reach. A new choice under an "any one" task, or a note under a "by action" one, never reopens it.
docs.quests.stepsAdded.reopen = 玩家下次读取存档时，任务会重新变为进行中：他们做过的目标仍然算完成，第一个新目标开始。只有在干净的追加时才会这样——每个新目标都排在任务原有的所有目标之后，并且玩家把原有目标全部做完了。否则任务保持已完成，免得有人被卡在一个到不了的目标后面。「任选其一」目标下新增的选项，或「由动作决定」目标下新增的说明，永远不会让它重新开启。

docs.quests.stepsAdded.startOver = 玩家在这个任务上的进度会被清除，任务回到「未开始」，然后按它平时的方式重新开始——它的开始条件，或一个任务动作。每个目标的动作会在完成时再执行一次。

A save from before this was remembered has no record, so for it the tasks the player never did that come after the last one they did count as new. Only your own quests: one of the game's quests stays as the player left it.
docs.quests.stepsAdded.whichNew = ModForge 会记住每个存档上次用你的模组包游玩时，这个任务有哪些目标。在开始记录之前的存档没有这份记录，所以对它来说，玩家从没做过、并且排在他们做过的最后一个目标之后的目标，都算作新的。只适用于你自己的任务：游戏自带的任务会保持玩家离开时的样子。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: On a subtask. The journal lists every subtask of a task it shows, started or not; ticked, this one stays out of the journal until it starts - under a task whose subtasks run in order, once the subtask before it is done. A top-level task needs no tick: the journal already leaves one out until it starts.
docs.quests.subtaskJournalListsEvery = 用于子步骤。日志会列出它所显示的步骤下的全部子步骤，无论是否已经开始；勾选后，这一条在开始之前不会出现在日志里，而在子步骤按顺序进行的步骤下，那就是上一条完成的时候。顶层步骤不需要这个勾：日志本来就不会列出尚未开始的顶层步骤。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Hidden until conditions pass
docs.quests.anyTaskKeepsOut.term = 条件通过前隐藏

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: On any task. Keeps it out of the journal until the conditions under it all pass. Once they have, it stays in the journal for the rest of that save - remembered with the save - unless 'Hide it again when they stop passing' is ticked, in which case it is shown only while they pass. With no conditions it is shown at once. Hiding a task changes nothing about when it or its quest finishes.
docs.quests.anyTaskKeepsOut = 用于任何步骤。在下面的条件全部通过之前，让它不出现在日志里。一旦通过，它会在这一局存档的余下时间里一直留在日志中（随存档记住），除非勾选了「条件不再通过时重新隐藏」，那样它就只在条件通过期间显示。没有条件时它立刻显示。隐藏一个步骤完全不会改变它或它所属任务何时结束。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: How a task with subtasks finishes: in order (one subtask at a time), any order (all of them, started together), any one (the first one done), or by action. By action means its subtasks never start, so they read as notes under it.
docs.quests.taskSubtasksFinishesOrder = 带子步骤的步骤如何完成：按顺序（一次一个子步骤）、任意顺序（全部同时开始）、任选其一（最先完成的那个），或由动作决定。由动作决定意味着它的子步骤永远不会开始，因此它们读起来就像它下面的备注。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: In the Vanilla quests list: an entry about one of the game's own quests. The quest stays the game's; your pack changes what the journal shows of it, what happens as the player works through it, and - if you choose - which tasks it has. Everything it changes goes back to the game's own if your pack is removed.
docs.quests.vanillaQuestsListEntry = 在游戏任务列表里：一条关于游戏自有任务的条目。任务仍然属于游戏；你的模组包改变的是日志对它的呈现、玩家推进它时会发生什么，以及——如果你愿意——它有哪些步骤。一旦移除你的模组包，它改过的一切都会回到游戏原本的样子。

# en: Quest

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Which of the game's quests it is about, by the name the game's own instructions use. The journal title is shown beside it, and the two often differ.
docs.quests.whichGameSQuests = 它针对游戏的哪个任务，采用游戏自身脚本所用的名字。日志标题显示在旁边，两者往往并不相同。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: How much the entry changes, and a button that puts the whole quest back the way the game has it - its description and tasks, your tasks in it, your start and reset conditions, and every place that starts or resets it, including conditions taken out of them. The description has a Reset of its own. A task of the game's that you change is marked with a dot in the list; its panel lists each change with its own way back, and Reset puts the task back.
docs.quests.muchEntryChangesButton = 这条条目改了多少，以及一个把整个任务还原成游戏原样的按钮：它的说明和步骤、你加在里面的步骤、你的开始与重置条件，还有每一个开始或重置它的位置，包括从这些位置里拿掉的条件。说明另有自己的「还原」。你改过的游戏步骤会在列表里标上一个点；它的面板会逐条列出改动，每条都有自己的还原方式，「还原」则把这个步骤整个恢复。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Adds a top-level task of yours just after the selected top-level task, or at the end. The game waits for it like any of its own: the tasks after it hold until it is done, and the quest does not finish without it.
docs.quests.addsTopLevelTask = 在所选顶层步骤之后、或在末尾，添加一个你自己的顶层步骤。游戏会像对待自己的步骤一样等它：它后面的步骤会等到它完成，而任务没有它就不会结束。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: + Subtask
docs.quests.addsSubtaskYoursEnd.term = + 子步骤

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Adds a subtask of yours - at the end of the selected top-level task's subtasks, or just after the selected subtask. Works on the game's steps too, including one with no subtasks yet, which then finishes through its subtasks as the game does. Added tasks go two levels deep, as the game's do.
docs.quests.addsSubtaskYoursEnd = 添加一个你自己的子步骤——放在所选顶层步骤的子步骤末尾，或紧跟在所选子步骤之后。对游戏的步骤同样有效，包括本来还没有子步骤的那些；它们随后就像游戏那样通过子步骤来结束。添加的步骤最多两层深，和游戏的一样。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Selecting one opens the same panel a task of your own quest has: text, runtime name, Quest description once done, Hidden until it starts, Hidden until conditions pass, how it completes, its counter, and its completion conditions and actions. Quest actions and conditions name it on the Vanilla side, by the game's quest and its runtime name.
docs.quests.selectingOneOpensSame = 选中一个，会打开与你自有任务的步骤相同的面板：文本、运行时名称、完成时任务说明变为、开始前隐藏、条件通过前隐藏、完成方式、计数器，以及它的完成条件和动作。任务动作与条件在游戏任务这一侧引用它，用的是游戏的任务加上它的运行时名称。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Take it out of the quest
docs.quests.oneGameSTasks.term = 把它移出任务

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: On one of the game's tasks. It is not deleted - the game's own scenes still name it - but hidden, with everything under it, and the quest moves past it the moment it starts, as though the game had completed it. Under a task that finishes with any one of its subtasks it is left alone instead, so it cannot finish that task by itself. Untick to put it back.
docs.quests.oneGameSTasks = 用于游戏的步骤。它不会被删除——游戏自己的剧情仍然会引用它——而是连同它下面的一切一起隐藏，并且在它一开始的那一刻任务就越过它，就像游戏已经完成了它一样。在一个「任选其一」完成的步骤之下，它会被原样保留，以免它自己一个就结束那个步骤。取消勾选即可放回。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: In the journal
docs.quests.oneGameSTasks2.term = 在日志中

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: On one of the game's tasks: shown as the game has it, hidden until conditions of yours pass (with the same 'Hide it again when they stop passing' choice a task of your own has), always hidden, or (for a subtask) hidden until it starts. Hiding a task changes nothing about when the quest finishes.
docs.quests.oneGameSTasks2 = 用于游戏的步骤：按游戏原样显示、在你的条件通过前隐藏（带有和你自有步骤相同的「条件不再通过时重新隐藏」选项）、始终隐藏，或者（对子步骤而言）开始前隐藏。隐藏一个步骤完全不会改变任务何时结束。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: What the game does with it
docs.quests.readOnlyQuestEach.term = 游戏如何处理它

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Read-only, on the quest and on each of its tasks: every place the game starts the quest or puts it back to not started, and every place it completes, counts, fails or abandons a task. Each says which conversation and line it is on (as the line appears, or once it is done) and the lines leading there, or which script on which object runs it and which conversations that script plays just before and after it, and what has to be true - the conditions on the way and what the conversation needs to play. A condition ModForge has no equivalent for is listed in the game's own words. A step on a task only takes effect while that task is in progress. Every quest step in the game is covered.
docs.quests.readOnlyQuestEach = 只读，位于任务本身及其每个步骤上：游戏开始这个任务或把它退回「未开始」的每一处，以及游戏完成、计数、判定失败或放弃某个步骤的每一处。每一处都会说明它位于哪段对话的哪一行（按台词原文显示，或在完成之后显示），以及通往那里的台词；或者说明由哪个对象上的哪个脚本来执行，那个脚本在它前后紧接着播放哪些对话；还有需要成立的条件——沿途的条件，以及该对话得以播放所需要的条件。ModForge 没有对应写法的条件，会按游戏自己的措辞列出。作用于步骤的操作只在该步骤进行中时才生效。游戏里的每一处任务节点都已覆盖。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Changing a place
docs.quests.placesUnderStartedPut.term = 改动某一处

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: On the places under Started by and Put back to not started by. Each of the game's conditions around the step is listed where it lives - a line on the way, the line itself, the place that plays the conversation, or the script. Take out removes one from the game (struck through, with Put back); a line's condition is taken out of your version of that conversation, a place's out of its script, and either way whatever else it guards happens without it too - the Dialogues tab shows the same change. Where the script sits is a row of its own and can be taken out as well. And only if (yours) adds conditions of your own. Once you change anything at a place, that place is yours: what is left - the game's conditions you kept, the room, and yours - is asked over and over, and the quest starts (or goes back to not started) the moment it all passes, whether or not the game's own conversation or script ever gets there. Take everything out and add a key press, and the key press starts it; take the room out too and it starts anywhere. A place you have not touched behaves as the game wrote it, and putting everything back gives it back to the game. A place also says which of the room's conversations the game tries first, which decides the conversation but not your rule, and the game's log says which one the room played. A changed place is marked with a dot, and its reset arrow puts it back the way the game has it. Places that complete, count or fail a task are shown, not changed.
docs.quests.placesUnderStartedPut = 位于「由以下位置开始」和「由以下位置退回未开始」之下的各处。游戏在该节点周围的每个条件都列在它所在的地方——沿途的某一行、这一行本身、播放该对话的位置，或者脚本。「移除」会把某个条件从游戏里拿掉（带删除线，旁边有「放回」）；某一行的条件会从你那份对话里拿掉，某个位置的条件会从它的脚本里拿掉，无论哪种，它守护的其他一切在没有这个条件时也照样发生——对话页签会显示同一处改动。脚本所在的位置单独占一行，同样可以移除。「并且仅当（你的）」则添加你自己的条件。只要你在某一处改动了任何东西，那一处就归你了：剩下的内容——你保留的游戏条件、房间，以及你自己的条件——会被反复询问，并在全部通过的那一刻开始任务（或把它退回「未开始」），无论游戏自己的对话或脚本是否走到过那里。把条件全部拿掉再加上一个按键，按键就能开始它；连房间也拿掉，它在哪里都能开始。你没有动过的位置仍然按游戏写的那样运作，把一切放回去就等于交还给游戏。每一处还会说明游戏会先尝试房间里的哪段对话，这决定的是对话而不是你的规则，游戏日志会说明房间最终播放了哪一段。改动过的位置会标上一个点，它的还原箭头会把它恢复成游戏原来的样子。完成、计数或判定步骤失败的位置只做展示，不可改动。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Open conversation
docs.quests.besidePlaceConversationShows.term = 打开对话

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Beside a place in a conversation: shows that conversation on the Dialogues tab with the line selected. If your pack does not change it yet, you are asked before it is added to your vanilla conversations; it changes nothing until you edit it.
docs.quests.besidePlaceConversationShows = 位于对话中的某一处旁边：在对话页签里显示那段对话并选中该行。如果你的模组包还没有改动它，在把它加入你的游戏对话之前会先征询你；在你编辑之前它不会改变任何东西。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: When a task starts
docs.quests.everyTaskListGame.term = 步骤何时开始

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Every task in the list, the game's and yours, says when it starts. Nothing starts a task directly; the quest's order does. The first top-level task starts with the quest and each one after it once the one before is done; a task whose subtasks run in order starts the first of them, one whose subtasks run in any order - or finish with any one - starts them all, and one completed by an action starts none. A task your pack takes out is skipped on the way.
docs.quests.everyTaskListGame = 列表里的每个步骤，无论是游戏的还是你的，都会说明它何时开始。没有任何东西直接开始一个步骤；决定它的是任务的顺序。第一个顶层步骤随任务一起开始，之后每一个都在前一个完成后开始；子步骤按顺序进行的步骤会开始其中的第一个，子步骤可任意顺序进行——或任选其一完成——的步骤会把它们全部开始，而由动作完成的步骤一个都不开始。被你的模组包移出的步骤会在这条链上被跳过。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Saves under way
docs.quests.addingTakingOutTasks.term = 进行中的存档

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Adding or taking out tasks can leave a save that is part-way through the quest unable to finish it, and so can removing your pack later. The editor says so in orange on any entry that does it. The first time a player loads an existing save with such a pack, the game warns them, lists the quests whose tasks change with where their save is in each - not started, in progress, possibly stuck because a task was added before where they are, or already finished - and recommends a new game; new games are not warned. Changes to the game's conversations are warned about the same way - see Dialogues; changes to its places are not. A save that has progress on tasks a quest no longer has is put back on its feet once: the plugin completes steps whose subtasks are all done, starts the next step where nothing is in progress, and completes the quest when all its steps are.
docs.quests.addingTakingOutTasks = 添加或移除步骤，可能让一个正做到任务中途的存档再也无法完成它，之后移除你的模组包也一样。编辑器会在任何会造成这种情况的条目上用橙色提示。当玩家第一次用这样的模组包载入已有存档时，游戏会提醒他，列出步骤发生变化的任务以及他的存档在每个任务中的进度——未开始、进行中、可能卡住（因为在他所处位置之前插入了新步骤），或者已经完成——并建议开新档；新开的存档不会有提醒。对游戏对话的改动会以同样的方式提醒，参见「对话」；对游戏各处位置的改动则不会。如果存档里的进度停留在任务已经不再包含的步骤上，插件会把它扶正一次：子步骤全部完成的节点会被补完，没有任何内容进行中的地方会开始下一个节点，所有节点都完成时任务也随之完成。

# Docs: a point in the topic

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: The game only finishes a task that is in progress. Completing a task the player has not reached yet does nothing, and says so in the game's log.
docs.quests.gameOnlyFinishesTask = 游戏只会结束正在进行中的步骤。完成玩家还没走到的步骤不会有任何效果，游戏日志里会这么说明。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Finishing a quest gives the player the same reward the game's own quests give.
docs.quests.finishingQuestGivesPlayer = 完成一个任务会给玩家与游戏自有任务相同的奖励。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Players' progress is saved under the runtime names of the quest and its tasks. Renaming either after release starts that quest over for everyone who has the pack.
docs.quests.playersProgressSavedUnder = 玩家的进度按任务及其步骤的运行时名称保存。发布之后再改其中任何一个的名字，都会让所有装有这个模组包的玩家从头重做那个任务。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Variable
docs.conditions.compareVariableAgainstValue.term = Variable

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Compare a variable against a value. Source chooses between your pack's own variables and the game's; Comparison offers equals, greater than, greater or equal, less than, less or equal, and exists — which passes once the variable has been given any value at all.
docs.conditions.compareVariableAgainstValue = 把变量与一个值作比较。来源在你模组包自己的变量和游戏的变量之间选择；比较方式提供等于、大于、大于或等于、小于、小于或等于，以及「存在」——只要该变量被赋过任何值就通过。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: VariableStartsWith
docs.conditions.passesWhenTextVariable.term = VariableStartsWith

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Passes when a text variable begins with something. The one the comparisons above cannot express: where a value is compound, like a location built from a room and a spot in it, a prefix answers which room am I in without listing every spot.
docs.conditions.passesWhenTextVariable = 当文本变量以某段内容开头时通过。它能表达上面那些比较做不到的事：当一个值是复合的，比如由房间和房间里的某个点组成的位置，用前缀就能回答「我在哪个房间」，而不必把每个点都列一遍。

# Docs: a heading inside the topic
# en: Lists
docs.conditions.heading.lists = 列表

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: ListContains
docs.conditions.passesWhenListHolds.term = ListContains

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Passes when a list holds a particular value. Negate it for does not contain, which is how you express not already taken.
docs.conditions.passesWhenListHolds = 当列表中含有某个特定值时通过。取反即为「不包含」，也就是表达「还没被拿走」的写法。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: ListCount
docs.conditions.compareManyEntriesList.term = ListCount

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Compare how many entries a list holds against a number. Equals zero is the empty test, and negating that gives has something in it.
docs.conditions.compareManyEntriesList = 把列表的条目数量与一个数字作比较。等于零就是「列表为空」的判断，对它取反则是「里面有东西」。

docs.conditions.heading.whereThings = 东西在哪里

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Keyboard layouts
docs.conditions.gameReadsKeysPOSITION.term = 键盘布局

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: The game reads keys by POSITION, not by the letter printed on the cap — the key called A is wherever A sits on a US keyboard, which is Q on AZERTY. The picker groups the letters, number row and punctuation separately for that reason; mouse buttons, arrows, modifiers, function keys and the numpad sit in the same place everywhere and are the safe choice for anything a player has to be able to press.
docs.conditions.gameReadsKeysPOSITION = 游戏按位置读取按键，而不是按键帽上印的字母——名为 A 的键就是美式键盘上 A 所在的那个位置，在 AZERTY 上那是 Q。选择器正因如此把字母、数字行和标点分开归组；鼠标按钮、方向键、修饰键、功能键和小键盘在各地都在同一个位置，是玩家必须按得到的操作的稳妥选择。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Mouse buttons are keys too
docs.conditions.gameMouseButtonJust.term = 鼠标按钮也是按键

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: To the game a mouse button is just another key, so there is one Key field and the Device picker only decides which list it offers.
docs.conditions.gameMouseButtonJust = 对游戏来说，鼠标按钮不过是另一个按键，所以只有一个「按键」字段，设备选择器只决定它提供哪份列表。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Random is offered only where a condition is checked once and then done with — a node's own conditions, and a place's enter and exit hooks. Anywhere that is checked continuously, a fresh roll every frame would flicker rather than decide, so DailyChance is the one to reach for.
docs.conditions.randomOfferedOnlyWhere = Random 只在条件检查一次就作罢的地方提供——节点自身的条件，以及地点的进入和离开挂钩。凡是会被持续检查的地方，每一帧重掷一次只会闪烁而不是作出判断，这时该用的是 DailyChance。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Timer is offered only on integration rules, because it measures from the last time the rule fired and nothing else has a moment like that.
docs.conditions.timerOfferedOnlyIntegration = Timer 只在集成规则上提供，因为它是从规则上次触发的时刻起计时的，而别的地方没有这样一个时刻。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: InputKey is offered everywhere, but its two edge phases — Pressed and Released — are only meaningful where a condition is re-checked continuously. On a node's own conditions, which are checked once when the conversation reaches the node, Validate warns and points you at Down or Up.
docs.conditions.inputkeyOfferedEverywhereBut = InputKey 在任何地方都提供，但它的两个边沿阶段——Pressed 和 Released——只有在条件会被持续重新检查的地方才有意义。在节点自身的条件上（它们只在对话抵达该节点时检查一次），「校验」会给出警告并指引你改用 Down 或 Up。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: The one Variable entry is six types underneath, chosen by the Comparison box: VariableEquals, VariableGreaterThan, VariableGreaterOrEqual, VariableLessThan, VariableLessOrEqual and VariableExists. Source picks between your pack's variables and the game's; it is a parameter, not a different type.
docs.conditions.oneVariableEntrySix = 唯一的 Variable 条目在底层其实是六种类型，由「比较方式」字段决定：VariableEquals、VariableGreaterThan、VariableGreaterOrEqual、VariableLessThan、VariableLessOrEqual 和 VariableExists。来源在你模组包的变量和游戏的变量之间选择；它是一个参数，不是另一种类型。

# Docs: a point in the topic

docs.conditions.everythingElseWrittenUnder = 其余的一切，都按选择器显示的名字来书写。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: GameVariableEquals, GameVariableNumberGreaterThan, GameVariableNumberGreaterOrEqual, GameVariableNumberLessThan and GameVariableNumberLessOrEqual were how a pack used to reach the game's own variables. Opening such a pack rewrites each of them to the matching Variable type with Source set to Vanilla, so they will not survive the next save and there is no reason to write a new one.
docs.conditions.gamevariableequalsGamevariablenumbergreaterthanGamevariablenumbergreaterorequalGamevariablenumberlessthan = GameVariableEquals、GameVariableNumberGreaterThan、GameVariableNumberGreaterOrEqual、GameVariableNumberLessThan 和 GameVariableNumberLessOrEqual 曾是模组包访问游戏自有变量的方式。打开这样的模组包时，它们会被逐一改写为对应的 Variable 类型并把来源设为 Vanilla，所以它们撑不过下一次保存，也没有理由再写新的。

# en: Everything else is written under the name the picker shows it under.
docs.actions.everythingElseWrittenUnder = 其余的一切，都按选择器显示的名字来书写。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Older packs
docs.actions.activatesceneSeparateActionShowing.term = 旧版模组包

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: ActivateScene was a separate action for showing a scene. Opening such a pack rewrites it to SetGameObjectActive with the category set to Scene and the switch set to on, which is the same thing with the ability to turn the scene off again.
docs.actions.activatesceneSeparateActionShowing = ActivateScene 曾是显示场景的一个独立动作。打开这样的模组包时，它会被改写成 SetGameObjectActive，类别设为 Scene、开关设为开启；效果相同，而且还能再把场景关掉。

# Docs: a point in the topic

# Docs: a point in the topic

docs.targetPicker.whichPlaceLookInside = 当类别是 GameObjects 时，要在哪个地点里面查找。在地点之间移动时，指明它很重要：它能确保找到的是你所指地点里的那个对象，而不是正在离开的地点里同名的另一个对象。

docs.targetPicker.whichPlaceSTwo = 当类别是 Places 时，要作用于地点的两张美术图中的哪一张——后景或前景，与「地点」页签用的是同样的说法。

# Docs: a heading inside the topic
# en: Worth knowing
docs.targetPicker.heading.worthKnowing = 值得了解

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Not every action offers every category. Switching something on or off leaves out Places, because activating a whole place is what moving between places is for.
docs.targetPicker.notEveryActionOffers = 并不是每个动作都提供全部类别。开关某样东西时不提供 Places，因为激活整个地点正是「在地点之间移动」要做的事。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: The GameObjectActive condition uses the same row, and offers the same four categories switching something on and off does. Asking whether a whole place is on screen is what LevelActive is for.
docs.targetPicker.gameobjectactiveConditionUsesSame = GameObjectActive 条件使用同一行控件，提供的四个类别也和开关某样东西时一样。要询问某个地点是否整个在屏幕上，那是 LevelActive 的用途。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Objects that start switched off are still found, so an action can turn on something that was never visible.
docs.targetPicker.objectsStartSwitchedOff = 一开始就处于关闭状态的对象同样能被找到，所以动作可以开启一个从未显示过的东西。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: A target that resolves to nothing is reported in the game's log rather than stopping anything, so a mistyped name shows up as an action that quietly does nothing.
docs.targetPicker.targetResolvesNothingReported = 指向不到任何东西的目标只会记进游戏日志，而不会中断什么，所以名字打错时表现为一个默默什么都不做的动作。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: In a condition there is no log line for it: an object that cannot be found reads the same as one switched off. Negate therefore means off or absent, not off — so a mistyped target in a negated condition passes rather than complaining.
docs.targetPicker.conditionThereNoLog = 在条件里连这条日志也没有：找不到的对象读起来和被关闭的对象一模一样。因此取反的含义是「关闭或不存在」，而不是「关闭」——于是取反条件里打错的目标会通过，而不会提出抗议。

[Docs: Validating]

# Docs: a topic's title
# en: Validating
docs.validating.title = 验证

# Docs: the line under the topic's title
# en: Checking the pack before it goes anywhere.
docs.validating.summary = 在模组包发往任何地方之前先检查一遍。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Validate (F5) checks the whole pack and fills the Issues list on the ModForge tab. Double-click a row to jump to whatever it is complaining about.
docs.validating.validateF5ChecksWhole = 「验证」（F5）会检查整个模组包，并填充 ModForge 页签上的问题列表。双击某一行即可跳到它所抱怨的地方。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Errors are things that will not work: a missing file, a required name left blank, a reference to something that no longer exists, two items sharing a key.
docs.validating.errorsThingsWillNot = 错误是那些根本不会生效的东西：缺失的文件、留空的必填名称、指向已不存在之物的引用、两个条目共用同一个键。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Warnings are things that will run but are probably not what you meant — a default value that does not parse as its type, a character set to borrow a bust without one chosen, two placements landing on the same spot.
docs.validating.warningsThingsWillRun = 警告是那些能运行、但多半不是你本意的东西——默认值按它的类型解析不出来、角色设成了借用半身像却没选任何一个、两处摆放落在了同一个点上。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: References to places the game ships with are checked against the version the editor knows, so a name that changed between game versions is caught here rather than in play.
docs.validating.referencesPlacesGameShips = 对游戏自带地点的引用，会按编辑器所知的版本来核对，这样在不同游戏版本之间改过名的地点就能在这里被发现，而不是留到游玩时才暴露。

# Docs: a heading inside the topic
# en: Worth knowing
docs.validating.heading.worthKnowing = 值得了解

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Validate runs against what is on screen. Save before exporting, since the exporter bundles what is on disk.
docs.validating.validateRunsAgainstScreen = 「验证」针对的是屏幕上的内容。导出前请先保存，因为导出器打包的是磁盘上的内容。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Clear any debug tracking before a release — see The ModForge tab.
docs.validating.clearAnyDebugTracking = 发布前请清掉所有调试标记——参见「ModForge 页签」。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Lists every language the editor has a translation for. Pick one and the editor switches to it at once, with your pack open just as it was - nothing is closed and nothing is lost. "Windows' language" follows Windows, and is English where there is no translation.
docs.translations.languageMenu = 列出编辑器有翻译的所有语言。选一个，编辑器会立即切换到该语言，你的模组包保持原样打开——什么都不会关闭，也不会丢失。「Windows 的语言」跟随 Windows，没有对应翻译时则为英文。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Edit texts on screen
docs.translations.editMode.term = 在屏幕上编辑文字

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: In the Language menu, in any language but English: a pencil appears beside every text on screen. Click one to see the English and write your own words for it - a text that says a number shows each form the language uses. Saved in your languages folder and shown at once. Texts in menus, messages and tooltips have no pencil: Find a text to edit searches all of them by their words.
docs.translations.editMode = 在“语言”菜单中，除英语外的任何语言都可用：屏幕上每段文字旁都会出现一支铅笔。点击它即可看到英语原文并写下你自己的文字——表示数量的文字会列出该语言使用的每种形式。保存在你的语言文件夹里并立即显示。菜单、消息和提示中的文字没有铅笔：“查找要编辑的文字”可以按词语搜索所有这些文字。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Translation files
docs.translations.files.term = 翻译文件

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: One text file per language, named for it: es.txt, pt-BR.txt, zh-Hans.txt. Each line is key = text, with the English it was translated from in a note above it. Any text editor opens one, and a Replace All in Notepad++ is safe: Check a translation finds the keys it changed and puts them back.
docs.translations.files = 每种语言一个文本文件，按语言命名：es.txt、pt-BR.txt、zh-Hans.txt。每一行都是「键 = 文本」，上方的注释里写着它所依据的英文原文。任何文本编辑器都能打开，在 Notepad++ 里做「全部替换」也很安全：「检查翻译」会找出被它改坏的键并把它们放回去。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: New or update a translation…
docs.translations.newOrUpdate.term = 新建或更新翻译…

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Writes a file of your own to start from, in your languages folder, or brings yours up to date after an update of the editor: new texts arrive in English, changed ones are marked 'changed from', and nothing you wrote is lost.
docs.translations.newOrUpdate = 在你的语言文件夹里写出一份属于你的文件作为起点，或在编辑器更新之后把你的文件补齐：新增的文本以英文呈现，改动过的会标上「changed from」，你写过的内容一个都不会丢。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Check a translation…
docs.translations.check.term = 检查翻译…

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Lists what is missing, broken or out of date, line by line: a {gap}, a <tag> or a count form lost in translation, a line the editor cannot read.
docs.translations.check = 逐行列出缺失、损坏或过时之处：翻译中丢失的 {gap}、<tag> 或复数形式，以及编辑器读不懂的行。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Passing one on
docs.translations.sharing.term = 把它交给别人

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Send the file. Whoever gets it puts it in their languages folder (Language ▸ Open my languages folder), and it appears in their Language menu. A file there is laid over the one that came with the editor: its words show wherever it has some for the English as it is now, and the editor's own translation everywhere else - so an update of the editor still brings its new texts, and never touches the file.
docs.translations.sharing = 把文件发出去。收到的人把它放进自己的语言文件夹（语言 ▸ 打开我的语言文件夹），它就会出现在“语言”菜单中。那里的文件会叠加在编辑器自带的文件之上：凡是它有针对当前英语原文的文字之处都显示它的文字，其余地方显示编辑器自己的翻译——因此更新编辑器仍会带来新的文字，而且永远不会动这个文件。

docs.translations.heading.packs = 让你的模组包说别的语言

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen

# Docs: a point in the topic

docs.translations.writtenIn = 在 ModForge 标签页：你的模组包自身文字所用的语言。翻译由它而来，台词按它检查拼写，游戏使用该语言的玩家会原样看到你的文字。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen

docs.translations.editingIn.term = 编辑语言

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Beside the tabs: pick a language to see the whole pack in it - lines, names, quests, buttons - and edit it right where you edit your own words. What you type goes into that language's file and your own words stay as they are; saving writes both, and lists the translation's changes with the rest.
docs.translations.editingIn = 在标签页旁边：选择一种语言，就能以该语言查看整个模组包——台词、名字、任务、按钮——并在你编辑自己文字的同一位置编辑它。你输入的内容进入该语言的文件，你自己的文字保持不变；保存时两者都会写入，翻译的更改也会与其他更改一起列出。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Translate the pack…
docs.translations.translatePack.term = 翻译模组包…

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: File ▸ Pack translations ▸ Translate the pack…: translates by machine (Google) whatever is still untranslated, into the languages you tick. Characters' names are kept as they are and spelled in each language's own alphabet - Google suggests, and you check, before anything is translated - and the pronouns you gave your characters tell it who says each line. Everything a machine writes is marked, and Editing in corrects any of it.
docs.translations.translatePack = 文件 ▸ 模组包翻译 ▸ 翻译模组包…：把尚未翻译的内容机器翻译（Google）成你勾选的语言。角色的名字保持原样，并用每种语言自己的文字书写——在翻译任何内容之前由 Google 建议、你来核对——你为角色设定的代词会告诉它每句台词是谁说的。机器写下的一切都会被标记，可以用“编辑语言”修改其中任何部分。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: Pack translations
docs.translations.packFile.term = 模组包翻译

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: File ▸ Pack translations ▸ New or update a translation… writes translations/<code>.txt in the pack's folder, with every text a player reads: lines and choices, character names, quest titles, descriptions and tasks, navigator and world map buttons, and the texts on your screens. Each has a note saying where it appears. The folder goes into the exported pack.
docs.translations.packFile = 「文件 ▸ 模组包翻译 ▸ 新建或更新翻译…」会在模组包文件夹里写出 translations/<code>.txt，其中包含玩家会读到的每一段文本：台词与选项、角色名、任务标题、说明与步骤、导航和世界地图按钮，以及你自建界面上的文字。每一条都带有一句注释说明它出现在哪里。这个文件夹会随导出的模组包一起打包。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: With an AI tool
docs.translations.ai.term = 借助 AI 工具

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Give it the whole file and ask it to translate only the text after each =, changing nothing else. Save what it gives back in place of the file, then run File ▸ Pack translations ▸ Check the pack's translations: it finds any key, gap, tag or [PV:...] token that did not come back as it went.
docs.translations.ai = 把整个文件交给它，要求它只翻译每个 = 之后的文本，别的一律不动。把它返回的内容原样保存覆盖该文件，然后运行「文件 ▸ 模组包翻译 ▸ 检查模组包的翻译」：它会找出任何没有原样返回的键、花括号占位符、标签或 [PV:...] 标记。

# Docs: the bold label in front of the next text, usually a name on screen
# en: What players see
docs.translations.players.term = 玩家看到的

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: A player whose game is set to that language gets the pack in it, with nothing to set up. A line you change after it was translated shows your new words until the translation is brought up to date, never an old translation of something else.
docs.translations.players = 游戏语言设为该语言的玩家，玩到的就是该语言版本的模组包，无需任何设置。某行台词在被翻译之后又被你改动过，那么在翻译补齐之前会显示你新写的文字，而绝不会显示另一段内容的旧译文。

# Docs: a heading inside the topic
# en: In the game
docs.translations.heading.game = 在游戏中

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: The plugin's Language setting, in BepInEx\config\treboy.starmakerstory.smsmodforge.packplugin.cfg, picks the language for ModForge's own words and for every pack. auto follows the language Windows is set to.
docs.translations.setting = 插件的 Language 设置位于 BepInEx\config\treboy.starmakerstory.smsmodforge.packplugin.cfg，它决定 ModForge 自身用语以及所有模组包所用的语言。auto 会跟随 Windows 设定的语言。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: The game itself is in English and stays so. Its fonts only draw Latin letters, so for Chinese, Japanese, Korean or Russian the plugin puts Windows' own font for that language behind them.
docs.translations.fonts = 游戏本身是英文的，而且会保持英文。它的字体只能绘制拉丁字母，所以对于中文、日文、韩文或俄文，插件会把 Windows 自带的对应语言字体垫在后面。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: ModForge's own words in the game - the pack list on the main menu, the save warning - come from the same translation file as the editor's, under [In the game]. Put the file in BepInEx\plugins\SMSModForge\Languages and players see them in that language.
docs.translations.gameWords = 游戏中 ModForge 自己的用语——主菜单上的模组包列表、存档提醒——与编辑器用语来自同一个翻译文件，位于 [In the game] 之下。把文件放进 BepInEx\plugins\SMSModForge\Languages，玩家就会看到该语言的版本。

# Docs: a heading inside the topic
# en: Worth knowing
docs.translations.heading.worthKnowing = 值得了解

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Translations that came with the editor were made by a machine, and say so in the Language menu until somebody checks them. Corrections are welcome: make them with Edit texts on screen, or in the file, and pass the file on.
docs.translations.machine = 编辑器自带的翻译由机器完成，在有人校对之前，“语言”菜单中会注明这一点。欢迎指正：用“在屏幕上编辑文字”或直接在文件中修改，然后把文件传给大家。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: What a pack stores does not change with the language: keys, names you gave things and the values conditions compare stay as you typed them.
docs.translations.storedStays = 模组包保存的内容不随语言变化：键名、你给各种东西起的名字，以及条件所比较的值，都保持你输入时的原样。

# Docs: a point in the topic
# en: Text you put into a variable and show with [PV:...] is not translated: it is data, and a condition may be comparing it.
docs.translations.variables = 你放进变量、再用 [PV:...] 显示出来的文本不会被翻译：它是数据，而且可能正被某个条件拿来比较。
