[Tutorial: First steps]

# Tutorials: a tutorial's name
# en: First steps
tutorial.firstSteps.title = 第一步

# Tutorials: the line under a tutorial's name
# en: Make a pack, put a character in it, and give it to somebody.
tutorial.firstSteps.summary = 做一个模组包，往里放一个角色，然后把它交给别人。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: What you will end up with
tutorial.firstSteps.willEndUp.title = 你最终会得到什么

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: A pack on disk with one character in it, ready for the game to load. Nothing here is throwaway — this is a real pack, and the next tutorial carries on in the same one.
tutorial.firstSteps.willEndUp.body = 磁盘上一个装着一个角色的模组包，可以直接被游戏加载。这里没有一次性的东西——这是一个真正的模组包，下一篇教程还会在同一个包里继续。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Start a fresh pack
tutorial.firstSteps.startFreshPack.title = 新建一个模组包

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Use File then New pack. Starting clean matters here: the steps that follow watch for things appearing, and in a pack you have already been working in some of them are there before you begin.
tutorial.firstSteps.startFreshPack.body = 使用「文件」然后「新建模组包」。从干净的状态开始在这里很重要：后面的步骤会盯着新出现的东西，而在你已经动过手的包里，其中一些早在你开始前就已经存在了。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: File then New pack. Anything unsaved will be offered back to you first.
tutorial.firstSteps.startFreshPack.hint = 「文件」然后「新建模组包」。未保存的内容会先让你处理。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Save it somewhere
tutorial.firstSteps.saveSomewhere.title = 把它保存到某个地方

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Use File then Save, and make a NEW empty folder for it — not your Desktop or Documents. A pack owns its whole folder: everything inside ends up in the exported file. Saving here also matters because previews and file pickers resolve paths against that folder, and stay empty until there is one.
tutorial.firstSteps.saveSomewhere.body = 使用「文件」然后「保存」，并为它新建一个空文件夹——不要用桌面或「文档」。模组包拥有它整个文件夹：里面的一切都会进到导出的文件里。在这里保存还有一层意义：预览和文件选择器都按那个文件夹来解析路径，在还没有它之前它们都是空的。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: File then Save, or Ctrl+S. Make a new folder rather than reusing one.
tutorial.firstSteps.saveSomewhere.hint = 「文件」然后「保存」，或按 Ctrl+S。请新建一个文件夹，而不要复用已有的。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Some art to work with
tutorial.firstSteps.someArtWork.title = 一些可供上手的素材

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Practice sprites have been copied into a TutorialArt folder inside your pack: busts, a room, a couple of NPCs and a scene. They are yours now — edit them, replace them, or delete the folder when you are done with it.
tutorial.firstSteps.someArtWork.body = 练习用的精灵图已经复制到你模组包里的 TutorialArt 文件夹：几张半身像、一个房间、两个 NPC 和一个场景。它们现在是你的了——随你改、随你换，用完了也可以把文件夹删掉。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Add a character
tutorial.firstSteps.addCharacter.title = 添加一个角色

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Characters are anyone who speaks. Use + Character, which is the one that draws its own bust rather than borrowing the game's.
tutorial.firstSteps.addCharacter.body = 角色就是所有会说话的人。使用 + 角色，它指的是自带半身像的那一种，而不是借用游戏的那一种。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: + Character sits in the toolbar above the character list.
tutorial.firstSteps.addCharacter.hint = + 角色在角色列表上方的工具栏里。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Give them a name
tutorial.firstSteps.giveThemName.title = 给他起个名字

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: This is what players read above a line of dialogue, and the only name you have to write — the identifiers the pack uses internally follow from it.
tutorial.firstSteps.giveThemName.body = 这就是玩家在一行台词上方读到的名字，也是你唯一必须写的名字——模组包内部使用的标识符都由它推导而来。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Type over the placeholder name in the Identity box.
tutorial.firstSteps.giveThemName.hint = 在「身份」框里把示例名字覆盖掉。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Give them a face
tutorial.firstSteps.giveThemFace.title = 给他一张脸

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Every character needs at least one outfit, and every outfit needs a base sprite: the picture of them from the chest up. Point this one at one of the five practice busts. They live in TutorialArt/Busts, one folder each, and the base picture is the file whose name ends in Base00 — so Bust1 is TutorialArt/Busts/Bust1/Bust1Base00.png, and Bust2 through Bust5 follow the same pattern.\n\nAny of them works here. They differ in shape on purpose, which will matter when you paint a jiggle mask later; the fuller ones give you more to see. The other files in those folders are the blink, mouth and expression art, and a later tutorial uses them.\n\nBust art is 256 by 256 pixels, PNG, with a transparent background. Other sizes are scaled to fit rather than refused, but 256 is the only size whose pixels land exactly as you drew them, and anything that is not square gets transparent bars on two sides. When you come to draw your own, that is the shape to draw.
tutorial.firstSteps.giveThemFace.body = 每个角色至少需要一套服装，每套服装都需要一张基础精灵图：胸部以上的那张画。把这一张指向五张练习半身像中的一张。它们在 TutorialArt/Busts 里，各占一个文件夹，基础图就是文件名以 Base00 结尾的那个文件——所以 Bust1 是 TutorialArt/Busts/Bust1/Bust1Base00.png，Bust2 到 Bust5 也遵循同样的规律。\n\n这里用哪一张都行。它们的形状是故意做得不一样的，等你以后要画晃动遮罩时这一点就有意义了；更丰满的那几张能让你看得更清楚。那些文件夹里的其他文件是眨眼、嘴部和表情素材，后面的教程会用到。\n\n半身像素材是 256×256 像素的 PNG，背景透明。其他尺寸会被缩放适配而不是拒绝，但只有 256 这个尺寸能让像素完全按你画的那样落位，而任何非正方形的图都会在两侧补上透明条。等你自己动手画时，要画的就是这个规格。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Use the Browse button beside Base, under Sprites.
tutorial.firstSteps.giveThemFace.hint = 使用「精灵图」下「基础图」旁边的「浏览」按钮。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: There they are
tutorial.firstSteps.thereThey.title = 他出来了

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: The preview draws the bust the way the game will, and keeps up as you work — worth watching rather than exporting to find out.
tutorial.firstSteps.thereThey.body = 预览会按游戏的方式绘制半身像，并随着你的改动同步更新——与其导出了才知道，不如盯着它看。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Say what this outfit has
tutorial.firstSteps.sayOutfitHas.title = 说明这套服装有些什么

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: A bust can carry more than its base picture: a blink frame, four mouth frames that animate while it talks, and four expressions. The three tickboxes are how you DECLARE which of those exist — they are not features to switch on, they are you telling the game what art to go looking for.\n\nYou are not using any of them yet, so untick all three: Has Blink frame, Has Mouth frames, Has Expressions. Getting this wrong is worth understanding now, because it fails silently — a ticked box with no art behind it reads as a BROKEN outfit, and the game skips the whole character rather than skipping the blink. A missing character with no error is almost always this.\n\nLeave Mask empty. An empty mask means the bust does not move, which is a perfectly good answer and the one you want until you paint one.
tutorial.firstSteps.sayOutfitHas.body = 一张半身像可以带的不只是基础图：还有一帧眨眼、四帧说话时会动的嘴部，以及四种表情。那三个勾选框是你用来「声明」这些东西存不存在的——它们不是可以打开的功能，而是你在告诉游戏该去找哪些素材。\n\n你现在一个都还没用上，所以把三个都取消勾选：有眨眼帧、有嘴部帧、有表情。现在就把这一点弄明白很值得，因为它失败时不声不响——勾了框却没有对应素材，会被读作一套“损坏的”服装，于是游戏跳过的是整个角色，而不只是眨眼。角色不见了又没有任何报错，几乎总是这个原因。\n\n把「遮罩」留空。遮罩为空意味着半身像不会动，这是一个完全站得住脚的答案，在你画出遮罩之前也正是你想要的。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Two tickboxes in the Sprites box, one in Expressions just below it.
tutorial.firstSteps.sayOutfitHas.hint = 「精灵图」框里有两个勾选框，正下方的「表情」里还有一个。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: That is a pack
tutorial.firstSteps.pack.title = 这就是一个模组包

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Save it, and File then Export pack bundles the folder into a single .smspack — the file the game loads. Drop that into the Mods folder in your game folder, beside the game's .exe, and it is installed.\n\nExport is the quick loop, for trying your own work in the game. Use it as often as you like: it changes nothing about the pack itself.\n\nThere is nothing to see in the game yet, because a character only appears once something gives them a line.
tutorial.firstSteps.pack.body = 保存它，然后「文件」再「导出模组包」，就会把这个文件夹打包成单个 .smspack——也就是游戏加载的那个文件。把它丢进游戏文件夹里、与游戏 .exe 并列的 Mods 文件夹，就算装好了。\n\n导出是快速循环，用来在游戏里试你自己的成果。想用多少次都行：它完全不会改动模组包本身。\n\n此刻游戏里还没什么可看的，因为角色要等到有东西给他一句台词才会现身。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Giving it to somebody else
tutorial.firstSteps.givingSomebodyElse.title = 把它交给别人

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: File then Publish for players is the other end of that. It checks the pack over first and says so if anything is broken, then writes a zip holding one thing: your pack, already sitting in a Mods folder. Whoever downloads it extracts that into their game folder and is finished — there is no step left for them to get wrong, which is where most mods are lost. Nothing else is in the archive, so installing two packs never asks anybody to overwrite anything.\n\nIt opens the folder it wrote to afterwards, so the file is never somewhere you have to go looking for.
tutorial.firstSteps.givingSomebodyElse.body = 「文件」再「发布给玩家」是这件事的另一端。它会先把模组包检查一遍，有问题就告诉你，然后生成一个只装了一样东西的 zip：你的模组包，而且已经放在一个 Mods 文件夹里。下载的人把它解压到自己的游戏文件夹就完事了——没有留下任何一步可以让他做错，而大多数模组正是折在这一步上。压缩包里没有别的东西，所以装两个模组包永远不会要求谁覆盖什么。\n\n完成后它会打开写入的那个文件夹，所以文件绝不会落在你还得费力去找的地方。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: The language it is written in
tutorial.firstSteps.writtenIn.title = 它所用的语言

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Written in, on this tab, says which language your pack's own words are in. It matters once the pack is translated: translations are made from it, and a player whose language has no translation reads the pack in it.\n\nWhen you export or publish, ModForge offers to translate by machine whatever is not yet in every language it has. Only what is still untranslated is sent, and a line you corrected yourself stays as you wrote it. To read or correct a translation, pick its language under Editing in, at the right end of the tabs, and edit as usual — it is saved with the pack.
tutorial.firstSteps.writtenIn.body = 此标签页上的“编写语言”说明你的模组包本身的文字是用哪种语言写的。模组包被翻译后它就很重要：翻译以它为基础，而没有自己语言翻译的玩家会用这种语言阅读模组包。\n\n导出或发布时，ModForge 会提议用机器翻译还没有覆盖其所有语言的内容。只发送仍未翻译的内容，你亲自改过的行会保持你写的样子。要阅读或修改某个翻译，请在标签页右端的“编辑语言”中选择它的语言，然后照常编辑——它会随模组包一起保存。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: The number beside your pack
tutorial.firstSteps.numberBesidePack.title = 模组包旁边的那个数字

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Publishing also moves the pack's version, and only publishing does. Saving does not, and neither does exporting: a version says what players were given, and an afternoon of saving gives them nothing.\n\nIt moves on its own, by what actually changed since your last release. Something new — a character, a room, a conversation — moves the middle number. A change to something that was already there moves the last one. The first is yours alone: type it into the Version box when you decide a release is a new thing rather than more of the old one.\n\nA place of your own is next: it builds a room and puts a door to it in the bedroom you wake up in, and every tutorial after that goes through the same door.
tutorial.firstSteps.numberBesidePack.body = 发布还会推进模组包的版本，而且只有发布才会。保存不会，导出也不会：版本说明的是交到玩家手里的是什么，而保存了一下午并没有给他们任何东西。\n\n它会自行推进，依据的是自你上次发布以来实际发生的改动。新增的东西——一个角色、一个房间、一段对话——会推进中间那位。已有内容发生改动则推进最后一位。第一位只属于你：当你认定这次发布是一件新东西、而不是旧东西的延续时，就在「版本」框里自己把它写上。\n\n接下来是一个属于你自己的地点：它会搭出一个房间，并在你醒来的那间卧室里放一扇通往它的门，之后的每一篇教程都从这扇门进去。

[Tutorial: A place of your own]

# Tutorials: a tutorial's name
# en: A place of your own
tutorial.aPlace.title = 属于你自己的地点

# Tutorials: the line under a tutorial's name
# en: Build a room out of two layers, give it depth, and a door from your bedroom.
tutorial.aPlace.summary = 用两个图层搭出一个房间，给它做出纵深，再从你的卧室开一扇门过去。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Rooms are two pictures
tutorial.aPlace.roomsTwoPictures.title = 房间是两张图

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: A place is built from a base layer — the room itself — and a secondary layer seen past it. Keeping them apart is what lets a room have depth instead of looking like a painted backdrop.
tutorial.aPlace.roomsTwoPictures.body = 一个地点由基础图层——房间本身——和越过它看到的次级图层构成。把这两者分开，房间才会有纵深，而不像一块画出来的背景板。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Add a place
tutorial.aPlace.addPlace.title = 添加一个地点

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Use + Place, under Your places. The other list is for adding things to rooms the game already has, which is a different job.
tutorial.aPlace.addPlace.body = 在「你的地点」下使用 + 地点。另一份列表是用来给游戏已有的房间添加东西的，那是另一码事。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: + Place is in the toolbar above the left-hand list.
tutorial.aPlace.addPlace.hint = + 地点在左侧列表上方的工具栏里。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: The room itself
tutorial.aPlace.roomItself.title = 房间本身

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Set the back sprite to TutorialArt/Locations/RoomB.png. That is the layer players read as the room they are standing in.
tutorial.aPlace.roomItself.body = 把后景精灵图设为 TutorialArt/Locations/RoomB.png。这一层就是玩家读作「我所站的房间」的那一层。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Level art, then the Back sprite row.
tutorial.aPlace.roomItself.hint = 「关卡美术」，然后是后景精灵图那一行。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: And something standing in it
tutorial.aPlace.somethingStanding.title = 再放点东西在里面

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Now set the front sprite to TutorialArt/Locations/Room.png — a vase on a transparent background. This layer draws in front of the back one, so it reads as an object in the room rather than part of the wall behind it.
tutorial.aPlace.somethingStanding.body = 现在把前景精灵图设为 TutorialArt/Locations/Room.png——透明背景上的一个花瓶。这一层画在后景之前，所以它读起来是房间里的一件物品，而不是它背后那面墙的一部分。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Level art, then the Front sprite row.
tutorial.aPlace.somethingStanding.hint = 「关卡美术」，然后是前景精灵图那一行。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Give it some depth
tutorial.aPlace.giveSomeDepth.title = 给它做出纵深

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Turn off Same as front under Behaviour, and set the back layer to drift by a different amount from the front. Anything different will do — the further apart they are, the deeper the room reads. Watch the preview with Preview parallax switched on.
tutorial.aPlace.giveSomeDepth.body = 在「行为」下关掉「与前景相同」，并让后景图层的漂移幅度与前景不同。只要不一样就行——两者差得越远，房间读起来就越深。打开「预览视差」，一边看预览一边调。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Untick Same as front, then change the Parallax — back value.
tutorial.aPlace.giveSomeDepth.hint = 取消勾选「与前景相同」，然后改「视差——后景」的数值。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: A way back out
tutorial.aPlace.wayBackOut.title = 一条出去的路

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Add a navigator button and set its Target to My Room. These are the buttons along the bottom of a room, and without one your place is somewhere players can arrive but not leave. Sending it back to the bedroom closes the loop: in through the door you are about to build, out through this.
tutorial.aPlace.wayBackOut.body = 添加一个导航按钮，并把它的目标设为「我的房间」。这些就是房间底部的一排按钮，没有它，你的地点就成了玩家进得来却出不去的地方。让它通回卧室就把这个环闭上了：从你即将搭的那扇门进来，从这里出去。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: + Add navigator button, then pick My Room in the row's Target box.
tutorial.aPlace.wayBackOut.hint = + 添加导航按钮，然后在该行的「目标」框里选「我的房间」。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Say what the button reads
tutorial.aPlace.sayButtonReads.title = 说明按钮上写什么

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Set the button's Label. Without one the game falls back to a name worked out from the target, which is usually close but rarely what you would have written — and it is the difference between a button that says "My Room" and one that says whatever the level happens to be called underneath.\n\nWrite what a player would call the place. The label also takes [PV:name] tokens, so a button can change what it says once something in the pack has happened.
tutorial.aPlace.sayButtonReads.body = 设置按钮的标签。没有标签时，游戏会退而使用从目标推导出来的名字，通常八九不离十，但很少是你本来会写的那句——它决定了按钮上写的是「我的房间」，还是底层关卡恰好叫的那个名字。\n\n写玩家会用来称呼这个地方的说法。标签同样接受 [PV:name] 标记，所以模组包里发生某件事之后，按钮上的字可以随之改变。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: The Label box on the navigator button row.
tutorial.aPlace.sayButtonReads.hint = 导航按钮那一行里的「标签」框。

[Tutorial: Putting things in the room]

# Tutorials: a tutorial's name
# en: Putting things in the room
tutorial.dressingTheRoom.title = 往房间里放东西

# Tutorials: the line under a tutorial's name
# en: Add objects to a place, layer them, and leave one switched off.
tutorial.dressingTheRoom.summary = 给地点添加对象、安排层次，并留一个处于关闭状态。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: A room is more than two pictures
tutorial.dressingTheRoom.roomMoreThanTwo.title = 房间不只是两张图

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: The back and front layers are the room itself. Everything ELSE in it is a GameObject: its own picture, at its own position, with its own place in the stack — and, unlike the layers, something a dialogue or a rule can switch on and off while the game runs.\n\nThat is how a room changes. A door that opens is two objects, one switched off. A coat left on a chair is an object that appears once a conversation has happened. A lamp that comes on at night is the same trick again.\n\nObject art is NOT a level layer, so it is not 2048 by 1136. Draw it at whatever size the thing really is, on a transparent background; it is placed in the room rather than filling it.
tutorial.dressingTheRoom.roomMoreThanTwo.body = 后景和前景图层就是房间本身。房间里其他的一切都是 GameObject：有自己的图、自己的位置、在层叠中自己的位次——而且和图层不同，它是对话或规则可以在游戏运行时开关的东西。\n\n房间就是这样发生变化的。一扇会开的门是两个对象，其中一个是关着的。搭在椅子上的外套，是某段对话发生之后才出现的对象。到了夜里亮起的台灯，又是同一个把戏。\n\n对象的美术素材不是关卡图层，所以它不是 2048×1136。按那样东西实际的尺寸去画，背景透明；它是被摆进房间里的，而不是把房间填满。

tutorial.dressingTheRoom.openRoomBuilt.title = 打开你搭好的房间

# Tutorials: what the step says

# en: Your places are the upper list; vanilla extensions are below it.
tutorial.dressingTheRoom.openRoomBuilt.hint = 上面那份列表是你的地点；原版扩展在它下面。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step

# Tutorials: what the step says

tutorial.dressingTheRoom.addObject.body = 使用 + 添加 GameObject。它刚出现时是空的、没有名字，这没关系——下一步才让它成为一样东西。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: + Add GameObject, under the GameObjects box.

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Give it a name and a picture
tutorial.dressingTheRoom.giveNamePicture.title = 给它一个名字和一张图

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: The name is how everything else finds this object — a rule that switches it on names it, and names repeat all over a level, so pick something you would recognise months from now.\n\nFor the picture use TutorialArt/Locations/Vase.png. It is worth looking at before you pick it: it is a vase and almost nothing else, on a transparent background, at the size a vase actually is. Compare that to Room.png, which contains the same vase inside a full 2048 by 1136 room — the padding IS the difference between a layer and a prop. Art with a room's worth of empty space around it cannot be placed, only laid over everything.
tutorial.dressingTheRoom.giveNamePicture.body = 名字是其他一切找到这个对象的途径——开启它的规则要靠名字指名它，而名字在一个关卡里到处重复，所以挑一个几个月后你还认得出来的。\n\n图就用 TutorialArt/Locations/Vase.png。挑之前值得先看一眼：它就是一个花瓶，几乎没有别的，背景透明，尺寸也就是花瓶实际的大小。对比一下 Room.png，那里同样的花瓶被放在一整间 2048×1136 的房间里——周围那圈空白正是图层与道具的区别所在。四周带着一整间房那么多空白的素材是摆不进去的，只能盖在所有东西之上。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Select the object in the tree, then fill in Name and its sprite.
tutorial.dressingTheRoom.giveNamePicture.hint = 在树里选中该对象，然后填写「名称」和它的精灵图。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Put it where you want it
tutorial.dressingTheRoom.putWhereWant.title = 把它放到你想要的位置

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Drag the object in the preview. The gizmo moves the real position — this is not a preview-only control, it is the same X and Y you could type in by hand, and typing is the fallback when you want an exact number rather than a look.\n\nPut the vase somewhere it could plausibly stand. It will be drawing over the whole room at the moment, which the next step fixes.
tutorial.dressingTheRoom.putWhereWant.body = 在预览里拖动这个对象。操纵手柄移动的是真实位置——这不是只影响预览的控件，它就是你可以手动输入的那对 X 和 Y，而当你要的是一个精确数值而不是一种观感时，手动输入就是退路。\n\n把花瓶放在一个它有可能真的立在那里的位置。此刻它还会盖在整个房间之上，下一步就会修好这一点。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Click the object in the preview, then drag it.
tutorial.dressingTheRoom.putWhereWant.hint = 在预览里点中该对象，然后拖动它。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Decide what it stands in front of
tutorial.dressingTheRoom.decideStandsFront.title = 决定它站在什么前面

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Sorting order is the stack. The back layer sits at -12 and the front layer at -10, so an object at -11 stands BETWEEN them: behind the furniture in the front layer, in front of the wall behind it. Anything above -10 draws over the whole room.\n\nA new object starts at 0, which is over everything. Set it to -11 and watch the preview put it into the room rather than on top of it.
tutorial.dressingTheRoom.decideStandsFront.body = 排序值就是层叠顺序。后景图层在 -12，前景图层在 -10，所以排序值为 -11 的对象就夹在两者之间：在前景图层的家具之后，在它背后的墙之前。任何高于 -10 的东西都会盖住整个房间。\n\n新对象从 0 开始，也就是盖在一切之上。把它设成 -11，看预览把它放进房间里，而不是摆在房间上面。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Sorting order, on the selected object. -11 puts it between the layers.
tutorial.dressingTheRoom.decideStandsFront.hint = 选中对象上的「排序」。-11 会把它放到两个图层之间。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Leave it switched off
tutorial.dressingTheRoom.leaveSwitchedOff.title = 让它保持关闭

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Untick Start active. The object is still in the room, at the position you gave it — it simply is not shown when the player arrives.\n\nThis is half of every change a room ever makes. The other half is a Set-Active action, from a dialogue node or an integration rule, naming this object. Build the object switched off now and the rule that reveals it has something to point at later.
tutorial.dressingTheRoom.leaveSwitchedOff.body = 取消勾选「初始激活」。对象仍然在房间里，就在你给它的位置上——只是玩家到达时它不显示。\n\n房间所做的每一次改变，这都是其中一半。另一半是一条设为激活的动作，由对话节点或集成规则发出，指名这个对象。现在就把对象建成关闭的，将来那条负责揭示它的规则才有东西可指。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Start active, on the selected object. Untick it.
tutorial.dressingTheRoom.leaveSwitchedOff.hint = 选中对象上的「初始激活」。取消勾选它。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: What the preview is telling you
tutorial.dressingTheRoom.previewTelling.title = 预览在告诉你什么

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: An object that starts switched off is drawn faded, so you can still position it — the game will draw nothing there until something turns it on. Fade objects that start inactive turns that off while you work.\n\nThe same goes for anything under a switched-off parent: the game draws none of it, so the preview fades all of it. If a whole group of your objects has gone pale, that is why.
tutorial.dressingTheRoom.previewTelling.body = 初始处于关闭状态的对象会以变暗的方式绘制，好让你仍然能摆放它——在有东西开启它之前，游戏在那里什么都不会画。「初始未激活的对象变暗」可以在你工作时关掉这种显示。\n\n位于关闭的父对象之下的一切也是如此：游戏一点都不会画，所以预览会把它们全都变暗。如果你的一整组对象都发白了，原因就在这里。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: A room that can change
tutorial.dressingTheRoom.roomCanChange.title = 一个会变化的房间

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: You now have a room with something in it that is not part of the picture — placed, layered, and waiting to be switched on.\n\nObjects nest, so one can be a container for several and switching the parent moves all of them at once. And the Documentation section covers the rest of what a place carries: masks, weather, and the beach ambience a room can keep or drop.
tutorial.dressingTheRoom.roomCanChange.body = 现在你有了一个房间，里面有一样不属于背景图的东西——摆好了位置、分好了层次，只等被开启。\n\n对象可以嵌套，所以一个对象可以充当多个对象的容器，开关父对象就能一次带动全部。至于地点还承载些什么——遮罩、天气，以及房间可以保留或舍弃的海滩环境音——「文档」一节里都有。

[Tutorial: On the world map]

# Tutorials: a tutorial's name
# en: On the world map
tutorial.onTheWorldMap.title = 登上世界地图

# Tutorials: the line under a tutorial's name
# en: Put a place on the world map so a player can travel to it directly.
tutorial.onTheWorldMap.summary = 把一个地点放上世界地图，让玩家可以直接前往。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Two ways to reach a room
tutorial.onTheWorldMap.twoWaysReachRoom.title = 抵达一个房间的两种方式

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: You have already built one: a navigator button on the bedroom's strip, which is a door from one room to another. It works, and it is the right shape for somewhere that sits BEHIND another place — a back office off a shop, a bedroom off a hall.\n\nThe world map is the other way, and it means something different. A place on the map is a destination in its own right rather than a room reached through another one.\n\nThe map is not open from everywhere, mind: the player reaches it by going to the Garage, into the Car, and pressing Drive. So a map button suits somewhere they would deliberately travel to, and a navigator button suits somewhere they walk into from next door. Most places want one or the other, not both.
tutorial.onTheWorldMap.twoWaysReachRoom.body = 其中一种你已经搭好了：卧室导航条上的一个按钮，也就是从一个房间通往另一个房间的门。它管用，而且对于藏在另一个地点「后面」的地方来说形态正合适——店铺后面的办公室、走廊边上的卧室。\n\n世界地图是另一种方式，含义也不一样。地图上的地点是一个独立的目的地，而不是要穿过另一个房间才到得了的屋子。\n\n不过要注意，地图并不是随处都能打开的：玩家得先去车库、上车，再按「驾驶」。所以地图按钮适合玩家会特意前往的地方，而导航按钮适合他从隔壁走进去的地方。大多数地点要的是其中之一，而不是两个都要。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step

tutorial.onTheWorldMap.sayWhereGoes.title = 说明它通向哪里

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Set Target place to the room you built. A button with no target is the quietest mistake in the editor: it saves, it exports, it appears on the map, and clicking it does nothing at all.\n\nValidate catches it, which is the reason to run Validate before every export rather than after something goes wrong.
tutorial.onTheWorldMap.sayWhereGoes.body = 把「目标地点」设为你搭好的房间。没有目标的按钮是编辑器里最不声不响的错误：它能保存、能导出、能出现在地图上，可点下去什么都不会发生。\n\n「验证」能抓到它，这也正是每次导出前都该跑一遍验证、而不是等出了问题再跑的理由。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Target place, on the card you just added.
tutorial.onTheWorldMap.sayWhereGoes.hint = 你刚添加的那张卡片上的「目标地点」。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Choose which menu it lives in
tutorial.onTheWorldMap.chooseWhichMenuLives.title = 选择它属于哪个菜单

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: The world map is not one list of buttons — it is five radial menus, one per district: Seaside, The Line, Neon Row, Shopside and Foundry. District decides which one your button joins.\n\nA new button arrives in Foundry because it had to arrive somewhere. That is a default, not a decision. Pick the district your place actually belongs to — a player who opens the wrong menu will not find it, and nothing will tell them it exists.
tutorial.onTheWorldMap.chooseWhichMenuLives.body = 世界地图不是一份按钮列表——它是五个环形菜单，每个城区一个：Seaside、The Line、Neon Row、Shopside 和 Foundry。「城区」决定你的按钮加入哪一个。\n\n新按钮会出现在 Foundry，只是因为它总得先落在某处。那是默认值，不是决定。请挑选你的地点真正所属的城区——打开错误菜单的玩家根本找不到它，也不会有任何东西告诉他它存在。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: District, on the same card. Any of the five except the one it started in.
tutorial.onTheWorldMap.chooseWhichMenuLives.hint = 同一张卡片上的「城区」。五个里面随便选一个，只要不是它最初所在的那个。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Give it something to read
tutorial.onTheWorldMap.giveSomethingRead.title = 给它一点可读的字

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Label is the text on the button. Write what a player would call the place, not what you called it in the editor.\n\nIt also accepts [PV:name], which is replaced with one of your pack's variables while the game runs — so a button can read "The Shop" before a conversation and "Anna's Shop" after one, without a second button.
tutorial.onTheWorldMap.giveSomethingRead.body = 「标签」是按钮上的文字。写玩家会怎么称呼这个地方，而不是你在编辑器里给它起的名字。\n\n它同样接受 [PV:name]，在游戏运行时会被换成你模组包里的某个变量——于是同一个按钮可以在某段对话之前写着「那家店」，之后写着「安娜的店」，而不需要第二个按钮。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: The two you can leave alone
tutorial.onTheWorldMap.twoCanLeaveAlone.title = 可以不用管的两项

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Music switches the background track when the button is used, which is worth setting for somewhere with its own atmosphere and worth leaving empty otherwise — an empty Music keeps whatever was playing.\n\nConditions decide whether the button is shown at all. An empty list means always, which is what you want for now. Fill it in and the place stays off the map until the player has earned it — the same condition rows you will meet on dialogues and rules.
tutorial.onTheWorldMap.twoCanLeaveAlone.body = 「音乐」会在按下按钮时切换背景曲目，对于有自己氛围的地方值得设置，其他情况则值得留空——音乐留空会保留原本正在播放的曲子。\n\n「条件」决定按钮是否显示。列表为空表示始终显示，这也正是你现在想要的。填上条件，这个地点就会一直不出现在地图上，直到玩家把它挣到手——用的就是你在对话和规则里会遇到的同样那几行条件。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Somewhere to go
tutorial.onTheWorldMap.somewhereGo.title = 一个可去之处

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Validate, export, and open the world map in game: your place is in the district you chose, under the label you wrote.\n\nYou now have both routes — a door from the bedroom and a place on the map — and they are independent. Removing one does not affect the other, which is worth knowing when a place becomes reachable two ways by accident.
tutorial.onTheWorldMap.somewhereGo.body = 验证、导出，然后在游戏里打开世界地图：你的地点就在你选的城区里，写着你写的那行标签。\n\n现在两条路你都有了——一扇从卧室出发的门，和一个地图上的地点——而且它们彼此独立。去掉一条不会影响另一条；当一个地点不小心变得两条路都能到时，这一点值得记住。

[Tutorial: Your first conversation]

# Tutorials: a tutorial's name
# en: Your first conversation
tutorial.firstConversation.title = 你的第一段对话

# Tutorials: the line under a tutorial's name
# en: Write a dialogue that starts in your room, with a choice in it.
tutorial.firstConversation.summary = 写一段在你房间里开始的对话，中间带一个选择。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: A dialogue is a tree
tutorial.firstConversation.dialogueTree.title = 对话是一棵树

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Play runs down the node list from the top, and children are just nodes nested under a parent — reaching the end of a branch carries on to whatever is next in the list, not back out. Every node is one of three kinds. Text plays its line and moves on. Choice stops and offers its children to the player. Random picks one child itself. Everything else on a node — who speaks, what happens after, what it sets — hangs off that.
tutorial.firstConversation.dialogueTree.body = 播放从顶端开始沿着节点列表往下走，而子节点不过是嵌在父节点下面的节点——走到一条分支的尽头，会继续走列表里的下一项，而不是退回外层。每个节点都是三种类型之一。文本播放它的台词然后继续。选择会停下来，把它的子节点摆给玩家。随机则自己挑一个子节点。节点上的其余一切——谁在说、之后发生什么、它设定了什么——都挂在这上面。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while

# Tutorials: the heading of a step

tutorial.firstConversation.restNodeBox.title = 「节点」框里的其余部分

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Actor is who speaks, and leaving it empty plays the line with whoever is already on screen. Expression and Outfit swap the bust mid-conversation — your practice character has neither, so leave them be. Tag names a node so a jump can find it. Duration decides whether the line waits for a click or times out on its own.
tutorial.firstConversation.restNodeBox.body = 「演员」是说话的人，留空则由已经在画面上的人来说这句话。「表情」和「服装」会在对话中途更换半身像——你的练习角色两样都没有，所以别动它们。「标签」给节点起个名字，好让跳转能找到它。「时长」决定这一行是等待点击，还是自己到点推进。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Give the player a say
tutorial.firstConversation.givePlayerSay.title = 让玩家也有得说

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Add a second node, set its Kind to Choice, and hang two children off it with + Child. The Choice node's own Text is the prompt, shown under the options — it is what the player is answering. Each child IS an option, and the child's Text is the label on its button, not a line somebody says. Write all three: anything left blank shows up in game as blank, which is what an empty node looks like from the outside.\n\nGive the CHOICE node an actor too — its text is spoken, so it needs somebody to speak it. The children do not: an option is a button, not a line, and nobody says it aloud.
tutorial.firstConversation.givePlayerSay.body = 添加第二个节点，把它的「类型」设为选择，再用 + 子节点给它挂两个子节点。选择节点自身的「文本」是提示语，显示在各个选项下方——那正是玩家要回答的话。每个子节点本身就是一个选项，子节点的「文本」是它按钮上的字，而不是谁说出来的台词。三处都要写：留空的地方在游戏里就是空白，这正是空节点从外面看起来的样子。\n\n也给那个「选择」节点配一个演员——它的文本是说出来的，所以需要有人来说。子节点则不需要：选项是按钮，不是台词，没有人会把它念出来。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Set Kind to Choice, write its Text, + Child twice, then write each child's Text.
tutorial.firstConversation.givePlayerSay.hint = 把「类型」设为选择，写好它的「文本」，按两次 + 子节点，然后分别写每个子节点的「文本」。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: What happens after a node
tutorial.firstConversation.happensAfterNode.title = 一个节点之后会发生什么

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Jump decides where play goes next. Continue — the default — moves to the next node down the list, whether that is a child of this one or something further out. Exit stops the conversation and skips everything below. Jump resumes at whichever node carries the Tag you name.
tutorial.firstConversation.happensAfterNode.body = 「跳转」决定播放接下来去哪里。继续——默认值——前往列表中的下一个节点，无论它是本节点的子节点还是更外层的内容。退出会停止对话并跳过下面的一切。跳转则在带有你所指「标签」的那个节点继续。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Make Exit mean something
tutorial.firstConversation.makeExitMeanSomething.title = 让「退出」真有意义

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: At the very bottom of the list Continue and Exit are the same thing, because there is nothing left to skip either way. So give Exit something to skip: add one more root node below the choice with a closing line, then set ONE of your two options to Exit. Take that option in game and the conversation stops; take the other and it carries on into the closing line.\n\nGive the closing line an actor as well. Every node that SAYS something needs one — a line with no speaker shows no name, which reads as a fault rather than as a narrator.
tutorial.firstConversation.makeExitMeanSomething.body = 在列表最末尾，继续和退出是一回事，因为反正已经没有什么可跳过了。所以给退出一点可跳过的内容：在选择下面再加一个根节点，写上一句收尾台词，然后把你两个选项中的「一个」设为退出。在游戏里选那一个，对话就停住；选另一个，就会继续走到收尾台词。\n\n收尾台词也要配演员。凡是「说话」的节点都需要一个——没有说话人的台词不会显示名字，这读起来像是出了故障，而不像旁白。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: + Root for the closing line, then select an option and set Jump to Exit.
tutorial.firstConversation.makeExitMeanSomething.hint = 用 + 根节点写收尾台词，然后选中一个选项，把「跳转」设为退出。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Actions are how a line changes things
tutorial.firstConversation.actionsLineChangesThings.title = 台词靠动作来改变事物

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Actions on start and on finish hang off any node, and they are what makes a conversation matter: set a variable, unlock a wallpaper, swap a sprite, play a sound. Later tutorials use them properly. Worth knowing now that the line is the visible half and the actions are the other one.
tutorial.firstConversation.actionsLineChangesThings.body = 开始时和结束时的动作可以挂在任何节点上，正是它们让一段对话有分量：设定变量、解锁壁纸、更换精灵图、播放声音。后面的教程会认真用到它们。现在值得记住的是：台词是看得见的那一半，动作是另一半。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Check it before you export
tutorial.firstConversation.checkBeforeExport.title = 导出前先检查一遍

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Validate catches what the editor can see: an option with no text, an actor the pack never defines, a jump aimed at a tag no node carries, a level renamed underneath the dialogue. Run it now — a dialogue is the easiest thing in a pack to leave half-finished, because a blank node looks the same as a filled one in the tree.
tutorial.firstConversation.checkBeforeExport.body = 「验证」能抓到编辑器看得见的问题：没有文字的选项、模组包从未定义的演员、指向无人持有的标签的跳转、在对话底下被改了名的关卡。现在就跑一遍——对话是模组包里最容易写到一半就撂下的东西，因为空白节点在树里看起来和填好的一模一样。

tutorial.firstConversation.goHaveConversation.title = 去把这段对话走一遍

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Export, load a save, and take the bedroom button through to your room. The dialogue starts on arrival, because that is what the level in its start conditions means. Reading your own line in the game tells you things the editor cannot — whether it is too long, and whether the choice actually reads as a choice.
tutorial.firstConversation.goHaveConversation.body = 导出、载入存档，再从卧室的按钮走进你的房间。对话会在你抵达时开始，因为它开始条件里的关卡就是这个意思。在游戏里读自己写的台词，能看出编辑器看不出的东西——它是不是太长了，以及那个选择读起来是不是真的像一个选择。

[Tutorial: Lines that choose themselves]

# Tutorials: a tutorial's name
# en: Lines that choose themselves
tutorial.linesThatChoose.title = 会自己挑选的台词

# Tutorials: the line under a tutorial's name
# en: Gate a line on the state of the room, and let two speakers share a scene.
tutorial.linesThatChoose.summary = 让一句台词取决于房间的状态，并让两位说话者共处一幕。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Not every line should play
tutorial.linesThatChoose.notEveryLineShould.title = 不是每句台词都该播放

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: So far every node in your dialogue runs when play reaches it. A conversation that reacts to anything needs the opposite: lines that sometimes do not.\n\nThat is what a node's own Conditions box is for, and it behaves in two different ways depending on where the node sits. An ordinary node whose conditions fail is SKIPPED — play carries on to the next one. A node directly under a Choice is not offered at all: the player never sees that option, rather than seeing it greyed out.
tutorial.linesThatChoose.notEveryLineShould.body = 到目前为止，你对话里的每个节点只要播放走到就会运行。一段能对什么做出反应的对话需要的恰恰相反：有些台词有时候不该播放。\n\n这正是节点自身那个「条件」框的用途，而且它的表现会随节点所处的位置分成两种。普通节点的条件不成立时会被「跳过」——播放继续走向下一个。直接位于「选择」之下的节点则根本不会被摆出来：玩家压根看不到那个选项，而不是看到一个变灰的选项。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Open the conversation you wrote
tutorial.linesThatChoose.openConversationWrote.title = 打开你写的那段对话

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Same pack, same dialogue. Pick it in the list, then select any node in the tree — the one you want the condition on.
tutorial.linesThatChoose.openConversationWrote.body = 还是同一个模组包、同一段对话。在列表里选中它，然后在树里选中任意一个节点——你想给它加条件的那个。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Click the dialogue, then a node in the list beside it.
tutorial.linesThatChoose.openConversationWrote.hint = 先点那段对话，再点旁边列表里的一个节点。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Gate it on something real
tutorial.linesThatChoose.gateSomethingReal.title = 让它取决于真实存在的东西

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Add a condition to that node with + Add condition, then finish it — an empty condition row is not a gate, it is a blank that always passes.\n\nSet its Type to GameObjectActive. The row then asks the same three things in the same order the Set-Active action asks them: Category, Level, Target. That is deliberate — a condition reads back exactly what that action sets, so it addresses the object the same way.\n\nSet Category to GameObjects, since the object lives inside a place. Set Level to your place: the Target dropdown stays disabled until you do, because it lists that level's objects and nothing else. Then pick the object you put in the room and left switched off.\n\nDoing this against something you already built matters. The object is genuinely off, so the line genuinely will not play — and when a rule switches the object on later, the line starts appearing on its own. The conversation is reading the state of the room.
tutorial.linesThatChoose.gateSomethingReal.body = 用 + 添加条件给那个节点加一个条件，然后把它填完整——空白的条件行不是门槛，它是一处永远通过的空白。\n\n把它的「类型」设为 GameObjectActive。这一行随后会按设为激活的那个动作完全相同的顺序，问同样的三件事：类别、关卡、目标。这是有意为之——条件读取的正是那个动作所写入的内容，所以它寻址对象的方式也一模一样。\n\n把「类别」设为 GameObjects，因为这个对象住在某个地点里面。把「关卡」设为你的地点：在你设好之前，「目标」下拉框一直是禁用的，因为它只列出那个关卡里的对象。然后挑出你放进房间又留着没开的那个对象。\n\n拿你已经建好的东西来做这一步很重要。那个对象确确实实是关着的，所以这句台词确确实实不会播放——而等日后某条规则把对象开启，这句台词就会自己开始出现。这段对话读的是房间的状态。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Type = GameObjectActive, then Category, Level and Target below it.
tutorial.linesThatChoose.gateSomethingReal.hint = 类型 = GameObjectActive，然后是它下面的类别、关卡和目标。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Conditions do not stop at objects
tutorial.linesThatChoose.conditionsDoNotStop.title = 条件不止于对象

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: The same box offers everything the pack can ask about: which place is on screen, what a variable holds, whether it is raining, a percentage rolled once a day. Every condition in the list has to pass, and an empty list always passes — which is why a node with no conditions always plays.\n\nAny condition can also be negated, which is usually shorter than writing the opposite one.
tutorial.linesThatChoose.conditionsDoNotStop.body = 同一个框里提供了模组包能询问的一切：屏幕上是哪个地点、某个变量装着什么、是否在下雨、一个每天掷一次的百分比。列表里的每个条件都必须通过，而空列表永远通过——这正是没有条件的节点总会播放的原因。\n\n任何条件也都可以取反，这通常比写出相反的那一个更省事。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Someone else in the room
tutorial.linesThatChoose.someoneElseRoom.title = 房间里的另一个人

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: A conversation is rarely one voice. The Actor field on a node says who speaks it, and it holds from that node onward until another one changes it — you do not set it per line.\n\nEvery pack has a speaker it did not create: the player. Add a node and set its Actor to the player, and the line is the player talking back. Any mod addressing you reaches that same character, which is why its name and colour are fixed and yours to use rather than define.
tutorial.linesThatChoose.someoneElseRoom.body = 一段对话很少只有一个声音。节点上的「演员」字段说明由谁来说这句话，并且从那个节点起一直有效，直到另一个节点更改它——不必逐行设置。\n\n每个模组包都有一个自己没创造的说话者：玩家。添加一个节点，把它的「演员」设为玩家，这句台词就是玩家的回话。任何对你说话的模组都指向同一个角色，这也是为什么他的名字和颜色是固定的，供你使用，而不由你定义。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Add a node, write a line, then set Actor to player.
tutorial.linesThatChoose.someoneElseRoom.hint = 添加一个节点，写一句台词，然后把「演员」设为玩家。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Letting one option say more
tutorial.linesThatChoose.lettingOneOptionSay.title = 让其中一个选项多说几句

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Last tutorial you gave one option Exit and let the other fall through to your closing line. Both are still one line long. Real dialogue is lopsided — one answer earns a reaction, the other does not — and the branch that reacts still has to end up somewhere, or you write the ending twice.\n\nA TAG is a name you give a node so another node can send play to it. Two fields do it. Tag, on the node being aimed AT: a short word, yours to choose, and it is a label rather than anything the player sees. Jump, on the node doing the aiming: set it to Jump, and a Jump to box appears listing the tags in this dialogue.\n\nBuild it in that order — the tag first, so it is there to be picked. Tag your closing line, the one you added last tutorial, with something like ending. Then take the option you did NOT set to Exit, add a Child under it, and write the reaction that answer deserves. On that child set Jump to Jump, and choose ending in the Jump to box.\n\nPlay now runs the option, the reaction, and then lands on the closing line. The other option still ends where it ended. One ending, written once, reached two ways — and nothing repeats, because every jump here goes forward.
tutorial.linesThatChoose.lettingOneOptionSay.body = 上一篇教程里，你给一个选项设了退出，让另一个自然落到收尾台词。两个都还只有一行。真实的对话是不对称的——一个回答值得一句回应，另一个不值得——而作出回应的那条分支最终也得落到某个地方，否则你就得把结尾写两遍。\n\n「标签」是你给某个节点起的名字，好让另一个节点把播放送到它那里。要用两个字段。「标签」写在被指向的那个节点上：一个短词，你自己定，它是内部的记号，玩家看不到。「跳转」写在发出指向的那个节点上：把它设为跳转，就会出现一个「跳转到」框，列出本段对话里的标签。\n\n按这个顺序来搭——先写标签，它才会在那里可供挑选。给你上一篇教程加的那句收尾台词打个标签，比如 ending。然后找到你「没有」设为退出的那个选项，给它加一个子节点，写上那个回答应得的回应。在那个子节点上把「跳转」设为跳转，并在「跳转到」框里选 ending。\n\n现在播放会走过这个选项、这句回应，然后落到收尾台词。另一个选项仍然在原来的地方结束。一个结尾，只写一遍，两条路都能到——而且什么都不会重复，因为这里的跳转都是往前走的。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Tag the closing line, then + Child on the non-Exit option and set its Jump to that tag.
tutorial.linesThatChoose.lettingOneOptionSay.hint = 先给收尾台词打标签，然后在没有设为退出的那个选项上 + 子节点，并把它的「跳转」设为那个标签。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Jumping backwards on purpose
tutorial.linesThatChoose.jumpingBackwardsPurpose.title = 有意往回跳

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Forward is not the only direction, and backwards is not a mistake. Jumping back to a Choice is one of the most useful shapes there is, and the game itself is full of it: a menu where each option answers and then returns, except one that carries the conversation on. Ask about the weather, come back. Ask about her sister, come back. Say you should get going, and that one continues.\n\nTo build it, tag the Choice node itself, then give the last line of each returning branch a jump to that tag. The option that leaves simply does not jump — it continues, or exits.\n\nThe one thing to be sure of is a way out. If every option comes back, the player cannot leave; at least one has to not return. Put that option in the menu from the start rather than behind a condition, or a player who fails the condition is stuck in a conversation with no exit.\n\nAnd give a tag to exactly one node. Two nodes carrying the same tag make every jump aimed at it ambiguous: play goes to whichever the game reaches first, which is not something you chose and may not stay the same.
tutorial.linesThatChoose.jumpingBackwardsPurpose.body = 往前不是唯一的方向，往回也不是错误。跳回一个「选择」是最有用的结构之一，游戏本身里到处都是：一个菜单，每个选项答完就回来，只有一个把对话往下带。问问天气，回来。问问她妹妹，回来。说你该走了，这一个就继续。\n\n要搭出来，先给「选择」节点本身打标签，再给每条会返回的分支的最后一句台词设一个跳到那个标签的跳转。要离开的那个选项干脆不跳——它继续，或者退出。\n\n唯一要确保的是留一条出路。如果每个选项都回来，玩家就走不掉；至少得有一个不返回。把那个选项从一开始就放进菜单里，而不要藏在条件后面，否则没通过条件的玩家就困在一段没有出口的对话里了。\n\n还有，同一个标签只给一个节点。两个节点带着同一个标签，会让每个指向它的跳转都变得含糊：播放会去到游戏先找到的那一个，那并不是你选定的，而且未必一直不变。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: A conversation with a memory of the room
tutorial.linesThatChoose.conversationMemoryRoom.title = 记得房间的一段对话

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Your dialogue now has a line that only plays when the room is in a particular state, a second speaker, and a branch that rejoins.\n\nWhat it still cannot do is remember anything across visits — the room's state is the room's, not the conversation's. That is what variables are for, and they are the next group.
tutorial.linesThatChoose.conversationMemoryRoom.body = 现在你的对话里有一句只在房间处于特定状态时才播放的台词、第二位说话者，以及一条会重新汇合的分支。\n\n它仍然做不到的，是跨越多次到访去记住什么——房间的状态属于房间，不属于对话。那正是变量的用途，也是下一组教程的内容。

[Tutorial: A face that moves]

# Tutorials: a tutorial's name
# en: A face that moves

# Tutorials: the line under a tutorial's name
# en: Give a bust its blink, its mouth and its expressions.

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: A bust is a stack of pictures
tutorial.aFaceThatMoves.bustStackPictures.title = 半身像是一叠图

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: The base sprite is the whole character. Everything else is a small picture laid over the same 256 by 256 frame, in the same place, swapped in when the game wants it.\n\nThere are three kinds. A BLINK frame is the eyes closed, shown for a fifth of a second every few seconds. Four MOUTH frames cycle while a line types, so the character looks like they are speaking. Four EXPRESSIONS — Happy, Angry, Sad and Flirty — are swapped in by a dialogue line and stay until something changes them.\n\nEach is drawn on a transparent background at the same size as the base, so it lines up. Draw only the part that changes: for a blink, that is a pair of closed eyes and nothing else.
tutorial.aFaceThatMoves.bustStackPictures.body = 基础精灵图是整个角色。其余的一切都是叠在同一张 256×256 画框、同一位置上的小图，游戏需要时才换上来。\n\n一共有三类。「眨眼」帧是闭着的眼睛，每隔几秒显示五分之一秒。四张「嘴部」帧会在台词打字时循环，让角色看起来在说话。四种「表情」——Happy、Angry、Sad 和 Flirty——由对话台词换上，并一直保持到有东西改变它们。\n\n每一张都画在透明背景上，尺寸与基础图相同，这样才能对齐。只画会变化的那部分：对眨眼来说，就是一对闭着的眼睛，别的都不画。

tutorial.aFaceThatMoves.openCharacterMade.hint = 展开角色看到它的服装，然后点其中一套。

tutorial.aFaceThatMoves.fourMouthsNamedPrefix.body = 嘴部帧不是一张一张挑的。你给出一个「前缀」——一条从模组包根目录出发的路径，和上面的基础图、眨眼那两行一样，只是停在数字和 .png 之前。游戏会给它加上 1、2、3、4，所以 TutorialArt/Busts/Bust1/Mouth 会找到 Mouth1.png 到 Mouth4.png。框后面的灰色文字显示的是它当前将要去找的文件。\n\n那正好是四帧：不是三帧，也不是五帧。\n\n勾选「有嘴部帧」。文件夹已经填好了——它是从你刚设好的眨眼路径复制过来的，因为同一套服装的叠加图都放在一起——所以还差的是文件名那一段。在末尾补上 Mouth。

[Tutorial: Making it move]

# Tutorials: a tutorial's name
# en: Making it move
tutorial.makingItMove.title = 让它动起来

# Tutorials: the line under a tutorial's name
# en: Paint a jiggle mask and tune it until the bust moves the way you want.
tutorial.makingItMove.summary = 画一张晃动遮罩，并调到半身像按你想要的方式动。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: A mask is not artwork
tutorial.makingItMove.maskNotArtwork.title = 遮罩不是美术素材

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: It records how much of an effect each part of a sprite gets, and it holds three separate layers. Bounce moves those pixels up and down. Wave slides them side to side, in a wave that travels up the sprite. Noise scatters them irregularly. A fourth value, the overall intensity, scales all three at once — so black is no movement, and brighter is more. Painting a mask is deciding where a bust moves, which way, and by how much.
tutorial.makingItMove.maskNotArtwork.body = 它记录的是精灵图的每一部分受到多少效果，并且包含三个独立的层。弹跳让那些像素上下移动。波动让它们左右滑动，形成一道沿着精灵图向上传播的波。噪声让它们不规则地散动。第四个数值——总体强度——同时缩放这三者，所以黑色是不动，越亮动得越多。画遮罩，就是在决定半身像哪里动、朝哪动、动多少。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Open the character you made
tutorial.makingItMove.openCharacterMade.title = 打开你做的那个角色

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: This carries on in the same pack, on the same character as the first tutorial — masks belong to an outfit, so there has to be one selected before anything here applies to it. Click your character in the tree, then the outfit underneath it.
tutorial.makingItMove.openCharacterMade.body = 这一篇还在同一个模组包里继续，用的也是第一篇教程里的那个角色——遮罩属于服装，所以必须先选中一套服装，这里的东西才会作用在它上面。在树里点你的角色，再点它下面的服装。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Expand the character to see its outfits, and click one.
tutorial.makingItMove.openCharacterMade.hint = 展开角色看到它的服装，然后点其中一套。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Pick a bust with something to move
tutorial.makingItMove.pickBustSomethingMove.title = 挑一张有东西可动的半身像

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: The five practice busts differ in shape on purpose, and the one you chose first was picked before any of that mattered. Point this outfit's base sprite at one of the fuller ones instead — the effect is far easier to judge when there is more of it to see.
tutorial.makingItMove.pickBustSomethingMove.body = 五张练习半身像形状不同是故意的，而你最初挑的那一张，是在这些还都无关紧要时挑的。把这套服装的基础精灵图改指向更丰满的那几张——可看的东西多了，效果也就好判断得多。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: TutorialArt/Busts/Bust4 or Bust5 give you the most to work with.
tutorial.makingItMove.pickBustSomethingMove.hint = TutorialArt/Busts/Bust4 或 Bust5 能给你最多可发挥的余地。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Paint where it should move
tutorial.makingItMove.paintWhereShouldMove.title = 画出它该动的地方

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Open Edit Mask and paint the Bounce layer over the parts that should move. Keep away from the edges — a mask that reaches the outline drags the whole silhouette and reads as a wobble rather than weight.\n\nSaving offers to put the file beside the bust art, named after it: Bust4Base.png gets Bust4BaseMask.png. Nothing requires that — a mask can live anywhere inside the pack — but a mask is unreadable on its own, and months later the only thing that says which sprite it was drawn for is where it sits and what it is called.
tutorial.makingItMove.paintWhereShouldMove.body = 打开「编辑遮罩」，在该动的部位上涂「弹跳」层。离边缘远一点——够到轮廓的遮罩会把整个剪影一起拖动，读起来是整体摇晃，而不是重量感。\n\n保存时它会建议把文件放在半身像素材旁边、按它命名：Bust4Base.png 对应 Bust4BaseMask.png。这并不是强制的——遮罩可以放在模组包里的任何地方——但遮罩单看是读不懂的，几个月后，唯一能说明它是为哪张精灵图画的，就是它放在哪里、叫什么名字。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Edit Mask, brush with B, paint, then Save (Ctrl+S) in that window.
tutorial.makingItMove.paintWhereShouldMove.hint = 「编辑遮罩」，按 B 取画笔，涂好后在那个窗口里保存（Ctrl+S）。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: What the Jiggle numbers do
tutorial.makingItMove.jiggleNumbersDo.title = 晃动的那些数值做什么

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: The mask says WHERE the sprite moves; these say how. Strength is how far the pixels travel and Speed how fast the cycle runs. Wave frequency is how many waves fit across the sprite, so a low number sways and a high one ripples. The three Noise settings keep it from ticking like a metronome — Scale is how fine the grain is, Speed how fast it drifts, Strength how much of it shows through. Tint multiplies the sprite's colour and is almost always left white. The defaults are a sensible bust: change one at a time and watch the preview, because the numbers mean far less than what you see.
tutorial.makingItMove.jiggleNumbersDo.body = 遮罩说的是精灵图「哪里」动；这些说的是怎么动。强度是像素移动的幅度，速度是循环跑得多快。波频是精灵图宽度上能容下几道波，所以数值小是摇曳，数值大是涟漪。三个噪声设置让它不至于像节拍器那样机械——噪声缩放是颗粒的粗细，噪声速度是它漂移得多快，噪声强度是它透出来多少。着色会与精灵图的颜色相乘，几乎总是留白。默认值就是一个合理的半身像：一次只改一个并盯着预览，因为数值本身远不如你看到的效果重要。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: The droplets, over here too
tutorial.makingItMove.dropletsOverHereToo.title = 水珠，这边也有

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Under Jiggle there is a Particles box with one tickbox in it. The game's Wet particle preset — the droplets it uses for swim and shower art — is attached to every pack bust whether you ask for it or not; the tickbox only decides whether it is PLAYING when the bust appears.\n\nLeave it off for an ordinary outfit, or a dry character talks to the player in the rain. Tick it on the swimsuit or just-out-of-the-shower variant, which is the whole reason it exists.\n\nNPCs have the same setting on their own tab, with one difference worth knowing now: for an NPC the emitter's POSITION is set per placement rather than on the definition, because the same figure can stand in more than one room.
tutorial.makingItMove.dropletsOverHereToo.body = 「晃动」下面有一个「粒子」框，里面只有一个勾选框。游戏的「湿身」粒子预设——它用于泳装和出浴素材的那些水珠——无论你要不要，都挂在每一张模组包半身像上；这个勾选框只决定半身像出现时它是不是正在「播放」。\n\n普通服装请让它关着，否则一个干爽的角色会在雨里跟玩家说话。在泳装或刚出浴的那套上勾选它，这也正是它存在的全部理由。\n\nNPC 在自己的页签里有同样的设置，但有一点现在就值得知道：对 NPC 而言，发射器的「位置」是按每次摆放来设定的，而不是写在定义里，因为同一个人物可以站在不止一个房间里。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: What the preview can show you
tutorial.makingItMove.previewCanShow.title = 预览能告诉你什么

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Breathing is already on, and it is the mask doing its work — the fastest way to tell whether you painted too much. Depth beside it is how pronounced that idle motion is.\n\nThe other controls drive the overlays you added last tutorial: Blinking cycles the blink frame, Yapping runs the mouth, Mouth frame holds one frame still so you can look at it, and Expression swaps in one of the four faces. Turn them on while the mask is running — that combination is what the player actually sees.\n\nWatch the face while you do, because the mask does not reach it. Only the base sprite is displaced; the blink, the mouth and the expressions sit on top of it exactly where they were drawn and do not move at all.\n\nSo paint across the face and the face slides out from under its own eyes and mouth. Nothing looks wrong until an overlay is showing, which is why it is easy to miss: turn Blinking and Yapping on and the drift is obvious.\n\nThat is the whole reason most busts move at the chest and the hair and leave the face alone. Keep the mask off it, or dark enough there that nothing separates.
tutorial.makingItMove.previewCanShow.body = 「呼吸」本来就是开着的，那正是遮罩在起作用——也是判断你有没有涂过头的最快办法。旁边的「深度」是这种待机动作的明显程度。\n\n其余的控件驱动你上一篇教程加的那些叠加图：「眨眼」循环眨眼帧，「说话」让嘴动起来，「嘴部帧」把某一帧定住供你端详，「表情」则换上四张脸中的一张。在遮罩运行时把它们打开——那个组合才是玩家真正看到的样子。\n\n一边看一边盯着脸，因为遮罩够不到脸。被位移的只有基础精灵图；眨眼、嘴部和表情就叠在上面、停在画好的位置，一动也不动。\n\n所以只要你在脸上涂了遮罩，脸就会从自己的眼睛和嘴底下滑出去。在叠加图显示出来之前一切看着都没问题，这正是它容易被忽略的原因：把「眨眼」和「说话」打开，错位就一目了然了。\n\n这就是为什么大多数半身像只在胸口和头发上动，而放过脸部。把遮罩从脸上挪开，或者在那里涂得足够暗，让什么都不会分离。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Then judge it in the game
tutorial.makingItMove.thenJudgeGame.title = 然后在游戏里下判断

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Export and go through to your room, so the character speaks with the mask running. The preview and the game use the same code and the same numbers, but a bust at the size players see it, mid-line, is where too much movement gives itself away.
tutorial.makingItMove.thenJudgeGame.body = 导出并走进你的房间，让角色带着运行中的遮罩说话。预览和游戏用的是同一套代码和同样的数值，但只有在玩家实际看到的尺寸下、在台词进行当中，动得太多才会露出马脚。

tutorial.populating.dropletsDoNotWant.body = 湿身粒子是游戏用于泳装和出浴变体的水珠效果。新建的 NPC 默认是关的，这正是你这里想要的——一个干爽的人物站在房间里却带着水珠，读起来就像室内下雨。等你真的需要时再勾选「启用」，届时发射器的位置按每次摆放来设定，而不是在这里设，因为同一个 NPC 可以站在不止一个房间里。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: The shadow under an NPC is already on, and it is the cheapest thing there is for how a room reads: without one a figure looks pasted onto the picture rather than standing in it. Worth turning OFF only for someone who is not standing on anything, like a face at a window.
tutorial.populating.sitThemFloor.body = NPC 脚下的影子本来就是开着的，而它是改善房间观感最便宜的东西：没有影子，人物看起来像是贴在画面上，而不是站在画面里。颜色决定它有多深，排序值决定它画得多靠前——要远在人物之下，这样才不会压到腿。只有对没有站在任何东西上的人才值得「关掉」，比如窗户里的一张脸。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Back on Places, use + Add NPC on the place you built. That gives you an empty placement row, and the first field on it is the one that matters: NPC. Pick the one you just defined.\n\nA row with that field blank is a placement of nobody. It costs nothing, breaks nothing and shows nothing, which is exactly why it is easy to leave behind and hard to find later.\n\nThe same NPC can stand in as many rooms as you like — you are placing a copy, not moving the original.
tutorial.populating.putThemRoom.body = 回到「地点」，在你搭好的地点上使用 + 添加 NPC。这会给你一行空的摆放，而这一行上第一个字段才是关键：NPC。选上你刚定义的那个。\n\n这个字段留空的一行，就是一次谁也没放的摆放。它不花什么代价、不弄坏什么、也不显示什么——正因如此，它很容易被落下，事后又很难找到。\n\n同一个 NPC 想站在多少个房间里都行——你摆放的是一个副本，而不是把原件挪走。

tutorial.populating.putThemRoom.hint = 「GameObjects」下的 + 添加 NPC，然后在该行的「NPC」框里选你的 NPC。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step

tutorial.populating.switchThem.title = 把他们打开

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: A placement starts INACTIVE. The default assumes the common pattern — a parked variant that some rule switches on later — and yours has nothing to switch it on. Tick Start active on the placement, or the room will be empty when you walk into it and nothing will say why.
tutorial.populating.switchThem.body = 摆放默认是「未激活」的。这个默认值假定的是常见做法——一个先摆在那里、之后由某条规则开启的变体——而你这一个没有任何东西会去开启它。在摆放上勾选「初始激活」，否则你走进去时房间会空无一人，而且没有任何提示说明原因。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while

tutorial.populating.switchThem.hint = 「GameObjects」下该摆放自己那一行上的「初始激活」。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Place them properly
tutorial.populating.placeThemProperly.title = 好好安排他们的位置

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Drag them somewhere that makes sense for the room, and check the sorting order so they are not standing behind the furniture. The preview shows it at the size players will see.\n\nClick the figure and a small toolbar appears in the top-right corner of the preview. Its first row is what you are moving: Body, Shadow, Blink or Wet. The second is how — Move, Rotate or Scale.\n\nThat is how a shadow gets nudged out from under someone standing half behind a table, or the droplet emitter gets lifted to where the water would actually be. All four live on the PLACEMENT, not on the NPC, so moving the shadow here does not move it in any other room the same NPC stands in.\n\nThe same fields are on the placement row under Part transforms, if you would rather type a number than drag.
tutorial.populating.placeThemProperly.body = 把他们拖到房间里说得通的位置，并检查排序值，别让他们站在家具后面。预览显示的就是玩家将会看到的尺寸。\n\n点中人物，预览右上角会出现一个小工具条。它的第一行是你要移动的部分：身体、影子、眨眼或湿身。第二行是怎么动——移动、旋转或缩放。\n\n这就是把某个半掩在桌后的人的影子挪出来、或者把水珠发射器抬到水实际该在的位置的办法。这四项都属于「摆放」，而不属于 NPC，所以在这里挪动影子，不会影响同一个 NPC 站着的其他房间。\n\n如果你更愿意输入数值而不是拖动，摆放那一行的「部件变换」里有同样的字段。

tutorial.populating.seeWhetherRoomReads.body = 导出，再从卧室的按钮走进去。有人的房间，是一个地点第一次不再像一张画；而只有站在里面，你才判断得出他们是碍事，还是正好在位置上。

[Tutorial: One person, two places]

# Tutorials: a tutorial's name
# en: One person, two places
tutorial.npcsThatBelong.title = 同一个人，两个地方

# Tutorials: the line under a tutorial's name
# en: Reuse an NPC, and give it a reflection and a blink so it sits in the room.
tutorial.npcsThatBelong.summary = 复用一个 NPC，并给它加上倒影和眨眼，让它真正融进房间。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: A definition is not an appearance
tutorial.npcsThatBelong.definitionNotAppearance.title = 定义不等于出场

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: The NPCs tab does not put anybody in a room. It DEFINES one: the art, the movement, the blink, the shadow. Putting them somewhere is a separate act, on the Places tab, and it is called a placement.\n\nOne definition can have any number of placements — the same figure in three rooms, or twice in one. Change the definition and every placement changes with it. Change a placement and only that one moves.\n\nThat split is the whole point of the tab. It is also the thing to get straight before you wonder why editing one NPC moved another.
tutorial.npcsThatBelong.definitionNotAppearance.body = NPC 页签不会把任何人放进房间。它「定义」一个人：美术素材、动作、眨眼、影子。把人放到某个地方是另一回事，在「地点」页签里进行，称为摆放。\n\n一个定义可以有任意多次摆放——同一个人物出现在三个房间，或者在一个房间里出现两次。改定义，所有摆放都跟着变。改某一次摆放，动的只有那一个。\n\n这种分离正是这个页签的全部要义。也是在你纳闷「怎么改了一个 NPC，另一个也动了」之前，先要弄清楚的事。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Put the same person in twice
tutorial.npcsThatBelong.putSamePersonTwice.title = 把同一个人放两次

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Go to your room and add the NPC you already made a SECOND time. Two placements, one definition — the same drawing standing in two spots.\n\nIn a real pack this is a crowd: one figure, placed six times at different positions and scales, is six people as far as the player is concerned.
tutorial.npcsThatBelong.putSamePersonTwice.body = 回到你的房间，把你已经做好的那个 NPC「再」放一次。两次摆放、一个定义——同一张画站在两个位置上。\n\n在真正的模组包里，这就是人群：一个人物，以不同的位置和缩放摆放六次，在玩家看来就是六个人。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: + Add NPC on your place, then pick the NPC you defined.
tutorial.npcsThatBelong.putSamePersonTwice.hint = 在你的地点上 + 添加 NPC，然后选你定义好的那个 NPC。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Tell them apart
tutorial.npcsThatBelong.tellThemApart.title = 把他们区分开

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Give the new placement its own Name. This is the placement's name, not the NPC's: it is what a Set-Active action points at when a rule wants to hide THIS one and leave the other standing.\n\nTwo placements with the same name in one room is the mistake to avoid — a rule aiming at that name reaches whichever the game finds first, and which one that is may change.
tutorial.npcsThatBelong.tellThemApart.body = 给新的这次摆放起一个自己的「名称」。这是摆放的名字，不是 NPC 的名字：当某条规则想藏起「这一个」而让另一个继续站着时，设为激活的动作指的就是它。\n\n同一个房间里两次摆放同名，是要避免的错误——指向那个名字的规则会作用到游戏先找到的那一个，而那是哪一个可能会变。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Select the new placement and type a Name unlike the other one's.
tutorial.npcsThatBelong.tellThemApart.hint = 选中新的那次摆放，输入一个与另一个不同的「名称」。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Back to the definition
tutorial.npcsThatBelong.backDefinition.title = 回到定义

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Now change something on the NPC itself and watch BOTH placements follow. Open the NPCs tab and select the NPC you defined — the next two steps act on it.
tutorial.npcsThatBelong.backDefinition.body = 现在改一改 NPC 本身，看两次摆放会不会都跟着变。打开 NPC 页签，选中你定义的那个 NPC——接下来两步都作用在它上面。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Stand them on something
tutorial.npcsThatBelong.standThemSomething.title = 让他们站在什么东西上

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Turn on Reflection. It draws a mirrored copy beneath the pose, and it is what makes a figure look like it is standing on a wet street or a polished floor rather than in front of one.\n\nIt is off by default because most floors are not reflective, and a reflection on a carpet reads as a mistake. Alpha is how visible it is — the game's own reflections are a faint wash, not a second character — and Y offset moves it to meet the feet rather than the middle of the sprite.\n\nYou do not have to guess that offset. Once reflection is on, a Reflection chip appears in the preview toolbar on the Places tab alongside Body, Shadow, Blink and Wet — pick it and drag the reflection up or down against the actual floor of the actual room.\n\nIt drags vertically and nothing else, because that offset is the only number there is: the mirror is dropped a full pose height automatically, and this is the correction on top. And unlike the other four, it belongs to the NPC rather than the placement, so moving it here moves it in every room that NPC stands in. The toolbar says so while it is selected.
tutorial.npcsThatBelong.standThemSomething.body = 打开「倒影」。它会在姿势下方画一份镜像副本，正是它让人物看起来是站在湿漉漉的街道或抛光地面「上」，而不是站在它前面。\n\n它默认是关的，因为大多数地面并不反光，而地毯上的倒影读起来就像出了错。「Alfa」是它有多明显——游戏自己的倒影是一层淡淡的影子，而不是第二个角色——「Y 偏移」则把它挪到与双脚相接的位置，而不是精灵图的中间。\n\n这个偏移你不用靠猜。倒影一打开，「地点」页签的预览工具条上就会出现一个「倒影」标签，和身体、影子、眨眼、湿身并列——选中它，对着真实房间里真实的地面把倒影上下拖动。\n\n它只能竖直拖动，别的都不行，因为那个偏移是唯一的数值：镜像会自动下移整整一个姿势的高度，而这是在此之上的修正。而且与另外四项不同，它属于 NPC 而不属于摆放，所以在这里挪动它，会影响那个 NPC 所在的每一个房间。选中它时工具条上会这么写。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Enabled, in the Reflection box.
tutorial.npcsThatBelong.standThemSomething.hint = 「倒影」框里的「启用」。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Give them eyes that close
tutorial.npcsThatBelong.giveThemEyesClose.title = 给他们一双会闭上的眼睛

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: An NPC blinks the same way a bust does, from one eyes-closed frame drawn over the pose. Point Blink sprite at the blink art beside the pose you used: TutorialArt/NPCs/Dummy, where DummyNPC0.png has DummyNPC0Blink.png next to it.\n\nOpen wait min and max are the range the gap between blinks is drawn from, so a room full of the same figure does not blink in unison, and Closed hold is how long each blink lasts. Leave a blank blink sprite and the NPC simply does not blink, which is a fine answer.
tutorial.npcsThatBelong.giveThemEyesClose.body = NPC 的眨眼方式和半身像一样，靠一张画在姿势之上的闭眼帧。把「眨眼精灵图」指向你所用姿势旁边的眨眼素材：TutorialArt/NPCs/Dummy，那里 DummyNPC0.png 旁边就是 DummyNPC0Blink.png。\n\n「睁眼等待最小/最大」是两次眨眼之间的间隔取值范围，免得满屋同一个人物齐刷刷一起眨眼；「闭眼保持」则是每次眨眼持续多久。把眨眼精灵图留空，这个 NPC 就干脆不眨眼，这也是个完全说得过去的答案。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Blink sprite, in the Blink box.
tutorial.npcsThatBelong.giveThemEyesClose.hint = 「眨眼」框里的「眨眼精灵图」。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: The one to leave alone
tutorial.npcsThatBelong.oneLeaveAlone.title = 该放着别动的那一项

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Wet particles are the droplet effect, and they are off by default for a reason: they suit somebody who has just come out of the water and look like a fault on anybody else. Enabled turns the effect on at all; Start active decides whether it is already running when the room loads, rather than waiting for something to start it.\n\nLeave both off. Knowing where they are is enough.
tutorial.npcsThatBelong.oneLeaveAlone.body = 湿身粒子就是那个水珠效果，它默认关闭是有道理的：它适合刚从水里出来的人，用在别人身上就像出了故障。「启用」决定这个效果开不开；「初始激活」决定房间载入时它是不是已经在播放，而不是等着什么东西来启动它。\n\n两个都保持关闭。知道它们在哪儿就够了。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: A room with people in it
tutorial.npcsThatBelong.roomPeople.title = 一个有人的房间

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Both placements now reflect and blink, because both are the same definition — and they stand in different spots under different names, because a placement is its own thing.\n\nThat is the shape to remember: art and behaviour on the NPC, position and identity on the placement. A crowd is one definition and many placements; a cast is many definitions.
tutorial.npcsThatBelong.roomPeople.body = 现在两次摆放都会有倒影、都会眨眼，因为它们是同一个定义；而它们站在不同的位置、用着不同的名字，因为摆放本身是独立的一件事。\n\n这个结构值得记住：美术和行为在 NPC 上，位置和身份在摆放上。人群是一个定义配许多次摆放；而一整个角色阵容则是许多个定义。

[Tutorial: Sound in a room]

# Tutorials: a tutorial's name
# en: Sound in a room
tutorial.soundInARoom.title = 房间里的声音

# Tutorials: the line under a tutorial's name
# en: Add a music track and a sound effect, and make one fire from a line.
tutorial.soundInARoom.summary = 添加一首音乐和一个音效，并让其中一个由台词触发。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Two different jobs
tutorial.soundInARoom.twoDifferentJobs.title = 两件不同的活

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Music is the track underneath everything: one at a time, looping, changed by an action or by a map button. SFX are one-shots — a door, a slap, a splash — and several can play at once without cutting each other off.\n\nBoth take OGG, WAV or MP3, so use whatever your source gives you rather than converting for the sake of it. The practice files are deliberately one of each: an MP3 track and a WAV effect.\n\nKeep music long enough to loop without becoming obvious, and effects short. An effect that runs for two seconds is still playing when the line that fired it has gone.
tutorial.soundInARoom.twoDifferentJobs.body = 音乐是垫在一切之下的曲子：一次一首、循环播放，由动作或地图按钮来切换。音效是一次性的——一扇门、一记耳光、一声落水——而且可以几个同时响，互不打断。\n\n两者都接受 OGG、WAV 或 MP3，所以你的素材是什么格式就用什么，不必为了转而转。练习文件特意各给一个：一首 MP3 曲子和一个 WAV 音效。\n\n音乐要够长，循环起来才不明显；音效要短。一个响两秒的音效，在触发它的台词早已过去时还在响。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Add a track
tutorial.soundInARoom.addTrack.title = 添加一首曲子

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Use + Music, then point Audio path at TutorialArt/Audio/Neon Rain.mp3 — the longer of the two files in that folder, and the one meant to loop under a scene.\n\nGive it a display name you would recognise in a list of twenty. Note the Runtime name beside it: that key is what everything else in the pack asks for this track by, and you will type it into a button in two steps' time.
tutorial.soundInARoom.addTrack.body = 使用 + 音乐，然后把「音频路径」指向 TutorialArt/Audio/Neon Rain.mp3——那个文件夹里较长的那个文件，也是打算循环垫在一场戏底下的那一首。\n\n给它起一个你在二十条里也认得出来的显示名称。注意旁边的「运行时名称」：模组包里其他一切都用那个键来点名这首曲子，再过两步你就要把它写进一个按钮里。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: + Music, then Audio path in the pane on the right.
tutorial.soundInARoom.addTrack.hint = + 音乐，然后是右侧面板里的「音频路径」。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Hear it without leaving the editor
tutorial.soundInARoom.hearWithoutLeavingEditor.title = 不离开编辑器就能听

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Play sounds the track at the volume beside it. It plays ONCE here whatever Loop says — looping is a runtime behaviour, and the preview is for checking the file is the file you meant.\n\nLoop and Volume can both be left blank, and blank means "do what the game normally does" rather than zero. Fill them in only when you want to differ from that.
tutorial.soundInARoom.hearWithoutLeavingEditor.body = 「播放」会按旁边的音量播放这首曲子。无论「循环」怎么设，这里都只播放「一次」——循环是运行时的行为，预览是用来确认这个文件就是你想要的那个。\n\n「循环」和「音量」都可以留空，而留空的意思是「照游戏平常的做法来」，不是零。只有当你想与之不同时才去填。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Make something play it
tutorial.soundInARoom.makeSomethingPlay.title = 让什么东西去播它

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: A track nobody starts is a file in a folder. The shortest way to hear yours in game is the door you already built: back in tutorial 3 you put a navigator button on My Room pointing at your place.\n\nOpen that vanilla extension on the Places tab, find the button, and put your track's RUNTIME NAME in its Music box — the key from the Music tab, not the display name and not the filename. Walking through that door now changes the music.\n\nOne track plays at a time, and pressing the button switches to this one, so a button that leads back out wants a Music of its own or the room's track follows the player out. The same box lists the game's own tracks under their own heading, and the one called Music is the ordinary everyday music — that is usually what the way back out should say.\n\nMap buttons have the same box, and it is the more common place for it: the world map is how a player reaches most locations, so if you built one in tutorial 4, give it the same treatment. Same field, same runtime name, same one-at-a-time rule.
tutorial.soundInARoom.makeSomethingPlay.body = 没人去启动的曲子，只是文件夹里的一个文件。在游戏里听到你这首曲子的最短路径，就是你已经搭好的那扇门：第 3 篇教程里，你在「我的房间」放了一个指向你地点的导航按钮。\n\n在「地点」页签里打开那个原版扩展，找到那个按钮，把你曲子的「运行时名称」填进它的「音乐」框——是「音乐」页签里的那个键，不是显示名称，也不是文件名。现在走过那扇门，音乐就会换。\n\n同一时间只播放一首，而按下这个按钮就会切到这一首，所以一个通向外面的按钮也需要自己的「音乐」，否则房间的曲子会跟着玩家走出去。同一个框会在自己的标题下列出游戏自有的曲子，其中名为 Music 的那一首就是日常的普通音乐——回程按钮通常该填的就是它。\n\n地图按钮也有同样的框，而且那里更常用到：世界地图是玩家抵达大多数地点的途径，所以如果你在第 4 篇教程里做了一个，也照样处理。同一个字段、同样的运行时名称、同样的一次只播一首的规则。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Places tab, your My Room extension, the navigator button's Music box.
tutorial.soundInARoom.makeSomethingPlay.hint = 「地点」页签、你那个「我的房间」扩展、导航按钮的「音乐」框。

Its filename is somebody's credit for the recording, which is why it looks like that and why it has not been renamed.\n\nDefault volume is what plays when something asks for this effect without saying how loud — which is most of the time.
tutorial.soundInARoom.addEffect.body = 现在轮到「音效」页签。做法一样：+ 音效，然后把「音频路径」指向 TutorialArt/Audio 里「另一个」文件——那个短小的 WAV，内容是一扇门打开。它的文件名是录音者的署名，这就是它长这样、也一直没被改名的原因。\n\n「默认音量」是当有东西调用这个音效却没说明音量时所用的值——而那是绝大多数情况。
