# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: The part you would never guess
tutorial.soundInARoom.partWouldNeverGuess.title = 你绝对猜不到的那一部分

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: An effect does not need an action. Auto-trigger patterns is a comma-separated list of text, and whenever one of those appears in a line of dialogue the effect fires by itself.\n\nThe convention is a lowercase word between asterisks — *door* — because it reads as a stage direction in the script and never collides with an ordinary word. Put a pattern in now.\n\nThis is how a scene gets its sound without every line carrying an action. It is also completely invisible from the tab, which is the only reason this step exists.
tutorial.soundInARoom.partWouldNeverGuess.body = 音效不一定需要动作。「自动触发模式」是一串以逗号分隔的文本，只要其中之一出现在某行对话里，音效就会自行响起。\n\n惯例是用星号括起的小写单词——*door*——因为它在剧本里读起来像一句舞台提示，而且绝不会和普通词撞上。现在就填一个模式进去。\n\n一场戏就是这样获得声音的，而不必每一行都挂一个动作。它在页签上也完全看不出来，这正是本步骤存在的唯一理由。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Auto-trigger patterns, on the SFX you just made. Try *door*.
tutorial.soundInARoom.partWouldNeverGuess.hint = 你刚做的那个音效上的「自动触发模式」。试试 *door*。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Say it in a line
tutorial.soundInARoom.sayLine.title = 把它写进台词里

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Go back to your conversation and put the pattern into a node's text — the actual characters, asterisks and all. When that line plays, the effect plays with it.\n\nThe pattern stays in the text as written, so pick something you are content for a player to read, or keep it to a line where a stage direction belongs.
tutorial.soundInARoom.sayLine.body = 回到你的对话，把这个模式写进某个节点的文本里——原样的字符，连星号一起。那一行播放时，音效就会跟着响。\n\n模式会原封不动留在文本里，所以挑一个你不介意让玩家读到的，或者只放在适合出现舞台提示的那一行。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Select a node and add your pattern to its Text.
tutorial.soundInARoom.sayLine.hint = 选中一个节点，把你的模式加进它的「文本」里。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: The other way to fire one
tutorial.soundInARoom.otherWayFireOne.title = 触发音效的另一种办法

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Patterns are not the only route. A node can carry a PlaySFX action, on start or on finish, naming the effect by its runtime name — with a Volume to override the default and a Delay to hold it back a moment.\n\nThe two do the same job and differ in exactly one way: the pattern has to be IN the line, so the player reads *door* along with everything else. The action does not, so the sound plays over a line with nothing odd in it. Everything else is the same, including the random variants in the next step.\n\nUse patterns where the stage direction belongs in the script, and the action where it does not. Add one now, on the same conversation.
tutorial.soundInARoom.otherWayFireOne.body = 模式并不是唯一的路子。节点可以在开始时或结束时挂一个 PlaySFX 动作，按运行时名称点名那个音效——还可以用「音量」覆盖默认值，用「延迟」让它晚一点再响。\n\n两者做的是同一件事，区别恰恰只有一点：模式必须写「在」台词里，所以玩家会连着别的内容一起读到 *door*。动作则不必，所以声音可以叠在一句毫无异样的台词上。其余全都一样，包括下一步要讲的随机变体。\n\n舞台提示本来就该出现在剧本里时用模式，不该出现时用动作。现在就在同一段对话里加一个。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: + Add action on a node, Type = PlaySFX, then pick your effect.
tutorial.soundInARoom.otherWayFireOne.hint = 在某个节点上 + 添加动作，类型 = PlaySFX，然后选你的音效。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: More than one take
tutorial.soundInARoom.moreThanOneTake.title = 不止一条录音

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: One more thing with no field for it. Put extra recordings beside the first with _1, _2, _3 on the end of the same name, and the game picks between them at random each time the effect plays.\n\nAudio/Door.wav picks up Audio/Door_1.wav and Audio/Door_2.wav on its own. Number them without gaps — counting stops at the first missing number, so _1, _2, _4 loads two of the three. The extensions do not have to match, so a WAV alongside an OGG is fine.\n\nIt is the difference between a door that sounds the same every time and one that does not. Both routes draw from the same set: a pattern in a line and a PlaySFX action pick a variant the same way.
tutorial.soundInARoom.moreThanOneTake.body = 还有一件没有对应字段的事。把额外的录音放在第一个旁边，用同样的名字并在末尾加 _1、_2、_3，游戏每次播放这个音效时就会在它们之间随机挑一条。\n\nAudio/Door.wav 会自行收集 Audio/Door_1.wav 和 Audio/Door_2.wav。编号不要跳号——收集会在第一个缺失的编号处停止，所以 _1、_2、_4 只会载入三条里的两条。扩展名不必一致，所以 OGG 旁边放一个 WAV 也没问题。\n\n这就是一扇门每次听起来都一样、和每次都不太一样之间的区别。两条路子取的是同一组素材：台词里的模式和 PlaySFX 动作，挑变体的方式完全相同。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: A pack that makes noise
tutorial.soundInARoom.packMakesNoise.title = 一个会出声的模组包

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: You have a track, an effect, and a line that fires it without being asked. Scenes and Wallpapers are the other two media tabs and you have already met both — a scene in the rules tutorial, a wallpaper in the variables one.\n\nThat is every tab in the editor. What is left is not more kinds of thing, it is more of what you can already do.
tutorial.soundInARoom.packMakesNoise.body = 你已经有了一首曲子、一个音效，以及一句不用人吩咐就会触发它的台词。场景和壁纸是另外两个媒体页签，你也都见过了——规则那篇教程里的场景，变量那篇里的壁纸。\n\n编辑器的页签到此就全了。剩下的不是更多种类的东西，而是把你已经会做的事做得更多。

[Tutorial: Remembering things]

# Tutorials: a tutorial's name
# en: Remembering things
tutorial.remembering.title = 记住事情

# Tutorials: the line under a tutorial's name
# en: Give the pack a memory, and use it to gate what players can see.
tutorial.remembering.summary = 给模组包一份记忆，并用它来限制玩家能看到什么。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Packs forget by default
tutorial.remembering.packsForgetDefault.title = 模组包默认是会忘事的

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Every conversation starts the same way unless something remembers what happened. Variables are that memory, and almost everything interesting in a pack is built on them.
tutorial.remembering.packsForgetDefault.body = 除非有什么东西记住发生过什么，否则每段对话都以同样的方式开始。变量就是那份记忆，模组包里几乎所有有意思的东西都建立在它们之上。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Declare one
tutorial.remembering.declareOne.title = 声明一个

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Press + Variable. It arrives called var1, with Type already set to Bool — a yes/no value, which is what you want. Change Name to something that says what it MEANS: Met_Anna reads far better in a condition six months from now than Flag_3 or var1 does. No spaces, and you will be picking this name from a list later, so make it one you will recognise.
tutorial.remembering.declareOne.body = 按 + 变量。它出现时叫 var1，「类型」已经设成 Bool——一个是/否的值，正是你想要的。把「名称」改成能说明它「含义」的东西：半年后再看一个条件，Met_Anna 比 Flag_3 或 var1 好读得多。不要空格；而且以后你还要从列表里挑这个名字，所以起一个你认得出来的。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: + Variable, then type over var1 in the Name box.
tutorial.remembering.declareOne.hint = + 变量，然后在「名称」框里把 var1 覆盖掉。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Persisted, and why it is already on
tutorial.remembering.persistedWhyAlready.title = 持久保存，以及它为什么已经是开着的

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Persisted is ticked for you, and it means the value is written to the pack's save file — so what the player did is still true next time they load. Leave it on here. You would untick it for something that only matters during one visit, like a flag saying "this conversation already greeted them", which should start fresh every session rather than being remembered forever.
tutorial.remembering.persistedWhyAlready.body = 「持久保存」已经替你勾上了，它的意思是这个值会被写进模组包的存档文件——所以玩家做过的事，下次读档时依然成立。这里保持开启。只有当某样东西只在一次到访中有意义时你才会取消勾选，比如一个记录「这段对话已经跟他打过招呼了」的标记，它应该每次运行都从头开始，而不是被永远记住。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Set it from a conversation
tutorial.remembering.setConversation.title = 从对话里给它赋值

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Nothing has changed the variable yet — declaring one only says it exists. Four things, in order. One: on the Dialogues tab, click a node in the tree to select it. Two: under Actions on finish, press + Add action. Three: set that row's Type to Variable, and leave Operation on Set and Source on Pack. Four: choose your variable in the Variable box, and choose True — a Bool gets True and False buttons rather than a text field, so there is no spelling to get wrong. Actions on finish runs when the player moves past the line, so the value changes once they have read it.
tutorial.remembering.setConversation.body = 目前还没有任何东西改动过这个变量——声明它只是说明它存在。按顺序四件事。一：在「对话」页签里，点树中的一个节点把它选中。二：在「结束时的动作」下按 + 添加动作。三：把那一行的「类型」设为 Variable，「操作」保持 Set，「来源」保持 Pack。四：在「变量」框里选你的变量，并选择「真」——Bool 得到的是「真」和「假」两个按钮而不是文本框，所以不存在拼错的可能。「结束时的动作」在玩家看完这一行往下走时运行，所以值是在他读过之后才改变的。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: + Add action, Type = Variable, Operation = Set, pick your Variable, choose True.
tutorial.remembering.setConversation.hint = + 添加动作，类型 = Variable，操作 = Set，选好你的变量，选择「真」。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Give them something to unlock
tutorial.remembering.giveThemSomethingUnlock.title = 给他们一样可解锁的东西

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Now something the variable can gate. Press + Wallpaper and set its Sprite to TutorialArt/Wallpapers/DummyWPP.png. Then press + Add condition and fill that row in: Type is Variable, Source is Pack, Variable is the one you named, and the value is True. That is the whole mechanism — one place writes the value, another asks what it says.
tutorial.remembering.giveThemSomethingUnlock.body = 现在做一样能被这个变量限制的东西。按 + 壁纸，把它的「精灵图」设为 TutorialArt/Wallpapers/DummyWPP.png。然后按 + 添加条件，把那一行填好：类型是 Variable，来源是 Pack，变量是你起名的那个，值为「真」。整套机制就是这样——一处写入这个值，另一处询问它说了什么。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: + Wallpaper, set Sprite, then + Add condition with Type = Variable.
tutorial.remembering.giveThemSomethingUnlock.hint = + 壁纸，设好「精灵图」，然后 + 添加条件，类型 = Variable。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: When one value is not enough
tutorial.remembering.whenOneValueNot.title = 当一个值不够用时

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Bool, Int, Float and String each hold ONE thing. A LIST holds any number of strings, in order, and it exists for the question the other four answer badly: which of these has the player already done?\n\nSix topics they can ask about is six Bools and six conditions with the other four. It is one List with the topics they have used in it, and the same single condition on every option.\n\nAdd one now: + Variable, Type set to List. Give it a name that reads as a plural, because that is what it holds — TopicsDiscussed rather than Topic.
tutorial.remembering.whenOneValueNot.body = Bool、Int、Float 和 String 各自只装「一样」东西。而 List 可以按顺序装任意多个字符串，它的存在正是为了那个另外四种答得很糟的问题：这些事里，玩家已经做过哪些？\n\n用另外四种，六个可问的话题就是六个 Bool 加六个条件。用 List，就是一个变量，里面装着他已经问过的话题，而每个选项上都是同一个条件。\n\n现在加一个：+ 变量，「类型」设为 List。给它起一个读起来是复数的名字，因为它装的就是一堆东西——用 TopicsDiscussed，而不是 Topic。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: + Variable, then set Type to List and rename it.
tutorial.remembering.whenOneValueNot.hint = + 变量，然后把「类型」设为 List 并改名。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Putting things in and asking what is there
tutorial.remembering.puttingThingsAskingThere.title = 往里放东西，以及问问里面有什么

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: A list has its own verbs, and they are the whole feature.\n\nTo CHANGE it, from a node action: Add to list appends a value, Remove from list takes the first matching one back out, and Clear list empties it. Adding the same value twice really does store it twice — nothing de-duplicates for you, so check before you add if that matters.\n\nTo ASK about it, from conditions: ListContains passes when a particular value is in there — negate it for "has not been used yet", which is the shape you want on a menu option. ListCount compares how many entries there are against a number, so equals zero means empty and negating that means something is in it.\n\nTwo more read it: Random from list picks one entry and writes it into another variable, and List count writes the size into an Int. Both are operations on the Variable action rather than actions of their own.\n\nAdd one to your list from a node now — an action on finish, Add to list, naming the list and a value.
tutorial.remembering.puttingThingsAskingThere.body = 列表有自己的一套动词，而它们就是这个功能的全部。\n\n要「改动」它，用节点动作：「添加到列表」在末尾追加一个值，「从列表移除」取走第一个匹配的，「清空列表」把它倒空。同一个值加两次是真的会存两份——没有谁替你去重，所以如果这很要紧，加之前先检查。\n\n要「询问」它，用条件：ListContains 在某个特定值在里面时通过——取反就是「还没用过」，这正是菜单选项上想要的形态。ListCount 把条目数量和一个数字作比较，所以等于零就是空，对它取反就是里面有东西。\n\n还有两个会读它：「Random from list」挑出一个条目并写进另一个变量，「List count」把数量写进一个 Int。这两个都是 Variable 动作下的操作，而不是独立的动作。\n\n现在就从某个节点往你的列表里加一个：一个结束时的动作，「添加到列表」，指明列表和一个值。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: + Add action on a node, Type = AddToList, then the list and a value.
tutorial.remembering.puttingThingsAskingThere.hint = 在某个节点上 + 添加动作，类型 = AddToList，然后填列表和一个值。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: The list you will actually use it for
tutorial.remembering.listWillActuallyUse.title = 你真正会用到它的那种列表

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: The pattern worth remembering, because it turns up constantly: a Choice whose options each add their own name to a list and gate themselves on NOT containing it.\n\nThe player sees the topics they have not asked about yet, the menu shrinks as they work through it, and when the last one goes the choice is empty — so keep one option outside the pattern that always shows and ends the conversation.\n\nWritten with Bools that is a variable and a condition per topic. Written with a list it is the same two rows on every option, and adding a seventh topic costs nothing.
tutorial.remembering.listWillActuallyUse.body = 这个套路值得记住，因为它会反复出现：一个「选择」，每个选项都把自己的名字加进一个列表，同时以「列表里还没有它」作为自己的条件。\n\n玩家看到的是他还没问过的话题，菜单随着他一个个问下去而缩短；当最后一个也没了，这个选择就空了——所以留一个不走这个套路的选项，始终显示，并用来结束对话。\n\n用 Bool 来写，那是每个话题一个变量加一个条件。用列表来写，每个选项上都是同样两行，而加第七个话题不花任何代价。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Watch it change something
tutorial.remembering.watchChangeSomething.title = 看着它改变些什么

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Export and go through. Check the wallpaper is locked, have the conversation that sets your variable, then check again. That before-and-after is the whole point of state, and it is the one thing no preview can show you.
tutorial.remembering.watchChangeSomething.body = 导出并走进去。先确认壁纸是锁着的，再把那段会给变量赋值的对话走一遍，然后再确认一次。这个前后对比正是「状态」的全部意义，也是任何预览都没法给你看的唯一一件事。

[Tutorial: Putting a value into a line]

# Tutorials: a tutorial's name
# en: Putting a value into a line
tutorial.valuesInText.title = 把值写进台词里

# Tutorials: the line under a tutorial's name
# en: Show a variable in dialogue, and use one in place of a typed-in value.
tutorial.valuesInText.summary = 在对话里显示一个变量，并用变量代替手动填写的值。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Four ways, and they are not interchangeable
tutorial.valuesInText.fourWaysTheyNot.title = 四种写法，而且不能互换

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: You have a variable. So far it can only gate things — a line plays or it does not. It can also be SHOWN, and used in place of values you would otherwise type.\n\nThere are four notations and each works in one place:\n\n[PV:name] puts one of YOUR variables into dialogue text or a button label. {PC}, {M}, {D} and {B} put the GAME's names into dialogue text. $name uses a variable's value in an action or condition box instead of a fixed one. {item} stands for the current value in a rule that repeats over a list.\n\nUsing the wrong one is quiet: the text simply shows the notation you typed, because nothing was there to replace it.
tutorial.valuesInText.fourWaysTheyNot.body = 你已经有了一个变量。到目前为止它只能充当门槛——一句台词播放，或者不播放。它还可以被「显示」出来，也可以用来代替你本来要手动填的值。\n\n一共有四种写法，各自只在一个地方起作用：\n\n[PV:name] 把「你的」某个变量放进对话文本或按钮标签里。{PC}、{M}、{D} 和 {B} 把「游戏的」名字放进对话文本里。$name 在动作或条件框里用变量的值代替固定值。{item} 在按列表重复的规则里代表当前的那个值。\n\n用错了是悄无声息的：文本会原样显示你写下的那串记号，因为没有任何东西可以替换它。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Show your variable in a line
tutorial.valuesInText.showVariableLine.title = 在台词里显示你的变量

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Go to your conversation and put [PV:name] into a node's text, with the name of the variable you declared — square brackets, PV, a colon, the name, and a closing bracket, spelled exactly.\n\nThe line will read the variable's value where the token sits. That is how a character says a number back to the player, or a name the player chose earlier in your own pack.
tutorial.valuesInText.showVariableLine.body = 打开你的对话，在某个节点的文本里写上 [PV:name]，把 name 换成你声明的那个变量名——方括号、PV、冒号、名字、右方括号，一个字符都不能差。\n\n这一行会在标记所在的位置读出变量的值。角色把一个数字回给玩家，或者说出玩家早先在你自己模组包里选定的一个名字，靠的就是这个。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Select a node and add [PV:yourVariable] to its Text.
tutorial.valuesInText.showVariableLine.hint = 选中一个节点，在它的「文本」里加上 [PV:yourVariable]。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: The names the player chose
tutorial.valuesInText.namesPlayerChose.title = 玩家自己选定的那些名字

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: The game has names of its own, and they are not yours to set. {PC} is the player's name. The other three stand for the family, one token each:\n\n{M} is what this player calls Anna. {D} is what they call Josef. {B} is what they call Adrian.\n\nEach player chose those words at the start, so they differ from save to save — which is the whole reason the tokens exist.\n\nSo write {M} rather than Mom, or Anna. A line that spells it out says that one word to everyone, including the player who chose something else, and they cannot tell your line from the game losing their choice. Validate warns about it if you forget.\n\nThose four are the ones known to work. What makes a braced word one of these is not documented, so treat any other as unsupported until you have watched it resolve in game.
tutorial.valuesInText.namesPlayerChose.body = 游戏有自己的一套名字，那不是由你来定的。{PC} 是玩家的名字。另外三个代表那一家人，各占一个标记：\n\n{M} 是这位玩家对 Anna 的称呼。{D} 是他对 Josef 的称呼。{B} 是他对 Adrian 的称呼。\n\n每位玩家在开局时选定了这些词，所以它们因存档而异——这正是这些标记存在的全部理由。\n\n所以请写 {M}，而不要写「妈妈」或者 Anna。把称呼写死的台词，会对所有人都说同一个词，包括那些选了别的称呼的玩家；而他们分不清这是你的台词，还是游戏把他们的选择弄丢了。你要是忘了，「验证」会提醒你。\n\n这四个是已知可用的。一个花括号里的词凭什么算作其中之一并没有文档说明，所以在你亲眼看到它在游戏里被替换之前，请把其他任何写法都当作不受支持。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: A value you do not know yet
tutorial.valuesInText.valueDoNotKnow.title = 一个你此刻还不知道的值

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: [PV:name] SHOWS a variable to the player. $name is the other direction: it hands the variable's value to an action or a condition, in place of the number or word you would otherwise type in. Nobody reads it — it never reaches the screen.\n\nWhy that matters: a typed-in value is decided now, while you are authoring. A $name value is decided later, while the game is running, and can be different every time.\n\nMake one and watch. Declare a String variable — call it LastPropShown — and leave its default empty. Then on some node, add TWO actions on finish. The first is a Variable action, Operation Set, writing the name of the prop you put in your room into LastPropShown. The second is Set-Active with its Target typed as $LastPropShown — one dollar sign, then the variable name, no brackets and no spaces.\n\nThe second action does not name the prop. It hides whatever the first one wrote down. Change what the first action writes — from a different node, or an integration rule — and the second acts on something else without being edited.\n\nThat is the shape it earns its keep in: one action that says "put back whatever was showing" instead of one action per object that might have been. It works in condition boxes too, so a gate can compare against a value chosen at runtime.\n\nOne trap: $$ is how you write a literal dollar sign, for the rare line where you want one.
tutorial.valuesInText.valueDoNotKnow.body = [PV:name] 是把变量「显示」给玩家看。$name 是反方向的：它把变量的值交给动作或条件，代替你本来要填的数字或词。没有人会读到它——它永远不会出现在屏幕上。\n\n这为什么重要：手填的值是现在、在你创作时定下的。$name 的值是之后、在游戏运行时定下的，而且每次都可能不同。\n\n做一个来看看。声明一个 String 变量——就叫 LastPropShown——默认值留空。然后在某个节点上加「两个」结束时的动作。第一个是 Variable 动作，操作为 Set，把你放进房间的那个道具的名字写进 LastPropShown。第二个是设为激活的动作，它的「目标」直接填 $LastPropShown——一个美元符号，紧接着变量名，不加括号也不加空格。\n\n第二个动作并没有点名那个道具。它隐藏的是第一个动作记下来的任何东西。改变第一个动作写入的内容——从另一个节点，或者从一条集成规则——第二个动作就会作用在别的东西上，而它自己一个字都不用改。\n\n这就是它真正体现价值的形态：用一个「把刚才显示着的那个放回去」的动作，取代为每一个可能显示过的对象各写一个动作。它在条件框里同样有效，所以门槛也可以拿运行时才决定的值来比较。\n\n一个坑：$$ 是书写字面美元符号的写法，留给那种偶尔真要写一个的台词。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Add an action on finish, then type $ and your variable name into a value box.
tutorial.valuesInText.valueDoNotKnow.hint = 加一个结束时的动作，然后在某个取值框里输入 $ 加上你的变量名。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: And one for lists
tutorial.valuesInText.oneLists.title = 还有一种是给列表用的

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: The fourth is for integration rules, which you will meet next. A rule can repeat over a list — every value in a List variable, or a few typed in — and {item} stands for whichever value it is running for at the time.\n\nThe name is yours: the box beside it sets the word, and item is only the default. One rule written once then does the work of six near-identical ones.
tutorial.valuesInText.oneLists.body = 第四种是给集成规则用的，你接下来就会遇到。规则可以在一个列表上重复运行——List 变量里的每个值，或者手写的几个值——而 {item} 代表它当次正在处理的那个值。\n\n名字由你定：旁边的框设定这个词，item 只是默认值。一条只写一遍的规则，就此顶得上六条几乎一模一样的规则。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Which one goes where
tutorial.valuesInText.whichOneGoesWhere.title = 哪一种用在哪里

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Worth keeping straight, because none of them complains when it is in the wrong place:\n\n[PV:name] — your variables, in text and button labels.\n{PC} {M} {D} {B} — the game's names, in text.\n$name — your variables, in action and condition value boxes.\n{item} — the current value, inside a repeating rule.\n\nA token in the wrong place is shown to the player exactly as you typed it. If a line reads [PV:gold] in game, that is this.
tutorial.valuesInText.whichOneGoesWhere.body = 这个值得分清楚，因为用错地方时它们谁都不会吭声：\n\n[PV:name]——你的变量，用在文本和按钮标签里。\n{PC} {M} {D} {B}——游戏的名字，用在文本里。\n$name——你的变量，用在动作和条件的取值框里。\n{item}——当前的值，用在重复运行的规则里。\n\n用错地方的标记会原样显示给玩家，你怎么写它就怎么显示。如果游戏里有一行写着 [PV:gold]，就是这个原因。

tutorial.rules.notEverythingWaitsSpoken.body = 集成规则会观察游戏并自行行动——挪动角色、重建列表、对日期变化作出反应。模组包正是从这里开始，不再只是一堆对话。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Scenes are the game's big pictures
tutorial.rules.scenesGameSBig.title = 场景就是游戏里的大图

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: A scene is a full-screen CG: the art, a frame around it, and a sound when it appears. Most of what players remember about this game is scenes, so they are worth understanding properly. A pack's scenes are built switched OFF — something has to turn one on, which is what you are about to make a rule do.
tutorial.rules.scenesGameSBig.body = 场景是一张全屏 CG：画面、围着它的边框，以及出现时的一个声音。玩家记得住的这款游戏的内容，大多是场景，所以值得好好弄懂。模组包里的场景建出来时是「关着」的——必须有东西去开启它，而这正是你马上要用一条规则来做的事。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: The frame and the sound
tutorial.rules.frameSound.title = 边框和声音

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: A new scene already has a Vanilla frame — one of the game's own borders, so your CG sits in the picture the way the rest do. Custom frame takes your own art instead. Mode decides what plays when the scene appears: the prototype carries its own audio trigger, and picking kiss or flash swaps that for one of the game's signals, while Silent strips it. Change them if you like — nothing here has to be touched.
tutorial.rules.frameSound.body = 新建的场景已经带有一个游戏边框——游戏自己的边框之一，让你的 CG 像其他图一样嵌在画框里。「自定义边框」则改用你自己的素材。「模式」决定场景出现时播放什么：原型自带音频触发，选「亲吻」或「闪光」会把它换成游戏的某个信号，而「静音」则把它去掉。想改就改——这里没有非动不可的东西。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Sound comes from two other tabs
tutorial.rules.soundComesTwoOther.title = 声音来自另外两个页签

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Music holds background tracks, and an action switches between them. SFX holds one-shot effects, played by an action the same way. Both take an OGG, WAV or MP3 from your pack folder, and both have a Play button so you can hear one without starting the game.
tutorial.rules.soundComesTwoOther.body = 「音乐」页签放背景曲目，由动作在它们之间切换。「音效」页签放一次性的效果，同样由动作来播放。两者都接受模组包文件夹里的 OGG、WAV 或 MP3，也都有一个「播放」按钮，让你不启动游戏就能听。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: The trick worth knowing about SFX
tutorial.rules.trickWorthKnowingAbout.title = 关于音效值得知道的窍门

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: An effect does not need an action at all. Auto-trigger patterns is a list of words that fire it on their own whenever one of them shows up in a line, written between asterisks by convention, like *plap*. Drop extra recordings beside the first one named _1, _2 and so on and the game picks between them at random, so a sound used often does not wear thin. Neither is discoverable from the tab, which is the only reason this step exists.
tutorial.rules.trickWorthKnowingAbout.body = 音效根本不需要动作。「自动触发模式」是一串词，只要其中之一出现在某行台词里，它们就会自行触发；按惯例写在星号之间，比如 *plap*。把额外的录音丢在第一个旁边，命名为 _1、_2 等等，游戏就会在它们之间随机挑选，这样常用的声音就不会听腻。这两件事从页签上都看不出来，这也是本步骤存在的唯一理由。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Add a rule
tutorial.rules.addRule.title = 添加一条规则

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Use + Rule. Give it a description while you are there — in six months the conditions will still be readable and the reason will not.
tutorial.rules.addRule.body = 使用 + 规则。顺手给它写一句说明——半年后条件仍然读得懂，而当初为什么要写它就未必了。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: + Rule, in the toolbar above the list.
tutorial.rules.addRule.hint = + 规则，在列表上方的工具栏里。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Decide when it fires
tutorial.rules.decideWhenFires.title = 决定它何时触发

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: OnRisingEdge fires once when the conditions start passing, which is what you want almost every time. WhilePassing fires every frame they hold — reach for it only to keep something continuously in step.
tutorial.rules.decideWhenFires.body = OnRisingEdge 在条件开始成立时触发一次，这几乎总是你想要的。WhilePassing 在条件成立的每一帧都触发——只有当你要让某样东西持续保持同步时才用它。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Give it something to watch
tutorial.rules.giveSomethingWatch.title = 给它一样可看的东西

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Under Conditions, press + Add condition and gate it on the variable you made in the last tutorial. A rule with no conditions has nothing to wait for.
tutorial.rules.giveSomethingWatch.body = 在「条件」下按 + 添加条件，让它取决于你上一篇教程里做的那个变量。没有条件的规则，没有什么可等的。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: + Add condition, in the Conditions box above the Actions one.
tutorial.rules.giveSomethingWatch.hint = + 添加条件，在「动作」框上方的「条件」框里。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: And something to do
tutorial.rules.somethingDo.title = 再给它一件可做的事

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Under Actions, press + Add action. Set Type to Set active, Category to Scene, and Target to the scene you built, leaving Active ticked. That is what finally puts it on screen: a scene is built switched off, and this is the switch.
tutorial.rules.somethingDo.body = 在「动作」下按 + 添加动作。把「类型」设为 Set active，「类别」设为 Scene，「目标」设为你搭好的那个场景，「激活」保持勾选。正是这一步才终于把它放上屏幕：场景建出来时是关着的，而这就是那个开关。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: + Add action, Type = Set active, Category = Scene, pick your scene.
tutorial.rules.somethingDo.hint = + 添加动作，类型 = Set active，类别 = Scene，选你的场景。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Watch it think
tutorial.rules.watchThink.title = 看它怎么想

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Tick Set for condition debugging and the game logs what this rule decided, whenever that changes. It is the fastest way to find out why a rule did nothing — and worth clearing again before you share the pack.
tutorial.rules.watchThink.body = 勾选「勾选以调试条件」，每当这条规则的判定发生变化时，游戏就会把它记下来。这是查清一条规则为什么什么都没做的最快办法——而且在分享模组包之前值得再取消勾选。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: That is the shape of it
tutorial.rules.shape.title = 大致就是这么个结构

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Characters, places, conversations, art, memory and rules — and one door from the bedroom to go and stand in all of it. Three things these seven never made you build: Map Buttons, which put a place on the world map rather than on another room's strip; Music; and SFX. All three work like everything you have already done, and the Documentation section on this tab covers them field by field — start with Start here if any of it stopped making sense.
tutorial.rules.shape.body = 角色、地点、对话、美术、记忆和规则——再加上一扇从卧室出发、让你能走进这一切当中的门。有三样东西是这七篇教程从没让你做过的：地图按钮，它把地点放上世界地图而不是另一个房间的导航条；音乐；以及音效。这三样的用法都和你已经做过的一样，本页签的「文档」一节会逐个字段讲解——如果哪里读着读着不明白了，就从「从这里开始」看起。

[Tutorial: A quest in the journal]

# Tutorials: a tutorial's name
# en: A quest in the journal
tutorial.aQuest.title = 日志里的一个任务

# Tutorials: the line under a tutorial's name
# en: Make a quest that starts, moves along and finishes by itself.
tutorial.aQuest.summary = 做一个会自己开始、自己推进、自己完成的任务。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: The journal is the game's
tutorial.aQuest.journalGameS.title = 日志是游戏的

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: The game's quest screen lists the game's own quests. A quest made here goes into that same list, beside them, with the same popups when it starts and when it is finished.\n\nEverything about it is on this tab: what it is called, when it starts, its steps, and what finishes each one.
tutorial.aQuest.journalGameS.body = 游戏的任务界面列出的是游戏自有的任务。在这里做的任务会进到同一份列表里，与它们并列，开始和完成时也有同样的弹窗。\n\n关于它的一切都在这个页签上：它叫什么、何时开始、有哪些步骤，以及每一步由什么来完成。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Start one
tutorial.aQuest.startOne.title = 开一个头

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Press + Quest. It arrives with a title waiting to be replaced.
tutorial.aQuest.startOne.body = 按 + 任务。它出现时带着一个等你替换掉的标题。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: + Quest, at the top of the list on the left.
tutorial.aQuest.startOne.hint = 左侧列表顶部的 + 任务。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Call it something
tutorial.aQuest.callSomething.title = 给它起个名字

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Give it a title. The game writes its own as a name and then the person it is about in brackets - "Secrets (Adrian)" - and the journal paints the bracketed part pink by itself. Yours can follow that or not.\n\nThe runtime name under it follows the title while the quest is new. The game files a player's progress under it, which the last step comes back to.
tutorial.aQuest.callSomething.body = 给它一个标题。游戏自己的写法是先写名称，再把相关的人名放在括号里——「Secrets (Adrian)」——而日志会自行把括号里的部分涂成粉色。你的可以照办，也可以不照办。\n\n下面的运行时名称在任务还新的时候跟随标题。游戏按它来归档玩家的进度，最后一步还会回到这件事。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Title, in the Quest box on the right.
tutorial.aQuest.callSomething.hint = 右侧「任务」框里的「标题」。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Say when it starts
tutorial.aQuest.sayWhenStarts.title = 说明它何时开始

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Add a start condition - any of the conditions a dialogue or a rule can use. The quest starts by itself the moment all of them pass: a variable reaching a value, a day going by, the player being somewhere.\n\nAn empty list is the one place where empty does not mean 'always'. With no start conditions a quest waits for a Quest action to start it, so a quest you have only begun writing never turns up in a player's journal.
tutorial.aQuest.sayWhenStarts.body = 加一个开始条件——对话或规则能用的任何条件都行。所有条件通过的那一刻，任务就自行开始：某个变量达到某个值、过了一天、玩家身处某处。\n\n空列表是唯一一处「空」不等于「始终」的地方。没有开始条件时，任务会等着某个任务动作来开始它，所以一个你才刚动笔写的任务，绝不会冒到玩家的日志里去。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: + Add condition, in the Starts when box.
tutorial.aQuest.sayWhenStarts.hint = 「开始于」框里的 + 添加条件。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Give it steps
tutorial.aQuest.giveSteps.title = 给它几个步骤

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Add two tasks and write a line for each - the lines the player reads under the quest.\n\nTasks run top to bottom. Starting the quest starts the first one, finishing one starts the next, and finishing the last completes the quest. The journal shows each one as it starts.
tutorial.aQuest.giveSteps.body = 添加两个步骤，各写一行字——就是玩家在任务下面读到的那些行。\n\n步骤自上而下依次进行。开始任务会开始第一个，完成一个会开始下一个，完成最后一个则完成整个任务。日志会在每一步开始时把它显示出来。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: + Task twice, then select each one and fill in its Text.
tutorial.aQuest.giveSteps.hint = 按两次 + 步骤，然后逐个选中并填写它的「文本」。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Say what finishes a step
tutorial.aQuest.sayFinishesStep.title = 说明什么算完成一步

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Select a task and add a condition under Completes when. While the task is in progress, the moment its conditions all pass it is done and the next one starts.\n\nWhen it completes is the other half: actions that run as the task finishes - set a variable, show something, start another quest. They run however the task was finished, so they are the place for anything that should always follow it.
tutorial.aQuest.sayFinishesStep.body = 选中一个步骤，在「完成条件」下加一个条件。在这一步进行期间，它的条件全部通过的那一刻它就算完成，下一步随即开始。\n\n「完成时」是另一半：步骤结束时运行的动作——设定变量、显示某样东西、开始另一个任务。无论这一步是怎么结束的它们都会运行，所以凡是应当总是随之发生的事，都放在这里。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Select a task, then + Add condition under Completes when.
tutorial.aQuest.sayFinishesStep.hint = 选中一个步骤，然后在「完成条件」下 + 添加条件。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Steps inside steps
tutorial.aQuest.stepsInsideSteps.title = 步骤里的步骤

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: A task can have subtasks. The journal draws them under it with a small arrow, and never with a tick - even the game's finished subtasks look the same as unfinished ones. Only a top-level task gets the check mark.\n\nOnce a task has subtasks it finishes through them - in order, in any order, or when any one is done, as its Completes box says - so its own conditions and actions go away and its subtasks get them instead. Set it to 'by action' and its subtasks never start at all: they become notes under it, which is how the game gives hints.\n\nA task can also count - three flowers found, five photos taken. Quest actions can set the count, or it can follow a number variable you already keep. Either way it finishes the task when it reaches the target, and only shows beside a top-level task.
tutorial.aQuest.stepsInsideSteps.body = 一个步骤可以有子步骤。日志会带着一个小箭头把它们画在它下面，而且从不打勾——连游戏自己已完成的子步骤，看起来也和没完成的一样。只有顶层步骤才会得到那个对勾。\n\n一旦某个步骤有了子步骤，它就通过子步骤来完成——按顺序、任意顺序，或任选其一完成，具体看它的「完成方式」框——于是它自己的条件和动作就消失了，改由它的子步骤来承担。把它设成「由动作决定」，它的子步骤就永远不会开始：它们变成它下面的注解，游戏正是用这种方式给提示的。\n\n步骤还可以计数——找到三朵花、拍下五张照片。任务动作可以设定这个计数，它也可以跟随你本来就在维护的某个数字变量。无论哪种，计数达到目标就完成这一步，而且只在顶层步骤旁边显示。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: From a conversation, too
tutorial.aQuest.conversationToo.title = 从对话里也行

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: A dialogue node, a rule or a UI button can move a quest as well, with the Quest action: start it, complete the step the player is on, add to a count. The QuestState and QuestCounter conditions ask where a player has got to, so a conversation can wait until a quest is in progress. Both can name the game's own quests too.\n\nThe game only finishes a step that is in progress, so completing the second task before the first is done does nothing at all.
tutorial.aQuest.conversationToo.body = 对话节点、规则或界面按钮同样可以推动任务，靠的是任务动作：开始它、完成玩家当前所在的那一步、给计数加数。QuestState 和 QuestCounter 条件则询问玩家进行到了哪里，于是一段对话可以一直等到某个任务进行中才播放。这两者也都可以点名游戏自有的任务。\n\n游戏只会结束进行中的步骤，所以在第一步还没完成时去完成第二步，什么都不会发生。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Before you release it
tutorial.aQuest.beforeRelease.title = 发布之前

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: The game saves a player's progress on a quest under its runtime name, and on a task under the task's. Renaming either once players have your pack starts that quest over for every one of them, because to the game it is a different quest.\n\nRename freely while you are writing. After release, leave the names alone and change the title and text instead - those are only what the journal shows.
tutorial.aQuest.beforeRelease.body = 游戏按任务的运行时名称保存玩家在这个任务上的进度，按步骤的运行时名称保存步骤的进度。等玩家已经装上你的模组包之后再改其中任何一个名字，那个任务就会对所有人从头再来，因为在游戏看来那是另一个任务。\n\n写作期间尽管改名。发布之后，请让这些名字保持不动，改标题和文本就好——那些只是日志显示的内容。

[Tutorial: A screen of your own]

# Tutorials: a tutorial's name

# Tutorials: the heading of a step

tutorial.aScreenOfYourOwn.screensPackOwns.title = 归模组包所有的界面

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: This tab is for anything drawn on top of the game rather than in it — a window, a panel, a bar in the corner. Two kinds live here together, and deliberately so: screens you invent, and screens the game already has that your pack changes.\n\nThey share a tab because from your side they are the same job. A tree of objects on the left, what the selected one is underneath, and a picture of the result on the right.
tutorial.aScreenOfYourOwn.screensPackOwns.body = 这个页签管的是画在游戏「之上」而不是画在游戏「之中」的一切——一扇窗、一块面板、角落里的一条栏。这里有意地并存着两类：你发明的界面，以及游戏本来就有、而你的模组包去改动的界面。\n\n它们共用一个页签，是因为从你这边看它们是同一件活。左边是对象树，下面是选中对象的具体内容，右边是结果的图。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: Press + UI. That gives you a blank panel — the plainest shape there is, and the one to start from when you do not yet know what you are making. The arrow beside the button offers the others: a window with a title bar and a close button, a dialog that asks something, a list, a tooltip.\n\nIt arrives with a name and an id already filled in. The id is what everything else in the pack uses to point at this screen, so it stays put even when you rename the thing.
tutorial.aScreenOfYourOwn.startOne.body = 按 + UI。这会给你一块空白面板——最朴素的形态，也是你还不知道自己要做什么时的起点。按钮旁边的箭头提供其他几种：带标题栏和关闭按钮的窗口、用来发问的对话框、列表、提示框。\n\n它出现时名称和 id 都已经填好了。id 是模组包里其他一切用来指向这个界面的东西，所以即便你改了名字，它也纹丝不动。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: + UI, at the top of the list on the left.
tutorial.aScreenOfYourOwn.startOne.hint = 左侧列表顶部的 + UI。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step

tutorial.aScreenOfYourOwn.callSomething.body = 把「名称」改成一个月后你还认得出来的东西——用「任务便条」，而不是「空白面板 2」。每当有什么东西需要打开它时，你都要从列表里挑这个名字。\n\n下面的 id 不会跟着变，也不应该变：给界面改名，绝不能悄无声息地把原本打开它的那些东西脱钩。

Select the panel and press + Object to put an empty object inside it; the arrow beside that button offers ready-made pieces instead — a button, a row, a line of text.\n\nEverything is inside something else, and that is what decides where it sits: an object is positioned against its parent, so moving the panel takes everything on it along.
tutorial.aScreenOfYourOwn.putSomething.body = 这棵树就是界面的构成。选中面板，按 + 对象，就能在它里面放一个空对象；那个按钮旁边的箭头则提供现成的部件——一个按钮、一行、一行文字。\n\n一切都装在别的东西里面，而这决定了它的位置：对象是相对于它的父级来定位的，所以挪动面板会把它上面的一切一起带走。

# en: Select the panel in the tree first — + Object adds INSIDE whatever is selected.
tutorial.aScreenOfYourOwn.putSomething.hint = 先在树里选中面板——+ 对象是添加到当前选中项「里面」的。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Say what it is
tutorial.aScreenOfYourOwn.say.title = 说明它是什么

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: With the new object selected, the box underneath is everything about it: its name, where it sits, how big it is, and what it draws.\n\nPosition is measured from the object's anchors, in canvas pixels, and the canvas is the whole screen. Give it a name and a size you can see — a few hundred pixels each way is a good start, since anything smaller is hard to find in the preview.
tutorial.aScreenOfYourOwn.say.body = 选中新对象后，下面那个框就是关于它的一切：名称、位置、大小，以及它画什么。\n\n位置是从对象的锚点量起的，单位是画布像素，而画布就是整个屏幕。给它起个名字，并给一个看得见的尺寸——每边几百像素是个不错的开始，因为再小就很难在预览里找到了。

# Tutorials: the nudge shown when a step has been waiting a while
# en: Select the object you just added, then fill in Name and Size.
tutorial.aScreenOfYourOwn.say.hint = 选中你刚添加的那个对象，然后填写「名称」和「尺寸」。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: What the picture is telling you
tutorial.aScreenOfYourOwn.pictureTelling.title = 那张图在告诉你什么

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: The preview draws your screen against the game's own canvas at the size the game uses, with the selected object picked out. It is not an approximation of the layout — it is the layout, which is why an object off the edge here is off the edge in the game.\n\nA screen built on one of the game's own shows that screen underneath your changes, so you can see what you are altering rather than guessing at it.
tutorial.aScreenOfYourOwn.pictureTelling.body = 预览会按游戏使用的尺寸，把你的界面画在游戏自己的画布上，并把选中的对象标出来。它不是对布局的近似——它「就是」布局，所以在这里跑出边界的对象，在游戏里同样跑出边界。\n\n建立在游戏自有界面之上的界面，会把那个界面显示在你的改动之下，让你看得到自己在改什么，而不是全靠猜。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Making it appear
tutorial.aScreenOfYourOwn.makingAppear.title = 让它出现

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: A screen you build starts switched off, the same way a scene does. Something has to turn it on, and it is the action you already know: Set Active, with its category set to UI and your screen named as the target.\n\nPut that on a dialogue node, or in a rule, or on a button of your own — anywhere an action can go. Switching it off again is the same action with Active unticked.
tutorial.aScreenOfYourOwn.makingAppear.body = 你搭出来的界面一开始是关着的，和场景一样。必须有东西去开启它，而那正是你已经熟悉的那个动作：设为激活，类别设为 UI，目标指名你的界面。\n\n把它放进对话节点、规则，或你自己的按钮里——凡是动作能待的地方都行。要再把它关掉，就是同一个动作，只是不勾选「激活」。

# Tutorials: the heading of a step
# en: Changing one the game already has
tutorial.aScreenOfYourOwn.changingOneGameAlready.title = 改动游戏已有的界面

# Tutorials: what the step says
# en: + Vanilla is the other half of this tab. Choose one of the game's screens and the tree fills with what is actually in it, ready to be moved, hidden, relabelled or added to.\n\nOnly your differences are stored. An object you never touch is not in your pack at all, which is why Reset on an object makes it stop being a change and drop out — and why a pack that alters one label on the shop stays a pack that alters one label, rather than a copy of the shop that will not survive the game being patched.
tutorial.aScreenOfYourOwn.changingOneGameAlready.body = + 游戏界面是这个页签的另一半。选中游戏的某个界面，树里就会填满它里面实际的内容，随时可以移动、隐藏、改写标签或往里添加。\n\n只有你做出的差异会被保存。你从没动过的对象根本不在你的模组包里——这也是为什么在某个对象上按「还原」会让它不再算作一处改动、并从树里消失，以及为什么一个只改了商店一处标签的模组包，始终只是一个改了一处标签的模组包，而不是一份熬不过游戏更新的商店副本。

[Validation]

# Validation message; {word} is a word like mom, {token} the code that says it the player's way
# en: This line writes "{word}". Players choose what they call their family, so {token} says it back the way they picked it — writing the word says it to everyone, including whoever chose something else.
validation.dialogue.kinWord = 这一行写了「{word}」。玩家会自己决定怎么称呼家人，所以 {token} 会按他们选定的说法把它读出来——把这个词写死，就是对所有人都这么说，包括那些选了别的说法的玩家。

# A text typed only in a translation: the pack has no words of its own for it. {own} is the pack's language, {languages} the translations that have it, {first} the one other players get.
# en: Has no text in the default language ({own}), only in {languages}. A player reading any other language gets the {first} text - write its default text.
validation.text.onlyInTranslation = 在默认语言（{own}）中没有文字，只有{languages}里有。使用其他任何语言的玩家会看到{first}的文字——请为它写上默认文字。

# A line that does not fit the game's dialogue box even at the smallest size it shrinks to. {language} is the language of the words measured.
# en: Too long for the game's dialogue box in {language}: even at its smallest size it runs out of the box and over whatever is below it. Split it into two lines.
validation.dialogue.lineTooLong = 在{language}中对游戏的对话框来说太长：即使缩到最小也会超出对话框，盖住下面的内容。请把它拆成两句台词。

# A default text that plainly reads as another language. {language} is that language's own name, {own} the pack's.
# en: Looks like {language}, but the pack says it is written in {own} - so every player without a translation into their language reads it like this. If you meant to translate it, switch to that language in the list at the top of the window and type it there. If the whole pack is in {language}, change Written in on the ModForge tab.
validation.text.looksLike = 看起来是{language}，但模组包声明它是用{own}编写的——所以没有自己语言译本的玩家都会读到这样的文字。如果你本想翻译它，请在窗口顶部的列表中切换到那个语言，再在那里输入。如果整个模组包都是{language}，请在 ModForge 选项卡中更改“编写语言”。

# en: Is not in the alphabet {own} is written in, but the pack says it is written in {own} - so every player without a translation into their language reads it like this. If you meant to translate it, switch to that language in the list at the top of the window and type it there. If the whole pack is in another language, change Written in on the ModForge tab.
validation.text.notOwnLanguage = 不是用{own}所用的文字书写的，但模组包声明它是用{own}编写的——所以没有自己语言译本的玩家都会读到这样的文字。如果你本想翻译它，请在窗口顶部的列表中切换到那个语言，再在那里输入。如果整个模组包是另一种语言，请在 ModForge 选项卡中更改“编写语言”。

# Most of the pack reads as another language than the one it says it is written in.
# en: Most of this pack looks like {language}, but it says it is written in {own}. Change Written in on the ModForge tab to the language it is written in: translations are made from it, and players without a translation are given it.
validation.pack.looksLike = 这个模组包的大部分内容看起来是{language}，但它声明自己是用{own}编写的。请在 ModForge 选项卡中把“编写语言”改为它实际使用的语言：译本由它翻译而来，没有译本的玩家读到的也是它。

# en: Most of this pack is not in the alphabet {own} is written in, but it says it is written in {own}. Change Written in on the ModForge tab to the language it is written in: translations are made from it, and players without a translation are given it.
validation.pack.notOwnLanguage = 这个模组包的大部分内容不是用{own}所用的文字书写的，但它声明自己是用{own}编写的。请在 ModForge 选项卡中把“编写语言”改为它实际使用的语言：译本由它翻译而来，没有译本的玩家读到的也是它。

# Validation message
# en: This node is one of a Choice's options and has {count} actions on start. When an option counts as STARTED is not something the pack can rely on — it may be when the menu is drawn rather than when the player picks it, in which case these run for options nobody chose. Move them to Actions on finish, which happens once the option has been taken.
validation.dialogue.choiceOptionOnStart.other = 这个节点是某个「选择」的一个选项，并带有 {count} 个开始时的动作。一个选项什么时候算「已开始」，并不是模组包可以依赖的事——它可能是在菜单绘制时就算开始，而不是玩家选中时，那样这些动作就会为没人选的选项运行。请把它们移到「结束时的动作」里，那是在选项被选中之后才发生的。

# Validation message
# en: This node is a Choice with no children, so it is a menu offering nothing to pick. If it is meant to be a line, set Kind to Text. Older packs collect these on their own: adding a node with + Child or + Sibling used to copy the kind of the node it came from, so every option under a Choice was saved as a Choice as well.
validation.dialogue.choiceWithoutOptions = 这个节点是一个没有子节点的「选择」，也就是一个没有任何可选项的菜单。如果它本该是一句台词，请把「类型」设为「文本」。旧版模组包会自行积攒这类节点：过去用 + 子节点或 + 同级节点添加节点时会照抄来源节点的类型，于是「选择」下面的每个选项也都被存成了「选择」。

# Validation message; {name} is a variable's name
# en: Text uses [PV:{name}], but no variable called '{name}' is declared in this pack. An undeclared name reads as an empty string, so the token disappears from the line rather than showing up as a mistake.
validation.text.unknownPackVar = 文本里用了 [PV:{name}]，但这个模组包里并没有声明名为「{name}」的变量。未声明的名字会被读作空字符串，所以这个标记会从台词里消失，而不是作为错误显示出来。

# Validation message
# en: Text has a [PV: that never closes with a ]. The token only counts when it ends in a square bracket, so this one is shown to the player exactly as it was typed.
validation.text.malformedToken = 文本里有一个 [PV: 始终没有用 ] 收尾。标记只有以右方括号结束才算数，所以这一个会原样显示给玩家。

# Validation message; {name} is what the action names
# en: '{name}' is not an effect in this pack. A runtime name follows its display name, so renaming one leaves references behind.
validation.action.unknownSfx = 「{name}」不是这个模组包里的音效。运行时名称跟随显示名称，所以改了名字就会留下失效的引用。

# Validation message
# en: 'Random' re-rolls on every evaluation, and this condition is re-checked every frame (~60×/sec), so its chance doesn't mean what it says. Replace with 'DailyChance' (rolls once per in-game day), or a LevelRandom variable + a numeric comparison (once per visit). 'Random' is fine on a dialogue NODE's conditions or a level hook, which are evaluated once.
validation.condition.randomPerFrame = 「Random」每次求值都重新掷一次，而这个条件每一帧都会重新检查（约每秒 60 次），所以它的概率并不是字面上的意思。请改用「DailyChance」（游戏中每天掷一次），或者用一个 LevelRandom 变量加一个数值比较（每次进入掷一次）。「Random」用在对话「节点」自身的条件或关卡挂钩上没问题，那些只求值一次。

validation.condition.chanceRange = 参数「chance」应当是 [0,1] 之间的小数

# Validation message
# en: Param 'chance' should be a whole percentage in [0,100]
validation.condition.dailyChanceRange = 参数「chance」应当是 [0,100] 之间的整数百分比

# Title of the message box that offers to translate before an export or a publish
# en: Translate before exporting?
exportTranslate.title = 导出前翻译吗？

# The offer; {languages} is a list, one per line, of the line below
# en: Some of what players read in {pack} is not in every language ModForge has:\n\n{languages}\n\nTranslate it by machine first? Only what is still untranslated is sent. A line already translated, by you or by a machine, stays exactly as it is, and so does one marked as the same in that language. The text goes to Google's free translation service.\n\nYes opens the Translate window with these languages ticked: change them if you like, then press Translate. The export goes ahead when you close the window. No exports it as it is. Cancel does neither.\n\nOptions ▸ Offer to translate before exporting turns this off for this pack.
exportTranslate.ask = {pack} 中玩家读到的内容，有一部分还没有覆盖 ModForge 的所有语言：\n\n{languages}\n\n先用机器翻译吗？只发送仍未翻译的内容。已经翻译好的行——无论是你写的还是机器写的——都原样保留，在该语言中标记为相同的行也一样。文字会发送到 Google 的免费翻译服务。\n\n「是」会打开翻译窗口，并勾选这些语言：可以按需修改，然后点击「翻译」。关闭窗口后，导出会继续。「否」按原样导出。「取消」两者都不做。\n\n「选项 ▸ 导出前提议翻译」可以为本模组包关闭这个提问。

# One language in that list
# en: {language}: {missing} of {total} still to translate
exportTranslate.language = {language}：{total} 条里还有 {missing} 条要翻译

# A note on the Quests tab
# en: Once the quest has started - in progress, completed or failed - it goes back to not started the moment all of these pass, so it can be done again. Its tasks and counts start over, and its start conditions wait until these stop passing.
quests.resetNote.own = 任务一旦开始过——进行中、已完成或已失败——只要这些条件全部通过，它就会回到「未开始」，从而可以再做一次。它的步骤和计数会重新开始，而它的开始条件会一直等到这些条件不再通过。

# The Quests tab: the panel for what happens to players who had finished a quest when a new version of the pack gives it more tasks
# en: Tasks added after players finished it
quests.stepsAdded = 玩家完成后新增的目标

# Under that panel's heading
# en: What happens to a player who had already finished this quest when a new version of your pack gives it more tasks.
quests.stepsAdded.intro = 当你的模组包的新版本给这个任务加了更多目标时，已经完成它的玩家会怎样。

# The first choice: nothing changes. The default
# en: Leave it finished
quests.stepsAdded.leave = 保持已完成

# Under the first choice
# en: It stays finished, and they don't see the new tasks. The safest choice.
quests.stepsAdded.leave.note = 任务仍然是已完成，玩家看不到新目标。最稳妥的选择。

# The second choice: the quest is in progress again at the new tasks
# en: Reopen at the new steps
quests.stepsAdded.reopen = 在新步骤处重新开启

# Under the second choice
# en: The quest is in progress again, with every task they did still done and only the new ones left. Only when all the new tasks come after the ones the quest had, and they had done every one of those; otherwise it stays finished, so nobody is stuck behind a task they can't reach.
quests.stepsAdded.reopen.note = 任务重新变为进行中，玩家做过的目标仍然算完成，只剩新目标要做。只有当所有新目标都排在任务原有目标之后、且玩家把原有目标全部做完时才会这样；否则任务保持已完成，免得有人被卡在一个到不了的目标后面。

# The third choice: back to not started
# en: Start it over
quests.stepsAdded.startOver = 从头开始

# Under the third choice
# en: Their progress on it is cleared and it goes back to not started, so it starts again the way it always starts. Each task's actions run again as it is done.
quests.stepsAdded.startOver.note = 玩家在这个任务上的进度会被清除，任务回到「未开始」，然后按它平时的方式重新开始。每个目标的动作会在完成时再执行一次。

quests.noOwnCompletion = 这个步骤有子步骤，所以它通过子步骤来完成，自身没有完成条件和动作——请把那些交给它的子步骤。

# en: The editor will still be updated, but the plugin in your game folder could not be replaced:\n\n{problem}\n\nThe usual reason is something holding the file open — the game, or a build that put it there. Close it and run Options ▸ Check for updates now again, or unpack the plugin zip over your game folder by hand. Until then the two are out of step, and a pack written against one can fail against the other.
update.pluginFailed = 编辑器仍会更新，但你游戏文件夹里的插件无法被替换：\n\n{problem}\n\n通常的原因是有东西占用着这个文件——游戏本身，或者把它放到那里的某次构建。关掉它，再运行一次「选项 ▸ 立即检查更新」，或者手动把插件 zip 解压覆盖到你的游戏文件夹。在此之前两者版本不一致，针对其中之一写的模组包在另一个上可能会出问题。

# en: Restarting to finish {version}…
update.restarting = 正在重启以完成 {version} 的更新…

# Checking a translation file: of the texts above, the ones left word for word

# en: Line {line} is not a note, a [heading] or a key = text line: {found}

# en: Line {line}: '{key}' is not a key ModForge has, so nothing shows it.

# Asked before writing a translation file. \n starts a new line
# en: The code of the language you want to write, as Windows names it:\n\nes — Español\npt-BR — Português (Brasil)\nzh-Hans — 中文 (简体)\nja — 日本語\nko — 한국어\nru — Русский\nde — Deutsch\nfr — Français\n\nAny code Windows knows works, not only these — it — Italiano and pl — Polski just as well.\n\nThe file is written into your languages folder and opened for you. Every text in it starts as the English, or as the translation that came with the editor when there is one for that code, ready to be written over.
language.new.prompt = 你要编写的语言的代码，按 Windows 的命名：\n\nes — Español\npt-BR — Português (Brasil)\nzh-Hans — 中文 (简体)\nja — 日本語\nko — 한국어\nru — Русский\nde — Deutsch\nfr — Français\n\n不止这几个，Windows 认识的任何代码都可以——it — Italiano 和 pl — Polski 同样有效。\n\n文件会写进你的语言文件夹并为你打开。里面每条文本一开始都是英文；若该代码有随编辑器附带的译文，则以那份译文为起点，随时可以覆盖改写。

# Listed under the prompt above when the editor already has translations;
# {codes} is a list like "es (Español) and zh-Hans (中文)"
# en: You already have {codes}. Typing one of those brings that file up to date with this version instead of starting a new one, and keeps every word already written in it.
language.new.have = 你已经有 {codes}。输入其中之一会把那个文件补齐到这个版本，而不是新建一份，并保留里面已经写好的每一个字。

# en: Windows knows no language called '{code}'. Use a code like es, pt-BR, zh-Hans, ja or ru.

# The notes at the top of a pack's translation file. Each \n starts the next note; the \\n before 'starts a new line' is shown as \n, the way the file writes a line break
# en: Write the text in {language} after each =, and leave the key before it as it is.\nThe "# en:" line above each text is what the pack says now, whatever language that is in. The game uses a translation only while that is still what the pack says: when the pack changes a text, its old translation waits until somebody brings it up to date.\nKeep {words in braces}, <tags> and [TOKENS:like this] exactly as they are: the game fills them in or reads them.\n\\n starts a new line. A line with nothing after the = shows the default text.\nTo have an AI tool translate it, give it the whole file and ask it to translate only the text after each =, and to change nothing else: not the keys, not the notes, not the codes.\nIn ModForge, File > Pack translations > Check the pack's translations says what is missing or damaged.
packText.file.help = 请在每个 = 之后写上{language}的译文，= 前面的键保持原样。\n每条文本上方的 "# en:" 行是模组包当前的说法，无论它是什么语言。只有当模组包仍然这么说时，游戏才会使用对应的译文：模组包一旦改动某条文本，它的旧译文就会一直等到有人把它补齐。\n请把 {words in braces}、<tags> 和 [TOKENS:like this] 原样保留：游戏会填充或读取它们。\n\\n 表示另起一行。= 后面什么都不写的行，会显示默认文字。\n若要让 AI 工具来翻译，请把整个文件交给它，并要求它只翻译每个 = 之后的文本，别的一概不要改：键不要改，注释不要改，代码标记也不要改。\n在 ModForge 里，「文件 > 模组包翻译 > 检查模组包的翻译」会告诉你缺了什么、坏了什么。

[Pack translations: translating by machine]

# Said against one line that came back damaged. The line is not written and the
# pack's own words are kept, so these explain why a line was left alone.
# en: The translation came back without {codes}.
packText.mt.lostCodes = 翻译回来时少了 {codes}。

# The braces, tags and tokens are named without writing any of them out, since
# writing one here would make it a gap in this text rather than words about one.
# en: The translation does not carry the same words in braces, tags and tokens as the original.
packText.mt.differentCodes = 译文没有保留原文里同样的花括号词语、标签和记号。

# en: The translation came back empty.
packText.mt.empty = 翻译回来是空的。

# Said when a whole run stops. Each explains what to do next, because stopping
# is not a failure: everything already translated is kept.
# en: The translation service sent back a different number of lines from the number it was given, so nothing in that batch could be matched up to the text it came from.
packText.mt.miscounted = 翻译服务返回的行数和发出去的不一样，所以那一批里没有一条能对上它原本的文本。

# en: The translation service refused the request ({status}): {why}
packText.mt.refused = 翻译服务拒绝了这次请求（{status}）：{why}

# en: The translation service would not answer. Everything translated so far is kept - run it again later and it carries on from where it stopped.
packText.mt.noAnswer = 翻译服务没有应答。已经翻好的都保留着——过一会儿再跑一次，它会从停下的地方接着来。

# en: The translation service did not answer in time.
packText.mt.timedOut = 翻译服务没有及时应答。

# en: The translation service answered with something ModForge cannot read. It may have changed, or it may be asking for a captcha in a browser.
packText.mt.unreadable = 翻译服务返回了 ModForge 读不懂的内容。它可能改过了，也可能是在浏览器里要求验证码。

# en: Too many requests in too short a time. Leave it a while and run it again - everything already translated is kept.
packText.mt.tooMany = 短时间内请求太多。先放一会儿再跑一次——已经翻好的都保留着。

# en: The translation service refused this connection. It may have blocked this address for a while, or it may be asking for a captcha in a browser.
packText.mt.blocked = 翻译服务拒绝了这个连接。它可能把这个地址暂时封了，也可能是在浏览器里要求验证码。

# en: The translation service answered {status}.
packText.mt.answered = 翻译服务返回了 {status}。

# Said when too many lines in a row fail the markup check: the engine is answering but not translating.
# en: The translation service is answering, but {damaged} of the last {total} lines came back damaged - so it has probably changed, rather than having trouble with a few lines. The run stopped here; everything translated before this is kept.
packText.mt.notWorking = 翻译服务有应答，但最近 {total} 行里有 {damaged} 行回来是损坏的——所以它很可能改了，而不只是个别几行出问题。翻译在这里停下了；之前翻好的都保留着。

# Said when the test sentence sent before a run does not come back properly translated.
# en: The translation service did not translate a test sentence properly, so nothing of the pack was sent. It may have changed how it answers. Try again later, or use another translation engine.
packText.mt.probeFailed = 翻译服务没能正确翻译一句测试语句，所以模组包的内容一句都没有发送。它可能改变了应答方式。请稍后再试，或换一个翻译引擎。

# Said when Google answers with its block page rather than a translation. Not a slow-down: asking again only makes it last longer, so the run stops at once.
# en: Google has stopped answering this connection for now - it says your network may be sending automated queries, and wants a captcha solved in a browser. It usually lifts on its own after a while, so try again later. Everything already translated is kept.
packText.mt.captcha = 谷歌暂时不再响应这个连接——它说你的网络可能在发送自动化请求，要求在浏览器里完成验证码。这通常过一段时间会自己解除，请稍后再试。已经翻好的内容都会保留。

# The title of the small window that shows the Google Translate web page while a pack is translated through it
# en: Translating through the Google Translate website
packText.web.title = 正在通过谷歌翻译网站翻译

# At the top of that window
# en: ModForge is translating your pack through this page, because Google's free translation service is not answering as it should. The page is locked while ModForge works it - leave the window open; it closes by itself when the translation is done. If Google asks you something here, the translation stops and the page unlocks for you to answer; nothing here is answered for you.
packText.web.explain = 因为 Google 的免费翻译服务没有正常响应，ModForge 正在通过这个页面翻译你的模组包。ModForge 使用页面期间，页面是锁定的——请保持窗口打开，翻译完成后它会自动关闭。如果 Google 在这里向你提问，翻译会停止，页面会解锁让你回答；这里不会替你回答任何问题。

# Said while a run changes over to the web page

packText.web.switching = 谷歌的免费翻译服务没有正确翻译测试句子。正在改用谷歌翻译网站……

# Added to the free service's failure when the web page failed too. {why} is what went wrong with the web page
# en: The Google Translate website did not work either: {why}
packText.web.alsoFailed = 谷歌翻译网站也没能用：{why}

# The web page needs Windows' own browser control, which a few computers lack
# en: The Google Translate website could not be opened, because Microsoft Edge WebView2 is not installed on this computer. It comes with Windows 11, and Microsoft offers it as a free download.

# {why} is Windows' own reason
# en: The Google Translate website could not be opened ({why}).
packText.web.couldNotOpen = 无法打开谷歌翻译网站（{why}）。

# The person closed the web page's window while a run was going
# en: The translation window was closed, so the run stopped. Everything already translated is kept.
packText.web.closed = 翻译窗口被关掉了，所以翻译停了下来。已经翻译好的内容都会保留。

# Google shows a captcha or its unusual-traffic page in the web page's window. The run stops; the window stays for the person to see
# en: Google stopped translating and is showing a check in the translation window. ModForge does not answer those, so the run stopped here. Everything already translated is kept - try again later.
packText.web.blocked = 谷歌停止了翻译，并在翻译窗口里显示了一项验证。ModForge 不会替你回答这类验证，所以翻译在这里停下了。已经翻译好的内容都会保留——请稍后再试。

# Google asks a question (its consent page) before translating. The run stops; the window stays
# en: Google is asking something in the translation window before it translates. Answer it there yourself if you want to, then run the translation again. Everything already translated is kept.
packText.web.consent = 谷歌在翻译之前在翻译窗口里提了一个问题。你愿意的话可以自己在那里回答，然后重新开始翻译。已经翻译好的内容都会保留。

# {code} is the language's code, such as pt-BR
# en: The Google Translate website does not know the language code {code}, so nothing was translated into it.
packText.web.language = 谷歌翻译网站不认识语言代码 {code}，所以没有翻译成这种语言。

# The web page did not show a translation within half a minute
# en: The Google Translate website did not show a translation in time.
packText.web.timedOut = 谷歌翻译网站没有及时显示译文。

# Added to the top of a file a machine has written into, so whoever opens it
# knows what they are looking at before they read a word of it.
# en: Some of the lines below were translated by a machine and nobody has checked them. Correcting one is as simple as writing over it: a line that already says something is never translated again.
packText.mt.fileNote = 下面有些行是机器翻译的，还没有人校对过。要改正只要直接覆盖写上去：已经有内容的行不会再被翻译一次。

[Editing in a language]

# Beside the language list at the top of the window.
# en: Editing in:
editingLanguage.label = 编辑语言：

# The first entry: the pack as its author wrote it, not a translation.
# en: Default ({language})
editingLanguage.own = 默认（{language}）

# en: Show and edit every text a player reads in one of the pack's translations. Everything the game finds things by - keys, names of objects, targets - stays the same in every language, and anything you add or change that is not a text is added or changed for every language.
editingLanguage.tip = 用模组包的某个译本来显示和编辑玩家会读到的所有文本。游戏用来查找东西的一切（键、对象名、目标）在所有语言里都保持不变；你添加或修改的任何非文本内容，会在所有语言里一起添加或修改。

# A small button beside the Editing in list: opens the window listing the pack's translations
# en: Edit…
editingLanguage.edit = 编辑…

# Tooltip on that button
# en: See the pack's translations and how far along each one is, copy one over another language, or delete one.
editingLanguage.edit.tip = 查看模组包的各个翻译及其完成程度，把其中一个复制到另一种语言上，或删除一个。

# Title of that window
# en: Pack translations
packTranslations.title = 模组包翻译

# At the top of that window
# en: The translations beside your pack, and how far along each one is. Pick one to copy it over another language, or to delete it.
packTranslations.intro = 你的模组包旁边的各个翻译，以及每个的完成程度。选中一个，可以把它复制到另一种语言上，或者删除它。

# The window's list: its column headings
# en: Language
packTranslations.column.language = 语言

# en: Translated
packTranslations.column.translated = 已翻译

# en: Out of date
packTranslations.column.outOfDate = 已过时

# en: File
packTranslations.column.file = 文件

# In the Translated column: how much of the translation is done. {percent} is a whole number
# en: {percent}% ({done} of {total})
packTranslations.translatedValue = {percent}%（{done}/{total}）

# Shown instead of the list when the pack has no translations
# en: This pack has no translations yet. Start one by picking a language in Editing in, or with File ▸ Pack translations.
packTranslations.none = 这个模组包还没有翻译。在“编辑语言”中选一种语言，或通过 文件 ▸ 模组包翻译 开始一个。

# Beside the list of languages the picked translation can be copied over
# en: Copy to:
packTranslations.transferTo = 复制到：

# The button that copies the picked translation over the language chosen beside it
# en: Transfer…
packTranslations.transfer = 转移…

# Tooltip on that button
# en: Copy the picked translation's texts, word for word, over the language chosen beside it - for a translation typed in while the wrong language was up.
packTranslations.transfer.tip = 把选中翻译的文字逐字复制到旁边选的语言上——用于在选错语言时输入的翻译。

# The button that deletes the picked translation
# en: Delete…
packTranslations.delete = 删除…

# Tooltip on that button
# en: Delete the picked translation and every text in it.
packTranslations.delete.tip = 删除选中的翻译及其中的所有文字。

# The window's close button
# en: Close
packTranslations.close = 关闭

# Title of the question asked before a transfer
# en: Transfer a translation
packTranslations.transfer.title = 转移翻译

# The question asked before a transfer. {from} and {to} are languages, named with their codes
# en: Copy every text of {from} over {to}? Where {from} has a text, {to} takes it word for word, and loses what it had there. The rest of {to} stays as it is, and {from} keeps all of its own.
packTranslations.transfer.confirm = 要把{from}的所有文字复制到{to}上吗？{from}有文字的地方，{to}会逐字采用，并失去原来的内容。{to}的其余部分保持不变，{from}也保留自己的全部文字。

# Title of the question asked before deleting a translation
# en: Delete a translation
packTranslations.delete.title = 删除翻译

# The question asked before deleting a translation. {language} is the language, named with its code
# en: Delete {language} and every text in it? Its file goes to the Recycle Bin, and players reading this language get the default text.
packTranslations.delete.confirm = 要删除{language}及其中的所有文字吗？它的文件会移到回收站，使用这种语言的玩家会看到默认文字。

# After a transfer: what was done
# en: Copied {count} texts from {from} to {to}.
packTranslations.transferred.other = 已把 {count} 条文字从{from}复制到{to}。

# After a delete: what was done
# en: Deleted {language}.
packTranslations.deleted = 已删除{language}。

# The bar across the window while a translation is being edited.
# en: You are editing a translation. Every text a player reads shows it, and what you type goes into it. Anything that is not a text is shared by every language - adding a line adds it to all of them. A line you add, or a text that was empty, gets its words in this language only: its default text stays empty until you write it, and the pack's check lists it as an error.
editingLanguage.banner = 你正在编辑一个译本。玩家会读到的所有文本都显示这个译本，你输入的内容也写进它。非文本的内容由所有语言共用——添加一行，就会添加到所有语言里。你新添加的行，或原本为空的文本，只在这个语言里有文字：它的默认文字保持为空，直到你写上为止，模组包检查会把它列为错误。

# en: Editing in a language
editingLanguage.title = 用某种语言编辑

# When a pack that has never been saved is switched to a language.
# en: Save the pack first. A translation is kept in a file beside the pack, and a pack that has never been saved has nowhere to put one yet.
editingLanguage.saveFirst = 请先保存模组包。译本保存在模组包旁边的文件里，从未保存过的模组包还没有地方放它。

[Translate the pack]

# The window's own title.
# en: Translate this pack
translatePack.title = 翻译这个模组包

# The bold line at the top.
# en: Translate everything a player reads into the languages you tick.
translatePack.headline = 把玩家会读到的所有文本翻译成你勾选的语言。

# The grey paragraph under it. Said before the button, not after: handing a
# pack's text to somebody else's service is a decision, and the rate limit
# lands on the author's own connection.
# en: Your pack's text is sent to Google's free translation service. It is not an official one: it can refuse, it can block your connection for a while if asked too often, and it can change without warning. If it stops working, ModForge tries the Google Translate website instead, in a small window you can see. Nothing is sent while anyone is playing - the translation is written into your pack and stays there. Everything a machine writes is marked, and you can correct any of it by writing over it.
translatePack.warning = 你的模组包文本会发送给谷歌的免费翻译服务。那不是官方接口：它可能拒绝，请求太频繁时可能把你的连接封一段时间，也可能毫无预告地改掉。如果它不能用了，ModForge 会改用谷歌翻译网站，在一个你看得到的小窗口里进行。游戏过程中不会发送任何东西——译文写进你的模组包里，就留在那儿。机器写的内容都会标出来，你可以直接覆盖改写任何一行。

# The tick box under the warning. Off unless ticked.
# en: Translate character names too
translatePack.names = 也翻译角色名字

# Under that box: what happens with it off, and why it starts off. The example is a name that is also an ordinary word; any such name in your language will do
# en: Left unticked, every character's name stays as it is written - spelled in their own alphabet for languages that have one - in the name itself and wherever a line mentions it with a capital letter. Ticked, names are translated like any other text, and a translator reads a name as the word it looks like: a character called Hope can come back as Hoffnung in German, in every line that mentions her.
translatePack.names.warning = 不勾选时，每个角色的名字都保持原样（在有自己文字的语言中用该文字拼写），无论是名字本身，还是台词中以大写字母提到它的地方。勾选后，名字会像其他文本一样被翻译，而翻译器会把名字当成它看起来像的那个词：一个叫 Hope 的角色，可能在每一句提到她的台词里都变成“希望”。

# The heading over the list of names, when a ticked language uses another alphabet
# en: Names in other alphabets
translatePack.spellings = 其他文字中的名字

# The grey text at the top of that list
# en: These languages write names in an alphabet of their own. Type how each name is written, or press Translate to have Google suggest spellings first - then check every suggestion, because it can still come back as an ordinary word rather than a name. A name left empty stays in Latin letters.
translatePack.spellings.help = 这些语言用自己的文字书写名字。请输入每个名字的写法，或按“翻译”先让 Google 提供建议写法——然后逐一检查，因为它仍可能返回一个普通词语而不是名字。留空的名字会保持拉丁字母。

# The heading over the column of names
# en: Name
translatePack.spellings.name = 名字

# While Google is asked for spellings
# en: Asking Google how the names are spelled...
translatePack.spellings.asking = 正在向 Google 询问名字的写法...

# After it has answered. Nothing has been translated yet
# en: Google's suggestions are in the list of names, in italics. Check each one and correct any that came back as an ordinary word rather than a name, then press Translate again.
translatePack.spellings.check = Google 的建议以斜体显示在名字列表中。请逐一检查，把返回成普通词语而不是名字的改正，然后再按一次“翻译”。

# When it could not answer. The reason it gave is shown on the line under this
# en: Google could not suggest spellings, and nothing has been translated. Press Translate again later and it will ask again - or type the names in the list yourself first; a name left empty stays in Latin letters.
translatePack.spellings.failed = Google 无法提供建议写法，也还没有翻译任何内容。稍后再按一次“翻译”，会重新询问；也可以先在名字列表中自己输入。留空的名字会保持拉丁字母。

# Under the line above, one per language Google answered but no spelling could be read out of. {language} is the language's own name; {answer} is what Google sent back, as it was
# en: {language}: no spelling could be read out of Google's answer - type those names yourself. Google answered: {answer}
translatePack.spellings.unread = {language}：无法从 Google 的回答中读出写法——请自己输入这些名字。Google 的回答：{answer}

# Tooltip on a suggested spelling not yet checked
# en: Suggested by Google - check it before translating.
translatePack.spellings.suggested.tip = 由 Google 建议——翻译前请检查。

# Over the list of languages, above Google's own check page, when Google stops to make sure a person is asking
# en: Google wants to check that a person is asking before it translates any more. Answer its check below - ModForge never answers it for you - and the translation carries on by itself. Stop ends it here, and everything translated so far is kept.
translatePack.check = Google 想先确认是真人在请求，然后才继续翻译。请回答下面的验证——ModForge 从不替你回答——之后翻译会自动继续。按“停止”会在这里结束，已翻译的内容都会保留。

# While the check is showing
# en: Waiting for you to answer Google's check...
translatePack.check.waiting = 正在等你回答 Google 的验证...

# Once it has been answered
# en: Check passed - carrying on.
translatePack.check.passed = 验证已通过——继续。

# When Google's page, shown to the person, has no check on it - only "try again later"
# en: Google's page has nothing to answer - it only says to try again later - so ModForge carries on through the Google Translate website instead, which shows a check you can answer if it wants one.
translatePack.check.none = Google 的页面上没有可回答的内容——只说稍后再试——所以 ModForge 改用 Google 翻译网站继续；如果需要，它会显示一个你可以回答的验证。

# en: Languages
translatePack.languages = 语言

# Beside a language the pack has no file for at all.
# en: not started - {count} texts to translate
translatePack.notStarted = 还没开始——有 {count} 条文本要翻译

# Beside one where everything is translated.
# en: fully translated
translatePack.finished = 已全部翻译

# Beside one part-way through.
# en: {missing} of {total} still to translate
translatePack.partly = {total} 条里还有 {missing} 条要翻译

# When ModForge has no translations of its own to offer.
# en: ModForge has no other languages installed, so there is nothing to translate into yet.
translatePack.noLanguages = ModForge 没有装别的语言，所以暂时没有可以翻译成的目标。

# en: Select all
translatePack.selectAll = 全选

# en: Select none
translatePack.selectNone = 全不选

# en: Translate
translatePack.translate = 翻译

# en: Close
translatePack.close = 关闭

# The Close button while a run is going.
# en: Stop
translatePack.stop = 停止

# When Translate is pressed with nothing ticked.
# en: Tick at least one language first.
translatePack.pickOne = 请先勾选至少一种语言。

# While it runs.
# en: {language}: {done} of {total} translated...
translatePack.progress = {language}：已翻译 {done}/{total}……

# While it runs, over the bar for the whole run: every language picked, counted in texts. {number} is the language it is on, {count} how many languages were picked
# en: All languages: {done} of {total} texts (language {number} of {count})
translatePack.progress.all = 所有语言：{done}/{total} 条文字（第 {number} 种，共 {count} 种）

# Over the Translate window's bar once a run is over, having done every language picked.
# en: Translation finished.
translatePack.over.finished = 翻译完成。

# Over the Translate window's bar when a run was stopped, or failed, before the end.
# en: Translation stopped before the end. Everything translated so far is kept, and Translate picks up from there.
translatePack.over.stopped = 翻译在完成前停止了。已经翻译的内容都会保留，点击「翻译」会从那里继续。

# One line per language afterwards.
# en: {language}: {count} translated.
translatePack.did = {language}：翻译了 {count} 条。

# Added to that line when some came back unusable.
# en: {count} were left in the default text because the translation lost a gap, a tag or a token.
translatePack.damaged = 有 {count} 条保留了默认文字，因为译文弄丢了花括号、标签或记号。

# en: Nothing was translated.
translatePack.nothingDone = 没有翻译任何内容。
